Motivation: 机器人需要在陌生环境中执行新任务，且事先不知道相关概念，因此需要同时具备几何与语义理解能力，支持开放词汇场景理解和人机交互的语言标注 3D 地图。

Method: 在紧凑双高斯表示上进行在线语言特征自适应，联合重建场景几何、外观和开放词汇语义，内存开销适中；规划器高效选择信息量大的视角，实现主动开放词汇 3D 建图。

Result: 在 Replica 和 ScanNet++ 上，2D 与 3D 开放词汇分割相比在线和离线基线均有显著提升，表明主动探索场景能更高效地构建语言标注 3D 地图。

Conclusion: 主动探索结合语义不确定性引导可减少观测次数和计算成本，更高效地构建语言标注 3D 地图。

Abstract: As robots increasingly assist humans with diverse tasks, they need both geometric and semantic understanding of their surroundings. Moreover, robots often operate in unfamiliar environments and take on new tasks without knowing the relevant concepts ahead of time. This motivates language-annotated 3D maps that support open-vocabulary scene understanding and human-robot interaction. We introduce ActiveLang, an autonomous system for active open-vocabulary 3D mapping with semantic-uncertainty-guided exploration. ActiveLang performs online language-feature adaptation on a compact dual-Gaussian representation to jointly reconstruct scene geometry, appearance, and open-vocabulary semantics with modest memory overhead. Its planner efficiently selects informative viewpoints, enabling effective mapping with fewer observations and lower computational cost. Experiments on Replica and ScanNet++ demonstrate substantial improvements in 2D and 3D open-vocabulary segmentation over both online and offline baselines, highlighting that actively exploring scenes builds language-annotated 3D maps more efficiently.

</details>


### [71] [KASALv2: Fully Automatic 3D Rotational Symmetry Classification and Axis Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09534)
*Mengxin Zhang,Yulin Wang,Chen Luo,Yongzhe Li,Yijun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全自动、无参考的旋转对称性分析框架，可对3D物体进行八类旋转对称类型分类、旋转阶数识别与完整轴定位，并在多个数据集上验证其精度与下游6D位姿估计增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转对称性是6D位姿估计的重要先验，有助于提升位姿精度并支持对称性感知评估。但现有3D物体的对称性标注大多依赖人工或半自动方式，且常需预定义对称类型或阶数，难以规模化扩展。

Method: 方法首先定位主导的高阶对称轴，通过自一致性分析推断其旋转阶数，并在层级引导的建模式下重建完整对称结构，覆盖全部八种典型3D旋转对称类型。此外引入纹理感知扩展，可在保持轴方向的同时建模由外观引起的旋转阶数降低。整个框架无需参考模板，完全自动。

Result: 在理想化与真实世界数据集上实验显示，方法具有较强精度与泛化能力，在GSO的438个对称物体上取得94.75%的准确率。将估计得到的旋转先验用于训练FoundationPose，在五个BOP数据集上精度最高提升0.9%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，自动估计的旋转对称先验可有效改善下游6D位姿估计性能，为对称性标注的规模化与自动化提供了可行方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Rotational symmetry is an important prior in 6D pose estimation, improving pose accuracy and supporting symmetry-aware evaluation. However, current symmetry annotations for 3D objects remain largely manual or semi-automatic, often requiring predefined types or orders, which limits scalability. This work introduces a fully automatic, reference-free framework for symmetry-type classification, rotational-order identification, and full-axis localization across all eight canonical 3D rotational symmetry types. The method localizes a dominant high-order axis, infers its rotational order through self-consistency analysis, and reconstructs the complete symmetry structure under a hierarchy-guided formulation. A texture-aware extension further models appearance-induced reductions in rotational order while preserving axis orientations. Experiments on idealized and real-world datasets demonstrate strong accuracy and generalization, achieving 94.75% accuracy on 438 symmetric objects in GSO. Training FoundationPose with these priors improves accuracy by up to 0.9% across five BOP datasets, showing that automatically estimated rotational priors improve downstream 6D pose estimation. Code is available at https://github.com/WangYuLin-SEU/KASAL.

</details>


### [72] [WAPR: A Foundation Model for Wide-Angle Refinement in Unseen Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09535)
*Yulin Wang,Mengting Hu,Hongli Li,Jianghao Zhou,Chen Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAPR：一种零样本宽角度位姿精修模型，支持最大90度旋转偏差的精修，配以SA6D大规模数据集和角度平衡损失，在BOP七个核心数据集上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用要求6D位姿估计兼具准确性、快速性和对未见物体的可扩展性，而现有方法在宽角度旋转偏差下精修能力有限、训练数据不足、且大误差样本会干扰学习稳定性。

Method: 提出WAPR零样本宽角度位姿精修模型，利用旋转对称先验将对称等价位姿目标规范化后再计算损失；构建SA6D大规模6D训练数据集，通过KASAL辅助获取944个GSO扫描的旋转对称先验，并借助几何与纹理增强扩展至约5万物体实例、约200万渲染RGB-D图像；引入角度平衡损失降低无信息大误差样本的影响。

Result: 每个检测实例仅需12个候选位姿即可实现单帧1秒内快速推理，位姿估计吞吐量达每秒25个检测实例；在BOP七个核心数据集上，在快速与无约束推理设置下均达到未见物体6D位姿定位与检测的最先进水平。

Conclusion: WAPR通过宽角度零样本精修、对称先验规范化、大规模数据集SA6D及角度平衡损失，有效兼顾速度与精度，为未见物体的6D位姿估计提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world applications require 6D pose estimation to be accurate, fast, and scalable to unseen objects. This paper introduces WAPR, a zero-shot wide-angle pose refinement model that refines candidate poses with rotational deviations up to 90 degrees. With as few as 12 candidate poses per detected object instance, WAPR supports fast inference within 1 s per frame and reaches a pose-estimation throughput of up to 25 detected object instances per second. To support wide-angle training for rotationally symmetric objects, WAPR uses rotational symmetry priors to canonicalize symmetry-equivalent pose targets before loss computation. We further construct SA6D, a large-scale 6D training dataset with such priors. SA6D obtains KASAL-assisted rotational symmetry priors for 944 GSO scans and expands them through geometry and texture augmentation into about 50K augmented object instances and about 2M rendered RGB-D images. In addition, an angle-balanced loss stabilizes learning across different angular ranges by reducing the influence of uninformative large-error cases. Experiments on seven BOP core datasets show that WAPR achieves state-of-the-art performance in unseen-object 6D pose localization and detection under both fast and unconstrained inference settings. Project page: https://github.com/WangYuLin-SEU/WAPR.

</details>


### [73] [Visual Evidence Under Cross-Examination: Evaluating and Controlling Decision-Level Evidence Use in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09550)
*Huiyao Zhang,Jin Bai,Zilong Su,Rui Guo,Chaofan Qin,Jinze Lv,Wenhui Yu,Hongfei Wang,Ye Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究视觉语言模型中"候选绑定视觉贡献"问题，提出 CROSS-Bench 基准和 RIVET 接口，将视觉证据的效用与其候选特异性效应目的地分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型越来越依赖裁剪区域、工具产生的观察结果进行推理，但一个观察结果可能在不有利于其所支持候选的情况下影响最终答案。现有方法未能区分视觉证据的效用与其候选定向效应。

Method: 提出 CROSS-Bench 基准，包含 28,000 个决策问题，并在专门评估子集上进行匹配的无效化与重绑定测试；设计 RIVET 接口，保留证据身份与不确定性，构建候选条件响应并单独控制其强度。

Result: 共享证据实验显示任务准确率与证据归属可能分离；在匹配容量与训练下，RIVET 将归一化效应转移从 0.512 提升至 0.651；在四类冻结骨干模型上，RIVET 平均提升 CROSS-Bench 准确率 5.70 个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究结果成功将视觉证据的效用与其效应的候选特异性目的地分离，表明证据有益性与其对特定候选的支持作用可以独立控制。

Abstract: Vision-language models increasingly reason through crops, regions, and tool-produced observations. Yet an observation can influence the answer without benefiting the candidate it supports. We study candidate-bound visual contribution: valid evidence should help, invalidating its supporting relation should remove its additional effect, and valid rebinding should redirect that effect to the newly supported candidate. We introduce CROSS-Bench, a benchmark of 28,000 decision problems, with matched invalidation and rebinding tests on a dedicated evaluation subset. Our RIVET interface preserves evidence identity and uncertainty, composes a candidate-conditioned response, and separately controls its strength. Shared-evidence experiments show that task accuracy and evidence ownership can diverge. Under matched capacity and training, RIVET increases normalized effect transfer from 0.512 to 0.651 where clean evidence has a positive effect. The advantage persists on common evaluation examples and across repeated decision-layer fits. With evidence predicted from raw inputs, RIVET improves CROSS-Bench accuracy by an average of 5.70 pp across four frozen backbones, relative to the same models without auxiliary evidence. These results separate the utility of visual evidence from the candidate-specific destination of its effect.

</details>


### [74] [Gradient-Based Trajectory Optimisation over Continuous Poses for Sparse-View Cone-Beam CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09579)
*Linda-Sophie Schneider,Simon Wittl,Gabriel Herl,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出将CT扫描中每个光源位姿视为连续变量，在扫描仪运动流形上通过梯度上升联合优化所有位姿，用统一优化器处理多种参数化，无需物体特定的预计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轨迹优化依赖固定候选池，无法实现离网精调，且每更换一次采集流形就需针对具体物体重新预计算，灵活性差、代价高。作者希望让位姿连续可调并直接纳入衰减与扫描仪约束，从而提升稀疏视角采集设计质量。

Method: 将每个光源位姿设为独立连续变量，在扫描仪运动流形上做梯度上升联合移动全部位姿。目标函数由软Tuy平面覆盖、连续视角协方差损失和解析衰减感知射线束罚项组成，同一优化器适用于圆形、受限C臂、双轴和自由球面参数化。

Result: 在Defrise法兰上，连续选择可恢复圆形轨道不可见的层状缺陷；在自由球面上稀疏预算时与离散交换搜索持平，密集预算时领先；中等仰角带即可获得大部分自由球面增益。光子噪声保持法兰上的优劣排序。选择耗时数秒而非数分钟，无需物体特定重建基。机器人CT实验验证了可行性，但未见相较均匀带采样的稳定指标增益。

Conclusion: 连续位姿优化能把衰减与扫描仪约束直接融入稀疏视角采集设计，并可在有限扫描包络内迁移通用优化器；预扫描规划需与计划采集流形匹配，实际执行虽可行但增益尚不稳定。

Abstract: Trajectory optimisation for cone-beam computed tomography (CT) determines which information sparse-view scans acquire. Fixed candidate pools prevent off-grid refinement and require new object-specific precomputation for each acquisition manifold. We make every source pose an individual continuous variable and move all poses jointly by gradient ascent on the scanner's kinematic manifold. The objective combines soft-Tuy plane coverage, continuous View Covariance Loss, and an analytic attenuation-aware ray-bundle penalty. The same optimiser handles circular, limited C-arm, two-axis, and freesphere parametrisations. On a Defrise flange, continuous selection recovers laminar defects invisible to a circular orbit, matches discrete swap search on the free sphere at the sparser budget, and leads at the denser one, with the same objective evaluated in every arm. A moderate elevation band already recovers most of the free-sphere gain at the defects, so the same optimiser transfers to bounded scanner envelopes. Photon noise preserves the ordering on the flange and compresses it on a dense fuel nozzle. Sparseprescan planning benefits from matching prescan and planned acquisition manifolds. Selection takes seconds rather than minutes without an object-specific reconstruction basis. Prescan-planned poses were executed on a robot CT bench and reconstructed in a common frame, demonstrating feasibility but no consistent metric gain over uniform band sampling. Continuous pose optimisation incorporates attenuation and scanner constraints directly into sparse-view acquisition design.

</details>


### [75] [STORK: Spatio-Temporal Observation of uterine contRactions via neural networKs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09598)
*Melissa Schween,Tristan Gottwald,Jordina Aviles Verdera,Lisa Story,Mary Rutherford,Jana Hutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将子宫收缩活动检测（UCAD）形式化为弱监督学习问题，提出 STORK 多示例学习模型，仅用系列级标签训练，实现帧级收缩评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿MRI中的子宫收缩通常被人工识别后丢弃，限制了对收缩动态的研究，因此需要自动化检测方法。

Method: STORK将3D时空卷积分解为沿时间超平面的并行分支，以低代价捕捉组织运动；帧级嵌入结合强度与Demons估计的位移场，经线性均值池化头聚合，从而在无帧级监督下恢复帧级评分。

Result: 在约700例多厂商动态胎儿MRI系列上，STORK取得系列级AUROC 95.0%、AUPRC 94.6%，显著优于3D ResNet和ConvNeXt基线。

Conclusion: Grad-CAM表明模型利用了超越胎盘、延伸至子宫组织的预测特征，为子宫行为表型分析提供了自动化工具。

Abstract: Uterine contractions in fetal MRI are typically identified manually and discarded, limiting insights into contraction dynamics. We formalize Uterine Contractile Activity Detection (UCAD) as a weakly-supervised learning problem and introduce STORK, a multi-instance learning model trained on dynamic MRI series using only coarse, series-level labels. STORK factorizes 3D spatio-temporal convolutions into parallel branches across temporal hyperplanes to capture coherent tissue motion without the cost of full 4D convolutions. Per-frame embeddings, combining intensity and Demons-estimated displacement fields, are aggregated by a linear mean-pooling head. This ensures that frame-level contraction scores can be recovered post-hoc without frame-level training supervision. Evaluated on around 700 multi-vendor dynamic fetal MRI series, STORK achieves a series-level AUROC of 95.0% and AUPRC of 94.6%, substantially outperforming 3D ResNet and ConvNeXt baselines. Grad-CAM analysis suggests that the model draws on predictive features extending beyond the placenta into the uterine tissue, offering an automated tool for richer phenotyping of uterine behavior.

</details>


### [76] [Identity-Duplication Auditing in National-Scale Neuroimaging Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09614)
*Jiheng Li,Michael E. Kim,Trent M. Schwartz,Yuhan Cui,Gaurav Rudravaram,Derek B. Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Lori L. Beason-Held,Victoria L. Morgan,Dario J. Englot,Angela L. Jefferson,for the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative,for the BIOCARD Study team,for the Health,Aging Brain Study,:,Health Disparities,Study Team,Lianrui Zuo,Guray Erus,Christos Davatzikos,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HAPPEN——一个用于审计大规模T1加权脑MRI仓储中"身份重复"问题的人机协同流程，结合SHA-256精确去重与监督对比检索潜在的同人非同一扫描，检索结果由人工界面复核，并在95,129例仓储与外部22,386例数据上验证了跨机构可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全国性MRI数据仓储不断整合不同研究与机构的数据，而仅在单个数据集内有效的受试者标识在聚合后不再保证全局唯一，同一受试者可能被赋予多个标识（即身份重复）。这会引致训练集与测试集之间的信息泄漏，夸大下游生物医学研究的表观性能。现有方法缺乏面向仓储规模的、端到端且基于图像的审计工作流。

Method: 提出HAPPEN人机协同流程：以SHA-256指纹做精确重复检测，并用监督对比学习检索可能来自同一人的非同一扫描；检索出的配对不以自动分类方式判定为重复，而是作为候选在本地部署的界面中交由人工复核。

Result: 在包含95,129例扫描的聚合仓储中部署，并用54对基因参考配对评估端到端召回。识别出1,316个精确重复扫描组与1,275个获得复核者支持的高相似受试者组，其中分别有56%与82%跨越数据集边界；54对基因参考配对全部被召回。在另一独立机构的22,386例扫描上本地部署同一工作流，无需模型重训练或影像传输，该外部团队以自行选定的操作阈值与复核标准独立完成了完整流程。

Conclusion: HAPPEN提供了可端到端审计仓储级身份重复的可行方案，具备跨机构可迁移性，能在不转移数据的前提下支持外部团队独立复现；人工复核环节为候选判定保留了必要把关，有助于缓解数据泄漏带来的性能虚高与数据整合偏差。

Abstract: National-scale magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) repositories increasingly integrate data from different studies and institutions. However, subject identifiers that are valid only within individual datasets are no longer guaranteed to remain globally unique after aggregation, making it possible for the same subject to be assigned multiple identifiers, which we define as identity duplication. Such duplication can create leakage between training and test data and inflate apparent performance in downstream biomedical studies. Existing methods do not provide an end-to-end, image-based workflow for auditing this problem at repository scale. In this work, we present HAPPEN, a human-in-the-loop pipeline for auditing identity duplication in T1-weighted brain MRI repositories. It combines SHA-256 fingerprinting for exact-duplicate detection with supervised contrastive retrieval of non-identical scans that may originate from the same person. Retrieved pairs are reviewed as candidates in a locally hosted interface rather than automatically classified as duplicates. We deployed the workflow in a 95,129-scan aggregated repository and assessed end-to-end recovery using 54 genetic-reference pairs. Transferability was assessed by locally deploying the same workflow on 22,386 scans at an independent institution without model retraining or image transfer. Deployment in the study repository identified 1,316 exact-duplicate scan groups and 1,275 reviewer-supported near-duplicate subject groups. Of these groups, 56% and 82%, respectively, crossed dataset boundaries. All 54 genetic-reference pairs were recovered. The external team independently completed the full workflow using a locally selected operating threshold and review standard.

</details>


### [77] [Quasi-Binarized Autoencoders: An Architecture-Independent Information Bottleneck for Medical Image Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09670)
*Shouhei Hanaoka,Takahiro Nakao,Atsushi Takamatsu,Takeharu Yoshikawa,Osamu Abe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出准二值化（QB）层，通过对潜变量加Laplace噪声并限制互信息上界，构建QBAE模型实现无监督医学图像异常检测，在MedIAnomaly基准上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重建的无监督异常检测依赖编码器-解码器网络，只有当编码器传到解码器的信息受限时才能有效检测异常；否则网络会学到恒等映射并重建异常。现有方法通过架构选择限制信息量，需逐数据集调参且无法用比特量化。

Method: 引入准二值化（QB）层，将每个潜变量压缩到[0,1]并加入尺度为1/epsilon的Laplace噪声，使每个元素满足epsilon-局部差分隐私。图像与重建之间的互信息被限定为仅依赖epsilon和QB元素数量的上界，与编码器解码器无关。在所有编码器-解码器路径（含跳连接）放置QB层，构建QBAE：七层注意力U-Net，含32768个QB元素。

Result: 在MedIAnomaly基准的七个数据集上，单一架构与配置的QBAE达到平均图像级AUROC 0.828，为不针对各数据集适配方法中的最高值；在BraTS2021上取得最佳结果（AUROC 0.911，像素级AP 0.838）。测试时保留噪声，使每次重建均满足上界。无输入损坏时，仅靠瓶颈即可防止恒等崩塌（平均AUROC 0.805 vs. 0.590）。

Conclusion: QB层通过互信息上界有效限制信息传递，防止恒等映射崩塌，QBAE以统一架构达成领先的无监督医学异常检测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection, which learns only from normal images, is a central task in medical image analysis and remains an open problem. Reconstruction-based methods pass an image through an encoder-decoder network trained on normal data and detect anomalies from the residual between the image and its reconstruction. This works only if the information passed from the encoder to the decoder is limited; otherwise the network learns an identity mapping and reconstructs anomalies too. This limit is usually imposed through architectural choices, tuned per dataset, that cannot be stated in bits. We introduce the quasi-binarizing (QB) layer, which squashes each latent element into [0, 1] and adds Laplace noise of scale 1/epsilon. Each element is then epsilon-locally differentially private, and the mutual information between an image and its reconstruction is bounded by a quantity that depends only on epsilon and the number of QB elements, whatever the encoder and decoder. Placing a QB layer on every encoder-decoder path, including all skip connections, we build QBAE, a seven-level attention U-Net with 32,768 QB elements. On the seven datasets of the MedIAnomaly benchmark, QBAE with one architecture and one configuration reaches a mean image-level AUROC of 0.828, the highest among methods that do not adapt to each dataset, and the best reported results on BraTS2021 (AUROC 0.911, pixel-level AP 0.838). The noise is kept at test time, so that every reconstruction satisfies the bound. Without input corruption, the bottleneck alone prevents identity collapse (mean AUROC 0.805 vs. 0.590). Code is available at https://github.com/hanaokalog/MedIAnomalyQB.

</details>


### [78] [A Multi-Source Ultrasound Benchmark Revealing the Limits of Contemporary Self-Supervised Anomaly Detection Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09677)
*Marco Riedenauer,Daniel Kienzle,Pratik Mayekar,Rainer Lienhart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SADUSI基准，一个多源超声数据集，用于跨解剖部位、视图和采集协议训练与评估自监督异常检测方法。实验显示现有方法在此设定下表现不佳，表明多源超声异常检测仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学超声自监督异常检测的评估多局限于单一解剖部位或任务，无法判断模型学到的是稳健的"正常超声"概念还是仅针对特定来源的表示。因此需要一个多源、多样化的基准来检验模型的泛化能力。

Method: 构建SADUSI基准：一个多源超声数据集，覆盖广泛的解剖区域、成像视图和采集协议，提供多样化的正常超声分布以及可从单张图像评估的可见结构性异常标注。并在此基准上评估代表性的自监督异常检测方法（包括基于重建的扩散方法和基于特征的PatchCore变体）。

Result: 重建类扩散方法（如AnoDDPM、DeCo-Diff）像素级AUROC仅0.56-0.72，最大F1为0.10-0.26，难以区分病理与正常区域；基于特征的PatchCore变体表现更好，像素级AUROC达0.76-0.83，但最大F1仍仅0.14-0.40，仍有局限。

Conclusion: 广泛的多源超声异常检测仍是开放挑战，SADUSI可作为开发超越解剖特异设定、具备泛化能力方法的资源。

Abstract: Self-supervised anomaly detection is a promising paradigm for medical ultrasound, as normal images are often easier to obtain than exhaustive annotations of all possible pathologies. However, most existing evaluations are limited to a single anatomy or task, making it unclear whether models learn a robust notion of normal ultrasound appearance or only a source-specific representation. We introduce the SADUSI benchmark, a multi-source ultrasound dataset designed to train and evaluate anomaly detection methods across a broad range of anatomical regions, views, and acquisition protocols. The goal of SADUSI is to provide a diverse normal ultrasound distribution and a benchmark for visible structural anomalies that can be assessed from single images. We evaluate representative self-supervised anomaly detection methods and find that current approaches struggle in this setting. In particular, reconstruction-based diffusion methods such as AnoDDPM and DeCo-Diff achieve pixel-level AUROC values of 0.56-0.72 and maximum F1 scores of 0.10-0.26, indicating limited separation of pathology from normal image regions. Feature-based PatchCore variants perform better, reaching pixel-level AUROC values of 0.76-0.83, but remain limited with maximum F1 scores of 0.14-0.40. These findings suggest that broad multi-source ultrasound anomaly detection remains an open challenge and that SADUSI can serve as a resource for developing methods that generalize beyond anatomy-specific settings.

</details>


### [79] [PARC-Loc: Text-to-Point-Cloud Localization with Partial Assignment and Relational Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09761)
*Shengkai Ma,Zhenyu Hou,Weihua Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PARC-Loc粗到精定位框架，通过部分分配与关系一致性建模解决子图选择中的布局不一致别名与边界证据不完整问题，显著提升文本到点云定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粗到精方法用聚合学习到的兼容性检索子图，但城市中重复或相似物体导致查询与多个子图的嵌入相似度被抬高，即便子图内实例布局与描述不符；同时查询相关实例常跨越子图边界，使检索到的子图缺乏完整上下文。作者将这两类失效分别命名为布局不一致别名和边界证据不完整。

Method: 提出PARC-Loc框架，基于部分分配与关系一致性（PARC）。PARC联合建模提示物体兼容性与成对空间关系，允许未匹配元素存在，同时倾向与查询布局一致的分配。粗阶段用候选级评估补充神经相似度以选出布局一致的子图；精阶段从相邻子图扩展查询相关实例扩充上下文，并由PARC产生的物体级匹配权重引导跨模态注意力。

Result: 在KITTI360Pose与CityLoc数据集上，PARC-Loc优于传统粗到精基线。在KITTI360Pose上，5米处的Top-1定位召回率从0.50提升到0.67，相对最强基线取得34%的相对增益。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模部分分配与空间关系一致性，并跨子图扩展上下文，PARC-Loc有效缓解了布局不一致别名与边界证据不完整问题，为城市级文本到点云定位提供了更强方案。

Abstract: Text-to-point-cloud localization estimates a position in a city-scale 3D map from descriptions of surrounding objects. Existing coarse-to-fine methods retrieve submaps using aggregate learned compatibility and then localize within a selected submap. However, repetitive or similar urban objects can inflate the embedding similarity between the query and multiple submaps, even when the instance layout within a submap violates the query description. Meanwhile, query-relevant instances often span submap boundaries, leaving the retrieved submap with incomplete contextual evidence. We term these failure modes layout-inconsistent aliasing and boundary evidence incompleteness, respectively. To address them, we propose PARC-Loc, a coarse-to-fine localization framework built on Partial Assignment with Relational Consistency (PARC). PARC jointly models hint-object compatibility and pairwise spatial relations, allowing unmatched elements while favoring assignments consistent with the queried layout. At the coarse stage, its candidate-level assessment complements neural similarity for layout-consistent submap selection. At the fine stage, the context is expanded with query-relevant instances from adjacent submaps, while PARC yields object-level matching weights that guide cross-modal attention. Extensive experiments on KITTI360Pose and CityLoc show that PARC-Loc outperforms conventional coarse-to-fine baselines. On KITTI360Pose, our method improves Top-1 localization recall at 5 m from 0.50 to 0.67, achieving a 34% relative gain over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [80] [What Makes Synthetic Hard Negatives Work in Vision-Language Pretraining?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09700)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Andreas Floros,Kleanthis Marios Papadopoulos,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析将表示空间合成硬负样本从单模态自监督学习迁移到视觉-语言预训练时的失败模式，提出避免涉及正样本的模态内硬负样本方法 SNAP，在 CLIP 与 FLIP 上取得一致的零样本检索、零样本分类与线性探测提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在表示空间中生成合成硬负样本已在单模态自监督学习中验证有效，但该思路迁移到视觉-语言预训练（VLP）时并不直接适用：跨模态构造易产生过于简单的负样本或将其拉向查询，模态内构造又会混入匹配的正样本。此外，使用合成硬负样本与可学习温度训练时会出现 logit 尺度饱和问题。

Method: 作者分析了六种表示空间合成策略，归纳出两类失败模式，并观察到固定温度可缓解 logit 尺度饱和、提升下游性能。基于该几何分析提出 SNAP：生成不涉及任一模态正样本的模态内硬负样本，从根本上规避两类失败模式。SNAP 与模型无关，无需外部生成模型，训练时间开销低于 10%。

Result: 将 SNAP 应用于 CLIP 与 FLIP，在多种架构与数据集上，于零样本检索、零样本分类和线性探测评测中均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 通过在表示空间中构造不包含正样本的模态内硬负样本，可有效规避合成硬负样本在视觉-语言预训练中的失败模式，且方法通用、开销低、效果稳定。

Abstract: Synthetic hard negatives generated in the representation space have proven effective for unimodal self-supervised learning, but transferring this idea to vision-language pretraining is not straightforward. We analyze six representation-space synthesis strategies and identify two failure modes in their transfer to vision-language pretraining: cross-modal constructions that produce overly easy negatives or pull them toward the query, and intra-modal constructions that incorporate the matched positive. We also observe logit-scale saturation when training with synthetic hard negatives and a learnable temperature, and find that fixing the temperature improves downstream performance. Using this geometric analysis we propose SNAP, which generates intra-modal hard negatives that never involve the positive from either modality, avoiding both failure modes entirely. SNAP is model-agnostic, requires no external generative models, and adds less than 10% training time overhead. Evaluated on top of CLIP and FLIP across multiple architectures and datasets, SNAP delivers consistent improvements on zero-shot retrieval, zero-shot classification, and linear probe evaluation.

</details>


### [81] [Latent Watermarks under Generative Editing: A Benchmark and Analysis of Detection Survival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09702)
*Sung Ju Lee,Nam Ik Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统评测了编辑操作对八种水印方法可检测性的影响，提出以干净分数分离度 $d'$ 作为经验诊断指标来解释编辑后的存活差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通基于提示的编辑会在并未刻意针对水印的情况下导致水印检测失败，需要弄清编辑本身与后续失真各自对检测存活的影响，以及聚合检测指标掩盖了哪些差异。

Method: 在四种生成骨干、四种编辑强度、五类语义类别下，用五种编辑器对八种水印方法进行基准测试，并加入编辑有效性与阈值检查；将编辑与七种后续失真分离考察，进行顺序编辑实验，并做嵌入强度干预与阈值重校准、替代复合权重等稳健性分析。

Result: 仅编辑本身主要能区分 Tree-Ring，加入失真后才暴露出更广的检测存活谱系；顺序编辑显示分数分离度可下降而检测率仍接近上限；标准化的干净分数分离度 $d'$ 能组织复合存活层级，空间重叠对预测仅编辑存活的增益有限；两种方法的嵌入强度干预显示更高干净分离对应更高编辑后分离；HSTR 中裕度对比为正，而匹配干净分离下的角度布局对比仍未解决。

Conclusion: 结果分解与连续分离度揭示了被聚合 TPR 掩盖的差异；方法层级在主要操作点阈值重校准与替代复合权重下保持稳定；干净 $d'$ 在该基准内是有用的经验诊断指标，但向未见方法的迁移效果不一。

Abstract: Ordinary prompt-based editing can cause latent watermark detection to fail without explicitly targeting the watermark. We benchmark eight watermark methods against five editors across four generative backbones, four editing strengths, and five semantic categories, with edit-validity and threshold checks. Separating editing from seven subsequent distortions reveals that editing alone primarily distinguishes Tree-Ring, while added distortions expose a broader spectrum of detection survival. Sequential edits reveal a second hidden difference: score separation can decline while detection rates remain near their ceiling. Across methods, standardized clean score separation ($d'$) organizes composite-survival tiers, whereas spatial overlap adds little to predicting edit-only survival beyond clean detectability. Embedding-strength interventions in two methods link higher clean separation to higher post-edit separation. In HSTR, the margin contrast is positive, while the angular layout contrast at matched clean separation remains unresolved. Together, outcome decomposition and continuous separation expose differences hidden by aggregate TPR. Method tiers are stable under threshold recalibration at the main operating points and alternative composite weights. Clean $d'$ is thus a useful empirical diagnostic within this benchmark, with mixed transfer to unseen methods. Code and supporting artifacts are planned for a separate release.

</details>


### [82] [Enhancing Multi-Region Stylization with Interior-Guided Boundary Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09706)
*Hong-Son Nguyen,Thi-Ngoc-Hanh Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量、模型无关的内景引导边界修复（IGBR）方法，解决多区域风格化中的边界伪影问题，无需重训练即可集成，并给出无标注的定量评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于区域的神经风格迁移允许对语义区域独立风格化，实现细粒度艺术控制，但区域合成时常出现边界伪影，降低视觉质量；现有融合技术难以兼顾边界一致性与风格隔离。

Method: IGBR 通过内景引导传播修复边界像素，并仅在目标-背景边界施加基于距离的向内融合，避免物体间风格泄漏；方法由区域约束的公式推导而来，具有闭式解，可无缝集成到现有风格化流程中，无需重新训练。同时提出无需标注风格化图像的量化指标，评估边界一致性、梯度伪影、物体间泄漏与内景保持。

Result: 实验与评估表明，IGBR 持续生成合理的边界，在边界一致性、梯度稳定性和内景保持方面优于现有融合技术。

Conclusion: IGBR 为多区域风格化提供了一种高效、通用的边界处理方案，其闭式解与轻量设计便于实际部署，所提出的无标注评价指标亦有助于该方向的客观比较。

Abstract: Region-based neural style transfer enables fine-grained artistic control by allowing independent stylization of semantic image regions. However, compositing these regions often leads to boundary artifacts, degrading visual quality. We propose Interior-Guided Boundary Repair (IGBR), a lightweight and model-agnostic method that improves boundary handling in multi-region stylization. IGBR repairs boundary pixels using interior-guided propagation and applies inward, distance-based blending restricted to object-background boundaries, preventing inter-object style leakage. The method is derived from a region-wise constrained formulation with a closed-form solution and can be seamlessly integrated into existing stylization pipelines without retraining. To evaluate efficiency of our IGBR, we introduce quantitative metrics that measure boundary consistency, gradient artifacts, inter-object leakage, and interior preservation without requiring annotated stylized images. Our experiments and evaluations demonstrate that the proposed IGBR consistently produces plausible boundaries, outperforming prior blending techniques in boundary consistency, gradient stability, and interior preservation. The code is available at https://github.com/Son-SDT/IGBR.

</details>


### [83] [DynStream: Online Streaming 4D Gaussian Reconstruction of Dynamic Worlds from Unposed Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09720)
*Dingwei Xian,Xiaoyu Zhou,Yajiao Xiong,Yongtao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynStream：面向长时、无位姿视频流的在线4D高斯重建框架，可实现全局一致的高保真动态重建与渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从长时、无位姿的流式视频中在线重建动态4D场景，需要同时满足连续处理与照片级渲染，但现有方法难以兼顾：前馈高斯方法只能离线处理，在线点云方法则难以保持稠密几何与高保真渲染。

Method: 提出DynStream框架。给定连续视频流，在局部时间窗口内进行场景重建，并增量地对齐与融合局部重建结果，形成全局一致的场景，无需逐场景优化；同时联合约束跨窗口几何一致性与时变场景内容建模。

Result: 实验表明，DynStream能够在长视频流上实现高保真在线动态重建与渲染，在多种动态室内与室外场景中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: DynStream实现了长时无位姿视频流的在线4D重建与高质量渲染，兼顾连续处理与照片级渲染，超越现有方法。

Abstract: Online reconstruction of dynamic 4D scenes from long, unposed streaming videos requires both continuous processing and photorealistic rendering, which existing methods struggle to achieve simultaneously. Existing feed-forward Gaussian methods are restricted to offline processing, whereas online point-cloud approaches struggle to maintain dense geometry and high-fidelity rendering. We present DynStream, a framework for streaming 4D Gaussian reconstruction from long, unposed videos. Given a continuous video stream, DynStream reconstructs the scene within local temporal windows and incrementally aligns and fuses these local reconstructions into a globally consistent scene, enabling online 4D reconstruction without per-scene optimization. By jointly enforcing cross-window geometric consistency and modeling time-varying scene content, DynStream supports efficient reconstruction and photorealistic rendering over extended video streams. Experiments demonstrate that DynStream enables high-fidelity online dynamic reconstruction and rendering from long video streams, achieving state-of-the-art performance across diverse dynamic indoor and outdoor scenes.

</details>


### [84] [MeshCarve: Artisan Mesh Generation with Flow Matching in Compact Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09723)
*Xiyu Wang,Ruocheng Wu,Yufei Wang,Zhihao Li,Lanqing Guo,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeshCarve提出一种在紧凑隐空间中进行流匹配的艺术家网格生成方法，将顶点位置与边连接分开生成，避免联合隐空间的重建质量下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归网格生成方法推理慢；而流匹配连续隐空间方法在几何与拓扑联合编码时重建质量显著下降，压缩隐空间后更严重。

Method: 提出MeshCarve：分别生成顶点位置和边连接以规避联合紧凑隐空间难题；设计分层稀疏Transformer骨干，实例化为VertexVAE与EdgeVAE，两者以离散顶点为锚点减少token序列长度；采用空间感知压缩进一步缩短序列；VertexVAE直接编码顶点占用，边连接方面提出vertex-link编码，将任意连接关系转为定长连续的逐顶点嵌入；结合锚点生成器，在缩短的token序列上做流匹配。

Result: 在Objaverse上优于当前最先进的自回归与流匹配方法，并可泛化到Toys4K；token序列长度仅为以往最压缩自回归与流匹配工作的一小部分。

Conclusion: MeshCarve是首批每个生成阶段都在空间压缩隐空间中运行的艺术家网格生成方法之一，在效率与重建质量上均具优势。

Abstract: Prior artisan mesh generation works largely predict face tokens autoregressively, which makes inference slow. Recent methods instead flow match continuous latents built by Variational AutoEncoders (VAEs), but reconstruction quality drops significantly when geometry and topology are jointly encoded, and further when the latent space is compressed. We present MeshCarve, a flow matching method that generates entirely in compact latent spaces, generating vertex positions and edge connections separately and sidestepping the difficulty of a joint compact latent. To shorten the token sequence, we propose a hierarchical sparse transformer backbone, instantiated as VertexVAE and EdgeVAE. Instead of encoding fields over the surface voxels, both VAEs anchor on discrete vertices in their latent spaces, which drastically reduces the token sequence length, and our spatial-aware compression shortens it further without costing reconstruction. VertexVAE directly encodes vertex occupancy. For connectivity, we propose vertex-link encoding, which turns arbitrary connectivity between vertices into fixed-length continuous per-vertex embeddings and recovers complex artistic topology faithfully. MeshCarve combines these VAEs with an anchor generator and flow matches on the shortened token sequences. It shows advantages over state-of-the-art autoregressive and flow matching methods on Objaverse and generalizes to Toys4K. To the best of our knowledge, it is among the first artisan mesh generation methods whose every generative stage runs in a spatially compressed latent, with a token sequence only a fraction of the most compressed previous autoregressive and flow matching works.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Group Splits: Specimen-Level Cross-Validation and Visual Attribution for Remaining-Shelf-Life Regression in Climacteric Fruit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09726)
*Rovhona Mudau,Jean Frederic Isingizwe Nturambirwe,Clement Nthambazale Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文基于Hass牛油果成熟度数据集，量化了观测级与个体级评估协议对剩余货架期预测性能的影响，发现个体级评估会显著降低（更保守）性能估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从图像估计易腐农产品的剩余货架期（RSL）可提供低成本的决策支持，但当同一生物个体有重复图像时，评估协议会显著影响报告的性能。作者希望厘清评估协议偏差并评估轻量模型在纵向货架期预测中的适用性。

Method: 使用Hass牛油果成熟度数据集（426个果实、三种储藏条件，共8,834个图像-RSL配对），在冻结的ImageNet预训练视觉骨干上接轻量回归头。量化观测级与个体分离（specimen-disjoint）评估的差异，在个体分离交叉验证下比较轻量与较重骨干，并用Grad-CAM分析空间归因。

Result: 观测级随机划分（十次）平均RMSE为2.37天（标准差0.03天）；个体分离5折交叉验证平均RMSE为3.12天（标准差0.11天），对应平均决定系数为0.553。配对个体比较确认个体分离评估误差更高（p<0.001，426个个体）。在个体分离评估下，MobileNetV3-Small（0.93M参数）精度与ResNet-18相当但吞吐量显著更高，Grad-CAM显示轻量骨干间空间归因存在差异。

Conclusion: 观测级数据划分会给该数据集和模型配置带来明显过于乐观的性能估计，因此评估轻量视觉模型进行纵向货架期预测时应采用个体分离评估并辅以归因分析。

Abstract: Estimating remaining shelf life (RSL) from images could provide affordable decision support for perishable produce, but evaluation protocols can substantially affect reported performance when repeated images are available from the same biological specimen. We use the Hass Avocado Ripening dataset, comprising 8,834 image-RSL pairs from 426 fruits across three storage regimes, to evaluate a frozen ImageNet-pretrained visual backbone with a lightweight regression head. Our contributions are threefold: we quantify the effect of observation-level versus specimen-disjoint evaluation, compare lightweight and heavier visual backbones under specimen-disjoint cross-validation, and examine their spatial attributions using Grad-CAM. Across ten observation-level random splits, the model achieves a mean RMSE of 2.37 days with a standard deviation of 0.03 days, whereas specimen-disjoint 5-fold cross-validation yields a mean RMSE of 3.12 days with a standard deviation of 0.11 days. The corresponding mean coefficient of determination is 0.553. A matched per-specimen comparison confirms higher error under specimen-disjoint evaluation, with a probability value below 0.001 across 426 specimens, showing that observation-level partitioning gives a substantially more optimistic estimate for this dataset and model configuration. Under specimen-disjoint evaluation, MobileNetV3-Small (0.93 million parameters) achieves accuracy comparable to ResNet-18 while providing substantially higher throughput, and Grad-CAM reveals differences in spatial attribution between the lightweight backbones. These results support specimen-disjoint evaluation and attribution analysis when assessing lightweight vision models for longitudinal shelf-life prediction.

</details>


### [86] [SoccerNet-FoulRet: Retrieving Semantically Similar Soccer Foul Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09742)
*Jacobus Arthur,Ahmad Sait,Batool Hani,Merey Ramazanova,Jan Held,Marc Van Droogenbroeck,Bernard Ghanem,Anthony Cioppa,Silvio Giancola*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个语义犯规检索基准SoccerNet-FoulRet，将裁判判罚一致性问题建模为检索问题，并在693条人工验证查询上评估了零样本与微调模型，结果显示语义犯规检索仍是开放难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业足球判罚不一致的根源在于裁判难以将当前争议犯规与历史相似判例进行对比。现有视频检索仅关注视觉相似或事件相同，无法刻画"判罚解释相关性"，因此需要新的任务设定与基准。

Method: 作者将问题定义为语义犯规检索：给定查询犯规，检索被判为相关判例的历史犯规，且不受机位、球队、外观影响。基于SoccerNet-MVFoul数据集构建SoccerNet-FoulRet基准，包含693条人工验证查询及类别相关性标签，并评估零样本视频/视觉-语言嵌入器与任务特定微调基线。

Result: 语义犯规检索仍具挑战性：最强零样本模型在人工验证判例上的HitRate@10不足5%；类别监督微调虽提升类别相关性，但迁移到判例检索时增益有限。

Conclusion: 作者发布SoccerNet-FoulRet，将语义犯规检索确立为一个开放问题，推动后续研究。

Abstract: Refereeing decisions in professional soccer remain inconsistent because referees cannot easily compare a contentious foul against similar past cases. We cast this as a retrieval problem and introduce SoccerNet-FoulRet, the first benchmark for semantic foul retrieval. Given a query foul, the task is to retrieve past fouls judged to be relevant precedents, regardless of camera angle, teams, or appearance. This differs from prior video-to-video retrieval, which matches clips by visual similarity or a shared event. Here, relevance is defined by refereeing interpretation. We build the benchmark from the SoccerNet-MVFoul dataset and evaluate retrieval ability of zero-shot video and vision-language embedders together with a task-specific fine-tuned baseline on 693 human-verified queries and category-relevance labels. Semantic foul retrieval remains challenging. The strongest zero-shot model achieves under 5% HitRate@10 on human-verified precedents, while category-supervised fine-tuning improves category relevance but transfers only modestly to precedent retrieval. We release SoccerNet-FoulRet to establish semantic foul retrieval as an open problem: https://github.com/SoccerNet/sn-foulret.

</details>


### [87] [Flow-of-Thought: A Framework for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09746)
*Mariia Baidachna,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Flow-of-Thought (FoT) 框架，将视觉草图生成作为中间推理步骤，模仿人类心理意象，以提升空间理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型和视觉 Transformer 进展迅速，但在需要空间理解的任务上仍表现不佳。人类心理意象（"用心灵之眼观看"）是认知的重要方面，作者希望借鉴这一机制来弥补模型在空间推理上的不足。

Method: FoT 框架在 SO(2) 群轨道和累积最短路径上训练坐标感知的轨迹流场，然后冻结所学动态；通过前景加权重构能量比较竞争性生成假设，完成"相同 vs. 不同"的判断。

Result: 在锁定测试中，FoT 在 Tetris 上达到 100.0% 准确率，在彩色形状任务上达到 99.0%。在冻结迁移下，基于轨道训练的 2D 流在 BLINK Multi-view 的 133 个公开验证对上优于仅端点对照（72.2% vs. 63.9%）。

Conclusion: 连续视觉痕迹是一种有效且可解释的空间推理表征，在某些分布外场景中表现良好。

Abstract: Mental imagery, ``seeing with the mind's eye'' is an essential aspect of human cognition. Despite rapid progress Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) still underperform on tasks requiring spatial understanding. To address this, we introduce Flow-of-Thought (FoT), a framework that integrates the generation of visual sketches as intermediate reasoning steps, mimicking mental imagery in humans. We train coordinate-aware trajectory flow fields on $SO(2)$ group orbits and cumulative shortest paths, then freeze the learned dynamics; same vs. different decisions compare competing generative hypotheses using foreground-weighted reconstruction energy. On locked tests FoT reaches 100.0% accuracy on Tetris and 99.0% on colored shapes. Under frozen transfer, the orbit-trained 2D flow improves over its endpoint-only control on BLINK Multi-view (72.2% vs. 63.9% on 133 public validation pairs), supporting continuous visual traces as an effective and interpretable representation for spatial reasoning in some out-of-distribution settings.

</details>


### [88] [Diffusion-Generated Image Watermarking: A Two-Axis Taxonomy and Three Protocol-Bounded Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09755)
*Sung Ju Lee,Nam Ik Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对扩散生成图像水印方法进行系统梳理，提出沿"插入机制"和"主要信号载体表示"两条轴线的分类体系，并形式化了一个代表性的 z_T-Fourier 验证/识别流程，通过三个协议受限案例研究探讨频率完整性、持久性、隐空间相位调制等问题，同时区分四类内容级攻击与模型/流程适配，给出评估协议与可检验猜想，强调构建"协议感知、可匹配比较"的框架而非普适排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成图像水印需在来源溯源信号与图像质量、鲁棒性、计算成本之间取得平衡，但现有方法缺乏统一的比较框架，难以进行公平、可复现的评估。本文旨在建立结构化分类与协议化评估框架，以支持匹配、协议感知的系统比较。

Method: 1）沿两条轴线（插入机制、主要信号承载表示）组织现有水印方法，构建分类体系；2）形式化一个代表性的 z_T-Fourier 水印流程用于验证与识别；3）基于该分类开展三个协议受限案例研究：频率完整性与检测/质量/裁剪行为关联、种子相关与独立编辑下的持久性（并提出范围受限的"语义印记假说"）、单次 VAE 隐空间相位调制的效率与再生鲁棒性及鲁棒性-质量操作点；4）区分四类内容级攻击与模型/流程适配，提出评估协议与可检验猜想，并指出面向视频的时间扩展方向。

Result: 研究显示：频率完整性与检测、质量、裁剪行为之间存在关联；水印在种子相关与种子独立编辑下具有不同持久性表现，并提出语义印记假说；单次 VAE 隐空间相位调制在效率与再生鲁棒性方面具备一定优势，并刻画了鲁棒性与质量的操作点权衡；同时识别了参数调优类威胁及视频时序扩展需求。整体并未得出普适排名。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于提供一个结构化的分类与协议化评估框架，支持扩散生成图像水印系统之间进行匹配的、协议感知的比较，而非给出绝对优劣排序，为后续研究与标准化评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Watermarking diffusion-generated images requires balancing provenance signals with image quality, robustness, and computational cost. This work organizes methods along two axes: insertion mechanism and primary signal-bearing representation, and formalizes a representative $z_T$-Fourier pipeline for verification and identification. We then use the taxonomy to structure three protocol-bounded case studies. The first examines associations among frequency integrity, detection, quality, and cropping behavior. The second revisits persistence under seed-linked and seed-independent editing and formulates a scoped Semantic Imprinting Hypothesis without claiming a localized carrier or causal mechanism. The third studies single-shot VAE-latent phase modulation, including its efficiency, regeneration robustness, and robustness--quality operating points. Finally, we separate four content-level attack families from model/pipeline adaptation, propose corresponding evaluation protocols and testable conjectures for parameter-tuning threats, and identify additional temporal extensions for video. These analyses do not establish a universal ranking; instead, they provide a framework for matched, protocol-aware comparisons of watermarking systems for diffusion-generated images.

</details>


### [89] [Relational Abstractions for Spatial Reasoning with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09780)
*Ana Ezquerro,Ozan Özdenizci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合无监督物体发现与物体关系抽象的扩散模型框架，以增强结构化空间推理能力，并发布大规模生成式空间推理基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像合成上表现优异，但在满足结构化空间推理约束方面存在局限。在具有隐式逻辑结构的条件数据分布建模任务中（如由可见线索与一致解构成的谜题），当前最优生成模型倾向于逼近像素空间分布，而未学习推理所需的底层逻辑规则。

Method: 提出一个面向空间推理的扩散模型新框架，利用无监督物体发现和物体关系抽象。由物体中心表示导出的关系知识为扩散模型提供结构原语，在训练和推理阶段引导生成式表示空间，从而实现满足推理约束的条件图像生成。同时构建了包含四个数据集的、受人类可解谜题启发的大规模生成式空间推理基准。

Result: 关系抽象显著提升了扩散模型在多种复杂推理任务上的推理能力，并在分布外设置下实现了稳健的泛化。

Conclusion: 通过将物体中心表示与关系抽象引入扩散模型，可有效弥补其在结构化空间推理方面的不足，并提升分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at image synthesis, but they remain limited in their ability to reliably satisfy structured spatial reasoning constraints. In conditional data distribution modeling tasks with implicit logical structure, such as puzzles defined by visible clues paired with consistent solutions, state-of-the-art generative models tend to approximate pixel-space distributions without learning the underlying logical rules required for inference. To address this limitation, we present a novel framework for spatial reasoning with diffusion models that leverages unsupervised object discovery and abstractions of object relations. We show that the relational knowledge derived from object-centric representations enriches diffusion models with structural primitives, allowing them to effectively guide the generative representation space during both training and inference, and enabling conditional image generation that satisfies reasoning constraints. Additionally, we introduce a large-scale generative spatial reasoning benchmark with four datasets inspired by human-solvable puzzles. Our results show that relational abstractions significantly improve reasoning capabilities of diffusion models on a variety of complex reasoning tasks, while enabling robust generalization in out-of-distribution settings.

</details>


### [90] [CIRSeg: Coarse-to-Fine Intensity-Robust Liver Segmentation with Source-Free Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09784)
*Ruoshi Xu,Mingqi Gao,Shengda Luo,Jingkun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CIRSeg——基于nnU-Netv2的粗到细、强度鲁棒的肝脏分割框架，结合3D CutMix、随机强度迁移、级联架构、无源测试时自适应及最大连通域后处理，在CARE 2026测试集上域内与未见域Dice分别达97.13%和97.93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比增强MRI肝脏分割对定量评估、治疗规划和纵向监测至关重要，但面临标注数据有限、扫描仪/厂商导致的强度差异，易造成过拟合和跨域泛化差；同时难以兼顾全局定位与边界精细勾画，且预测结果可能存在孤立的假阳性区域。

Method: 基于nnU-Netv2的粗到细肝脏分割框架：结合3D CutMix与随机强度迁移（Nyul增强或直方图匹配）提升对异质MRI强度的鲁棒性；级联架构将低分辨率解剖定位与全分辨率边界精修解耦；推理阶段采用基于置信度过滤预测和概率先验正则化的无源测试时自适应提升对分布外输入的鲁棒性；最后以最大连通域滤波作为确定性后处理去除孤立假阳性区域。

Result: 在CARE 2026测试集上，CIRSeg在域内和未见域子集上分别取得Dice 97.13%和97.93%，对应HD95分别为20.18 mm和11.30 mm，表明在域内及未见采集设置下均能实现一致准确的分割。

Conclusion: CIRSeg通过强度鲁棒增强、级联粗到细架构、无源测试时自适应及连通域后处理，有效缓解了数据有限、强度异质和边界勾画难题，在域内与未见域均实现高精度肝脏分割，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reliable liver segmentation in contrast-enhanced MRI is essential for quantitative hepatic assessment, treatment planning, and longitudinal disease monitoring. However, limited annotated data and scanner- or vendor-dependent intensity variations can cause overfitting and poor generalization to unseen acquisition domains. Moreover, simultaneously achieving robust global localization and precise boundary delineation remains challenging, while predictions may contain isolated false-positive regions outside the main liver component. To address these challenges, we propose CIRSeg, a coarse-to-fine, intensity-robust liver segmentation framework based on nnU-Netv2. CIRSeg combines 3D CutMix with stochastic intensity transfer using either Nyul augmentation or histogram matching to improve robustness to heterogeneous MRI intensities. Its cascaded architecture decouples low-resolution anatomical localization from full-resolution boundary refinement. At inference, source-free test-time adaptation based on confidence-filtered predictions and probability-prior regularization further improves robustness to out-of-distribution inputs. As a final deterministic post-processing step, largest connected component filtering removes isolated false-positive regions. On the CARE 2026 test set, CIRSeg achieves Dice scores of 97.13\% and 97.93\% on the in-domain and unseen-domain subsets, with corresponding HD95 values of 20.18 mm and 11.30 mm, respectively. These results demonstrate consistently accurate segmentation across both in-domain and unseen acquisition settings. The code is available at https://github.com/jingkunchen/MICCAI_CARE_2026

</details>


### [91] [UltraWorld: Learning Interactive Ultrasound World Models from Untracked Clinical Videos with Acoustic Sampling Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09785)
*Keke Yang,Erqi Wang,Sainan Guan,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraWorld提出自蒸馏方案，将临床超声视频先验迁移为交互式世界模型，无需真实动作标注即可实现自主超声扫描规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声自主扫描需要世界模型预测探头动作的结果，但学习动作-观测关系通常依赖同步的视频-姿态对，临床常规记录中此类数据稀缺且采集成本高；同时还需建模超声的横断面采样几何。

Method: 从临床视频出发，将视频基础模型适配为以参考图像和解剖掩码为条件的超声生成器；沿可编程轨迹在3D解剖中采样解剖掩码提供空间引导，合成动作-视频对；再用这些合成对把生成器自蒸馏为世界模型，仅在局部观测和动作下预测未来观测，推理时无需掩码或3D资产。并引入声学采样图（AsMap），以像素级3D采样位置、波束方向和深度表示探头位姿与成像设置。

Result: 实验表明预测保真度和动作跟随性均有提升；在九个模拟闭环局部规划回合中，与视觉伺服相比，最终到目标平均距离和方向误差分别降低29%和38%。

Conclusion: 无需真实动作标注，即可借助临床视频先验和合成动作-视频对蒸馏出可交互超声世界模型，并借助AsMap提升动作跟随能力，为自主超声扫描提供有效方案。

Abstract: World models can enable autonomous ultrasound scanning by predicting the outcomes of probe motions from local observations. Learning this action--observation relationship typically relies on synchronized video--pose pairs, which are costly to collect at scale and largely unavailable in routine clinical recordings. Reliable action following further requires modeling ultrasound's cross-sectional sampling geometry. We present UltraWorld, a self-distillation recipe that transfers priors from clinical ultrasound videos into interactive world models without real action annotations. Starting from clinical videos, we adapt a video foundation model into an ultrasound generator conditioned on reference images and anatomical masks. Anatomical masks sampled along programmable trajectories through 3D anatomy provide spatial guidance for synthesizing action--video pairs. We then use these synthetic pairs to self-distill the generator into a world model that predicts future observations from local observations and actions, without requiring anatomical masks or other 3D assets at inference time. To further improve action following, we introduce the Acoustic Sampling Map (AsMap), which represents probe poses and imaging settings as pixel-wise 3D sampling positions, beam directions, and depths. Experiments demonstrate improved prediction fidelity and action following. Across nine simulated closed-loop local planning episodes, UltraWorld reduces the mean final distance to the goal and orientation error by 29\% and 38\%, respectively, compared with visual servoing. Project Page: https://ultraworld-project.github.io/.

</details>


### [92] [Counterfactual Route Optimization for Gaussian Head Avatar Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09791)
*Shikun Zhang,Yong Li,Yiqun Wang,Qiuhong Ke,Cunjian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出反事实路径优化框架，用于高斯头部虚拟人建模，通过状态相关的优化偏好动态调整损失权重，在NeRSemble数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头部虚拟人建模需联合优化几何、外观和跨视角一致性等多个目标，其相对效果随训练状态变化，但现有流程依赖固定损失权重或人工设计的阶段式调度，难以判断每个训练状态下哪个优化方向更有益。

Method: 从相同训练状态出发，对几何、外观和联合更新路径进行短时域受限前瞻，在统一效用下评估结果；将反事实证据分解为几何—外观偏好与残差联合优势，分别刻画单方向相对偏好和协同优化的额外收益；再将离线证据摊销为轻量控制器，在全量优化中直接估计当前优化偏好并施加有界调制。

Result: 在NeRSemble数据集上验证了设计有效性，持续优于现有方法，并保留了更清晰的局部面部结构和更精细的细节。

Conclusion: 以反事实证据驱动的状态相关偏好估计可替代固定权重与人工调度，有效提升高斯头部虚拟人建模的几何、外观与一致性表现。

Abstract: Head avatar modeling requires jointly optimizing multiple objectives with different dominant effects on geometry, appearance, and cross-view consistency. However, their relative effectiveness varies across training states, while existing pipelines typically rely on fixed loss weights or handcrafted stage-wise schedules. A central challenge is therefore to identify which optimization direction is more beneficial at each training state. We propose a counterfactual route optimization framework for Gaussian head avatar modeling, which characterizes state-dependent optimization preference from the realized effects of alternative updates rather than predefined heuristic weighting. Starting from the same training state, we perform short-horizon route-restricted lookahead over geometry, appearance, and joint update routes and evaluate their outcomes under a unified utility. The resulting counterfactual evidence is factorized into a geometry--appearance preference and a residual joint advantage, separately capturing the relative preference between individual update directions and the additional benefit of coordinated optimization. We further amortize this offline evidence into a lightweight controller that directly estimates the current optimization preference and applies bounded modulation to the training objectives during full avatar optimization. Experiments on the NeRSemble dataset validate the effectiveness of the proposed design, consistently outperforming existing methods while preserving clearer local facial structures and finer details.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Masks and Trajectories: Flow-Guided Latent Action Injection for Stable Surgical Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09800)
*Tsz-Yui Qin,Siyu Zhou,Chi-Keung Tang,Yuxiang Nie,Shu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FLAIR框架，从光流学习动作先验，动态预测潜在动作表示并注入冻结基座模型，实现无参考条件的外科手术视频生成，同时构建SurgActionClip-30K数据集与SurgMetrics评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科手术视频生成对医学教育、仿真和数据增强有重要价值，但现有方法多依赖掩码、轨迹、深度或参考视频等辅助条件，这些条件需要额外人工标注或专门采集，难以扩展到大规模数据集，因此亟需一种在推理时无需辅助视觉条件的无参考架构。

Method: FLAIR（Flow-guided Latent Action Injection）：从真实手术视频的光流中学习动作先验，根据输入提示动态预测对应的潜在动作表示，并将其注入冻结的基座模型，以提升生成视频的动作一致性。此外构建SurgActionClip-30K大规模手术视觉数据集（含动作中心的分段片段与结构化描述标签），并提出SurgMetrics手术领域专用评价指标。

Result: 大量实验表明，FLAIR可在仅使用文本推理、无需辅助条件的情况下生成高质量手术视频；在SurgMetrics上的验证显示其与人类感知的一致性优于传统指标。

Conclusion: FLAIR为无参考条件下的手术视频生成提供了可行方案，并通过数据集与领域专用评价指标弥补了该领域在数据与评估标准上的空白。

Abstract: Surgical video generation holds substantial potential for surgical education, simulation, and data augmentation, yet generating surgical videos with realistic and clinically plausible motion remains challenging. Most existing methods rely on auxiliary conditions, such as masks, trajectories, depth, or reference videos, to achieve visually plausible synthesis. Yet, these auxiliary conditions typically require additional manual annotation or specialized acquisition, making it difficult to scale such methods beyond small, curated datasets. This motivates the need for a reference-free architecture capable of generating high-quality surgical video without requiring auxiliary visual conditions at inference time. We propose FLAIR, a Flow-guided LatentAction Injection framework for Reference-free surgical video generation. FLAIR learns action priors from optical flow of real surgical videos, dynamically predicts corresponding latent action representation from an input prompt, and injects it into a frozen base model to generate surgical videos with improved action consistency. We further construct SurgActionClip-30K, the first large-scale surgical vision dataset comprising action-centric segmented clips and structured caption labels, addressing the persistent lack of fine-grained, action-centric surgical datasets. Lastly, we introduce SurgMetrics, the first surgical domain-specific evaluation metrics for quantifying the quality of generated surgical videos, addressing the persistent absence of clinically grounded evaluation standards in this domain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FLAIR enables generating high-quality surgical videos using text-only inference without auxiliary conditions, and validation in SurgMetrics demonstrates its strength in alignment with human perception compared to traditional metrics.

</details>


### [94] [DisParQ: Self-Supervised Part Concepts for Interpretable Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09802)
*Adam Pardyl,Siddhartha Gairola,Sukrut Rao,Adam Wróbel,Bartosz Zieliński,Bernt Schiele,Dawid Rymarczyk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DisParQ：从冻结的纯视觉自监督骨干中学习空间定位的离散概念表示，无需类别标签和语言监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于概念的视觉模型通过可人工检验的中间概念层来表示图像，使模型依赖可追溯到概念。但现有模型常受限于固定类别或依赖语言定义概念。

Method: 提出DisParQ（Discrete Parts with Quantized attributes）。每个图像块被分配到一个可学习原型字典中的唯一概念，每张图仅稀疏子集概念可激活；学习连续残差并量化为离散属性以捕捉概念跨图像变化；空间解码器仅从概念和属性重建骨干表示，成功重建意味着离散表示保留了骨干信息。

Result: 在七个数据集上评估（ImageNet、PartImageNet、Places及CUB、Cars、Dogs、Flowers等细粒度基准）。ImageNet线性探针达83.2% top-1，接近冻结DINOv2教师；概念一致性高于语言对齐模型，细粒度识别具竞争力，支持跨类别部件检索。

Conclusion: DisParQ表明可从纯视觉自监督骨干中无标签、无语言地学习空间定位的离散概念表示，兼顾概念可解释性与识别性能。

Abstract: Concept-based vision models represent images through an intermediate layer of human-inspectable concepts, so what a model relies on can be traced to those concepts. However, those models are often limited to fixed categories or depend on language to define their concepts. We introduce DisParQ (Discrete Parts with Quantized attributes), a method that learns spatially grounded, discrete concept representations from a powerful frozen vision-only self-supervised backbone. It requires no class labels and no language supervision. Each image patch is assigned to exactly one concept from a learnable prototype dictionary, and only a sparse subset of concepts may activate per image. To capture how each concept varies across images (e.g., the type of a "wheel"), we learn continuous residuals alongside the concepts and then quantize them into discrete attributes. A spatial decoder reconstructs the backbone's representation from the concepts and attributes alone, so successful reconstruction means that the discrete representation preserves the backbone's information. We evaluate DisParQ across seven datasets, from general recognition (ImageNet, PartImageNet, Places) to fine-grained benchmarks (CUB, Cars, Dogs, Flowers). We show that DisParQ closely matches its frozen DINOv2 teacher on ImageNet linear probing (83.2% top-1), achieves higher concept consistency than language-aligned models, remains competitive on fine-grained recognition, and enables cross-category part-based retrieval.

</details>


### [95] [Concentration, Not Uncertainty: Why Targeted Synthetic Data Doesn't Help Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09807)
*Akshat Dobhal,Sanjay Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在固定合成数据生成预算下，不确定性引导的区域选择并不优于随机分配；观测到的训练集效应源于预算集中，而非不确定性定位；此外发现 CHAMELEON 数据集存在严重数据污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装目标检测需要像素级精确掩码，人工标注成本高、速度慢，因此合成训练图像成为有吸引力的替代方案。但在固定生成预算下，应针对哪些真实图像区域生成合成数据尚不明确，本文即考察不确定性引导的生成策略是否有效。

Method: 提出不确定性引导的生成策略：对未标注真实图像进行聚类，识别模型最不确定的簇，将合成数据生成预算向这些簇倾斜，并迭代重训练模型。共进行 103 次训练运行，并设计五项独立对照实验（如针对模型最确定的簇、以及其他集中规则），以检验该零结果是否为假象。

Result: 在 103 次训练运行中，基于不确定性的目标选择未优于随机分配。五项对照表明该零结果并非假象：目标训练集与随机集确实存在可测量差异，但该差异源于生成预算的集中，而非不确定性集中的位置——所有集中规则都能复现该效应，甚至在边界精度上，瞄准模型最确定的簇也能产生相同效应。此外，CHAMELEON 存在严重数据污染：76 张图像中有 50 张与训练数据重复，而标准重叠检查却报告零重叠。

Conclusion: 在固定合成数据预算下，是预算集中而非不确定性引导的目标选择解释了所观察到的训练集效应；同时提醒数据集重叠检测可能失效，需警惕隐性数据污染。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection requires pixel-accurate masks, but obtaining such annotations is slow and costly, making synthetic training images an attractive alternative. Under a fixed generation budget, however, it remains unclear which real-image regions to target for synthetic data generation. We study an uncertainty-guided generation strategy that clusters the unlabelled real images, identifies clusters on which the model is least certain, allocates synthetic generation toward those clusters, and iteratively retrains the model. Across 103 training runs, uncertainty-based targeting does not outperform random allocation. Five independent controls further show that this null result is not an artifact: targeted training sets are measurably different from random sets, but the difference is explained by concentrating the generation budget rather than by where uncertainty is concentrated, as every concentration rule we test reproduces the effect and, on boundary accuracy, so does aiming at the clusters the model was most certain about. Separately, we find substantial data contamination in CHAMELEON, with 50 of its 76 images duplicated from training data despite the standard overlap check reporting zero overlap. Together, these results show that, under a fixed synthetic-data budget, budget concentration, not uncertainty-based targeting, accounts for the observed training-set effects.

</details>


### [96] [Efficient 3D Gaussian Head Avatars for Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09821)
*Umar Farooq,Jean-Yves Guillemaut,Adrian Hilton,Marco Volino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种高效生成器架构，通过参数高效合成模块和深度可分离卷积，在保持生成质量的同时大幅降低3D高斯头部头像合成的计算成本，并支持CPU和移动端浏览器部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式3D高斯头部头像虽渲染质量高、效率好，但合成高斯表示的计算开销很大，难以在资源受限的边缘设备上部署。

Method: 基于参数高效的合成模块与深度可分离卷积，构建无条件3D高斯头部合成的高效生成器架构，同时保留基于样式的条件机制；无需模型压缩或量化。

Result: 相比基线模型，FLOPs降低94%，参数量减少70%，模型体积缩小81%，同时保持有竞争力的生成质量；并借助ONNX Runtime实现CPU推理和移动端浏览器执行。

Conclusion: 该架构可在无专用GPU硬件或特定软件的情况下实现3D高斯头像合成；除常规图像质量指标外，还评估了多视角一致性、训练成本和部署性能，代码、模型与评估工具将公开。

Abstract: Generative 3D Gaussian head avatars provide high-quality, efficient rendering, but synthesising the Gaussian representation remains computationally expensive, limiting deployment on resource-constrained and edge devices. We introduce an efficient generator architecture for unconditional 3D Gaussian head synthesis, based on a parameter-efficient synthesis block and depth-wise separable convolutions while retaining style-based conditioning. Our architecture reduces generator complexity without requiring model compression or quantisation. Compared with the baseline model, our approach reduces FLOPs by 94%, parameter count by 70%, and model size by 81%, while maintaining competitive generation quality. We further demonstrate practical CPU inference and browser-based execution on mobile devices using ONNX Runtime, enabling 3D Gaussian avatar synthesis without dedicated GPU hardware or application-specific software. In addition to conventional image-quality metrics, we evaluate multi-view consistency, training cost, and deployment performance. Code, trained models, and evaluation tools will be released publicly.

</details>


### [97] [UltraText Bench: A Comprehensive Bilingual Benchmark for Evaluating Visual Text Rendering in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09823)
*Deyuan Liu,Yihao Hu,Jingxuan Zhang,Xingying Li,Jun Xie,Jiacheng Liu,Jungang Li,Yu Huang,Xuanyi Liu,Yue Ding,Zecheng Wang,Lei Zhao,Mingda Wang,Zhenglin Cheng,Peng Sun,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UltraText Bench，一个面向密集视觉文本生成的英汉双语基准，含432条提示、24个场景类别、三级难度，并用 Q-Judger 多维度评估24个模型配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短文本渲染已趋成熟，但图像生成模型在多区域、长字符串的密集文本场景下能否保持稳定性能尚缺乏系统评测，因此需要一个更具挑战性的基准来检验持续生成能力。

Method: 构建 UltraText Bench：432条人工审核提示，覆盖24类真实场景与三个难度等级，英中各半；每条提示指定4至12个文本区域的精确字符串及结构化参考（内容、位置、视觉属性）。采用 Q-Judger 视觉语言模型，对生成图像与完整参考进行比对，报告文本保真度、文本清晰度、空间质量和场景质量四个指标。

Result: 在24个模型配置上，各指标揭示不同优劣势：Z-Image-Turbo 相比 Z-Image-Base 清晰度提升3.81分，但保真度下降14.76分；性能随任务负荷变化，Qwen-Image-2512 的英文综合分从 L1 的86.50降至 L3 的42.86。另由10名参与者对自动评分进行了人工评估。

Conclusion: 密集视觉文本生成仍是未解决的难题，不同模型在保真度与清晰度间存在权衡，且随文本密度增加性能显著下降；该基准为后续研究与评测提供了统一平台与参考。

Abstract: Dense visual text requires image generators to reproduce long strings across multiple regions with correct placement and legibility. As short-string rendering improves, evaluation must test sustained performance across more demanding scenes. We introduce UltraText Bench, a bilingual benchmark for prompt-only generation of dense visual text. It contains 432 prompts spanning 24 real-world scene categories and three difficulty levels, split equally between English and Chinese. Each human-reviewed prompt supplies exact strings for four to twelve text regions, paired with structured references for their content, placement, and visual attributes. We use the Q-Judger vision-language model to assess each image against the complete reference, reporting text fidelity, text clarity, spatial quality, and scene quality. Across 24 model configurations, these dimensions reveal different strengths: Z-Image-Turbo gains 3.81 clarity points over Z-Image-Base while losing 14.76 fidelity points under the reported settings. Performance also varies with workload; Qwen-Image-2512's English composite falls from 86.50 at L1 to 42.86 at L3. Ten participants took part in human evaluation of the automatic scores. Repository: https://github.com/LINs-lab/UltraText_Bench.

</details>


### [98] [MOTIF: Person-of-Interest Deepfake Detection Beyond 3DMM Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09830)
*Giovanni Affatato,Sara Mandelli,Paolo Bestagini,Stefano Tubaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文拆解了基于3DMM系数的POI深度伪造检测器，发现形状系数几乎承载全部判别力，并提出利用被忽视的稠密表面信息的纯视觉检测器MOTIF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对特定人物（POI）的深度伪造危害最大，且公众人物素材充足可构建定制检测器，但现有方法将3DMM系数整体使用，从未量化各部分对检测信号的贡献，也丢弃了拟合返回的稠密表面信息。

Method: 固定编码器、训练语料与注册协议，仅变化编码器所观察的输入，系统拆解3DMM系数各组分的贡献；并引入同一拟合返回的稠密表面作为额外身份信息。最终整合为MOTIF——仅用真实视频训练、无需伪造视频和POI专属数据的纯视觉检测器。

Result: 各组系数高度冗余，形状块单独即可恢复几乎全部精度，时序演化贡献有限但真实；稠密表面携带系数所不具备的身份信息，且恰好在系数最弱处提供帮助。MOTIF在基准的所有数据集与操纵类型、两个质量层级上均优于现有最先进POI检测器。

Conclusion: 3DMM系数描述存在大量冗余，形状信息最为关键；被丢弃的稠密表面是有效补充。所提MOTIF以纯真实视频训练即实现全面领先，代码将在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Video deepfakes targeting a specific individual, the Person-of-Interest (POI), are the most harmful ones, and, since a public figure is abundantly recorded, a detector can be built from genuine footage of that individual. Such detectors commonly describe a subject through a 3D Morphable Model (3DMM) and adopt its coefficients as a whole, so which part of that description carries the signal has never been measured. We dissect it, holding the encoder, the training corpus and the enrollment protocol fixed and varying only what the encoder observes. The groups of coefficients prove largely redundant, since the shape block alone recovers almost all the accuracy of the full vector, and their temporal evolution contributes a real but bounded amount. We further show that the dense surface the same fit returns, which these detectors discard, carries identity information that the coefficients do not, and that it helps precisely where they are weakest. We assemble the best configuration into MOTIF, a visual-only detector trained on real videos only, with no manipulated video and no POI-specific data. It improves on both state-of-the-art POI detectors in every dataset and manipulation of our benchmark and at two quality levels. Our experimental code will be released at https://github.com/polimi-ispl/MOTIF.

</details>


### [99] [ORCA: Hunting Compositional Failures in Text-to-Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09841)
*Arshia Hemmat,Amirhossein Vahidi,Amitis Shidani,Mohammad Vali Sanian,Hesam Asadollahzadeh,Aryan Yazdan Parast,Mohammad Lotfollahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ORCA 方法，通过显式的跨模态对齐训练信号解决文生图扩散模型的组合性问题，在零推理成本下提升 FID 与 GenEval 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型在组合性提示上可预测地失败：属性绑定错误、空间关系颠倒、多物体场景丢失计数。已有架构虽在 CLIP 之外加入 T5 编码器以保留组合结构，但失败依旧。作者认为问题不是信息缺失，而是信息错位：文本编码器保留了组合结构，但其表示空间由语言建模塑造而非视觉，且去噪目标并未直接奖励两者对齐。

Method: 作者证明这种对应关系可作为显式训练信号提供，且相关跨模态信息集中在自监督视觉特征的低秩子空间中，可折叠为扩散训练中的单一辅助损失。方法 ORCA（正交残差组合对齐）将扩散 Transformer 的隐变量与来自冻结视觉编码器的低秩目标对齐，其预测器的正交基由 T5 与 CLIP 嵌入之间的学习残差参数化，从而提供依赖提示的信号来选择视觉读出子空间。作者还证明给定秩下可恢复的跨模态信息受视觉编码器协方差在前若干主成分上的谱质量所限。

Result: 在三种扩散 Transformer 骨干（DiT-B/2、DiT-L/2、U-ViT-L）上，ORCA 在零推理成本下相较 vanilla 与 REPA 基线提升了 FID 和 GenEval；在 DiT-L/2 上 200K 步达到 FID 16.65、GenEval 0.291，以一半训练成本超过最强的 400K 基线，增益最大处集中在属性绑定、空间关系和多物体提示。

Conclusion: 组合性失败源于跨模态信息错位而非缺失，通过 ORCA 将低秩视觉对齐信号作为辅助损失注入扩散训练，可有效改善属性绑定、空间关系与多物体生成，且不增加推理开销，并在更少训练步数下超过强基线。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models fail predictably on compositional prompts: attributes bind to the wrong objects, spatial relations invert, and multi-object scenes lose count. Recent architectures already augment CLIP with a T5 encoder precisely because CLIP's contrastive embedding loses compositional structure, yet these failures persist. We argue the binding problem is therefore not one of missing information but of misaligned information: a text encoder preserves compositional structure, but in a representation space shaped by language modelling rather than vision, and the denoising objective does not directly reward aligning the two. We show this correspondence can be supplied as an explicit training signal, that the relevant cross-modal information is concentrated in a low-rank subspace of self-supervised visual features, and that supplying it can be folded into diffusion training as a single auxiliary loss. Our method, ORCA (Orthogonal Residual Compositional Alignment), aligns the latent of a diffusion transformer with a low-rank target derived from a frozen visual encoder, through a predictor whose orthogonal basis is parameterised by a learned residual between T5 and CLIP embeddings, which provides a prompt-dependent signal for selecting the visual readout subspace. We prove that the cross-modal information recoverable at a given rank is bounded by the spectral mass of the visual encoder's covariance in the top components. Across three diffusion-transformer backbones (DiT-B/2, DiT-L/2, U-ViT-L), ORCA improves FID and GenEval over both vanilla and REPA baselines at zero inference-time cost; on DiT-L/2 it reaches FID 16.65 and GenEval 0.291 at 200K steps, exceeding the strongest 400K baseline at half the training cost, with the largest gains concentrated on attribute binding, spatial relations, and multi-object prompts.

</details>


### [100] [Hard, Yet Reducible: Controlled Forward Transfer for Synthetic Degradation Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09849)
*Chunming He,Kailai Zhou,Jiaming Zuo,Hanqi Liu,Fengyang Xiao,Youwei Pang,Xiaofeng Liu,Weisi Lin,Xiaoqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出cRDG指标衡量合成退化在有限训练预算下的训练效用，并据此构建Curation of Reducible Bands (CoRB)方法，在语义分割与显著目标检测上提升代表性预测器性能且不损害干净性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为密集预测选择合成退化时，需要一个能反映其在有限训练预算下训练效用的估计。现有基于干净与退化孪生样本的误差差值的评分容易被干净性能本身的下降所混淆，而仅看退化图像上的提升又会把干净训练本身带来的进步误算进去。

Method: 提出受控可约退化差距(cRDG)：在按退化类型和严重程度划分的区域上，从同一检查点出发运行两个预算匹配的探针，二者仅在增强槽不同（一个放合成退化，一个放干净增强），以相对于干净控制探针在留出退化图像上的增益作为评分，并将干净性能受损作为单独的可行性约束。基于cRDG的Curation of Reducible Bands (CoRB)在不改动预测器的前提下选择合成数据。

Result: cRDG揭示出可修正的严重程度区间，在该区间内训练可获得高受控增益，且该区间随预测器、起始检查点和训练预算变化。在语义分割和显著目标检测上，CoRB在匹配的合成数据预算与训练计划下提升了代表性预测器，可扩展到已有数据生成流程，并保持干净性能。

Conclusion: cRDG提供了一种解耦干净训练增益与退化训练增益的效用度量，CoRB据此实现无需改动预测器的合成数据筛选，在多个密集预测任务上验证了有效性；代码与材料将公开。

Abstract: Selecting synthetic degradations for dense prediction requires an estimate of their training utility, the generalization gain they bring under a finite training budget. Clean and degraded twins share content and labels, suggesting a score based on how much short training reduces the excess error caused by degradation. However, this gap can also shrink when clean performance deteriorates. Measuring the improvement on degraded images alone avoids that confound, but it still credits progress that the same amount of clean training would have produced. We propose the \textbf{controlled Reducible Degradation Gap} (cRDG) for regions defined by degradation type and severity. From a common checkpoint, cRDG runs two budget-matched probes that differ only in one augmentation slot, which holds either a synthetic degradation or a clean augmentation. The score is the gain on held-out degraded images relative to the clean-control probe. Clean harm is a separate feasibility constraint. cRDG reveals a correctable severity band in which training on the degradation yields high controlled gain under the available budget, and the band moves with the predictor, the starting checkpoint, and the training budget. \textbf{Curation of Reducible Bands} (\method) uses cRDG to select synthetic data without changing the predictor. On semantic segmentation and salient object detection, \method{} improves representative predictors under matched synthetic-data budgets and training schedules, extends to existing data-generation pipelines, and preserves clean performance. Code and supporting materials will be publicly released.

</details>


### [101] [DeltaSplat: Iterative Gaussian Refinement for Pose-Free Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09853)
*Chanung Park,Seunghyeon Song,Joo Chan Lee,Eunbyung Park,Jong Hwan Ko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeltaSplat 是一个轻量级高斯细化模块，用于免位姿前馈 3DGS，通过迭代渲染并预测逐高斯更新来纠正相机估计误差，仅增加约 2.2% 参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免位姿前馈 3DGS 虽能从稀疏、无位姿图像单次重建场景，但相机估计误差会传播到预测的高斯中，并加剧单次预测的几何与光度不准确性，因此需要纠错机制。

Method: 在输入上下文视角迭代渲染当前高斯，并根据残差预测逐高斯更新；为缓解 2D 残差对 3D 校正欠定问题，以逐像素 Plücker 射线和渲染深度作为软几何先验进行条件化。采用双分支卷积混合器编码输入，逐属性头解码融合特征，输出位置、不透明度和颜色更新。模块仅增加约 2.2% 参数，推理时保持完全前馈。

Result: 在 DL3DV 上，免位姿设置下达 26.64 dB PSNR，比其最先进 backbone 提升 1.75 dB，并超过使用真值相机的基线；在 6-24 视角及所有相机配置下均有稳定增益。

Conclusion: DeltaSplat 通过轻量、前馈的高斯细化有效纠正相机估计误差传播，显著提升免位姿前馈 3DGS 的重建质量且开销极低。

Abstract: Pose-free feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructs a scene from sparse, unposed images in a single network pass, removing the need for camera calibration and per-scene optimization. However, camera estimation errors propagate into the predicted Gaussians and compound the geometric and photometric inaccuracies of single-pass prediction. To correct these errors, we introduce DeltaSplat, a lightweight Gaussian refinement module for pose-free feed-forward 3DGS. It iteratively renders the current Gaussians at the input context views and predicts per-Gaussian updates from the resulting residuals. A 2D residual alone, however, underdetermines the 3D correction. DeltaSplat therefore conditions each update on per-pixel Plücker rays and rendered depth as a soft geometric prior. A dual-branch convolutional mixer efficiently encodes these inputs, and per-attribute heads decode the fused features into position, opacity, and color updates. The module adds only ~2.2% parameters to the backbone and remains fully feed-forward at inference. On DL3DV, DeltaSplat reaches 26.64 dB PSNR in the pose-free setting, improving its state-of-the-art backbone by 1.75 dB and surpassing even baselines supplied with ground-truth cameras; consistent gains hold across 6-24 views and all camera regimes.

</details>


### [102] [DeepTopoClustering: Unsupervised Derivation of Surface Process Taxonomy from 4D Point Clouds for Topographic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09860)
*Jiapan Wang,Daan Hulskemper,Mathilde Letard,Roderik Lindenbergh,Katharina Anders*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出DeepTopoClustering（DTC）无监督框架，从4D变化对象中自动构建分层地表过程分类体系。方法将每个4D-OBC转化为GeoMorphogram分布序列，用卷积自编码器学习隐嵌入并结合分层深度聚类目标。在沙质海滩数据集上，DTC与专家标注一致性最高（F1=0.78，匹配精度=0.92），优于降维和常规扁平聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 永久激光扫描（PLS）获取的4D点云可实现动态地形环境的高频高精度地表变化监测，但现有方法难以将检测到的地表活动组织为有意义的过程类型，缺少从变化检测到过程知识推导的自动化手段。

Method: 提出DeepTopoClustering（DTC）无监督框架：将每个4D变化对象（4D-OBC）转换为GeoMorphogram——一种表示空间受限地表活动内地形变化时间演化的分布序列；使用卷积自编码器从GeoMorphogram学习隐嵌入；通过分层深度聚类目标联合优化，将地表活动组织为层次化分类体系。

Result: 在两个沙质海滩4D数据集及其组合上以专家标注评估：DTC配合GeoMorphogram在包含八种主要过程类型的分类层级上达到最高一致性（F1=0.78，匹配精度=0.92），优于降维方法和常规扁平聚类。所学分类体系能区分以侵蚀和沉积为主的活动，并依据变化幅度、持续时间、紧致度及时间演化区分更细的子类型。

Conclusion: DTC为从4D变化检测走向数据驱动、专家支持的地表过程分类体系提供了可扩展且可解释的路径，推动了地形监测中理解地表动态的自动化知识推导。

Abstract: 4D point clouds acquired by permanent laser scanning (PLS) enable accurate high-frequency monitoring of surface change in dynamic topographic environments. However, existing methods remain limited in organizing detected surface activities into meaningful process types. We propose DeepTopoClustering (DTC), an unsupervised framework for deriving a hierarchical process taxonomy from object-based surface activities, so-called 4D objects-by-change (4D-OBCs). We transform each 4D-OBC into a GeoMorphogram, a distributional sequence representing the temporal evolution of topographic change within a spatially bounded surface activity. A convolutional autoencoder learns latent embeddings from GeoMorphograms, which are jointly optimized using a hierarchical deep clustering objective to organize surface activities into a hierarchy. We evaluate the learned hierarchy using expert annotations on two 4D datasets of sandy beach sites and their combination. DTC with GeoMorphograms achieves the highest agreement with expert judgment at the taxonomy level comprising eight major process types ($F_1=0.78$, match accuracy $=0.92$), outperforming dimensionality reduction and conventional flat clustering. The learned taxonomy separates major erosion- and deposition-dominated activities and distinguishes finer subtypes based on change magnitude, duration, compactness, and temporal evolution. DTC thus provides a scalable and interpretable route from 4D change detection to a data-driven, expert-supported surface process taxonomy, advancing automated knowledge derivation for understanding surface dynamics in topographic monitoring.

</details>


### [103] [Global Average Precision for Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09863)
*Bill Psomas,Mohammad Mahdi,Michalis Thomas,Danda Pani Paudel,Giorgos Tolias,Giorgos Kordopatis-Zilos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 gSAP，一种可微的全局平均精度（gAP）代理损失，作为现有损失函数的即插即用替代，提升监督度量学习、跨模态对齐和自监督预训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准信息检索指标（如 mAP）及常见表示学习损失（如 InfoNCE、逐查询 AP 代理）都是逐查询评估的，忽略了不同查询之间相似度是否可比，而任何依赖单一决策阈值的系统都需要这种跨查询可比性。Global Average Precision (gAP) 通过将所有查询-候选对放入一个列表统一排序来解决此问题，但缺乏可微的代理。

Method: 提出 gSAP，一种 gAP 的可微代理。它仅需相似度矩阵和标记正样本对的二值矩阵，与现有损失输入相同，因此可作为即插即用替代，且对编码器、模态和监督来源无依赖。由于联合考虑批次中所有可能的成对比较，它在低温度下仍可训练，而逐查询代理在此情形下梯度枯竭。

Result: 将 gSAP 替换进现有流程后，在监督度量学习、跨模态对齐和自监督预训练中均有提升，在后两者中据作者所知是首个替代社区标准 InfoNCE 的排序损失。相同精度下，gSAP 检索到的正样本对最多为最强 AP 代理的四倍；在加入数据库中无正样本的查询时退化最小。此外模型学到更好的表示，具有更高的迁移、kNN 和零样本分类准确率。

Conclusion: gSAP 通过联合考虑批次内所有成对比较，使跨查询相似度更具一致性，从而在统一阈值下带来增益，并学习到更优的表示，验证了全局排序代理在表示学习和检索中的有效性。

Abstract: Standard information retrieval metrics, such as mean Average Precision (mAP), assess performance one query at a time, based on how the similarities between a query and its positives compare against those with its negatives. The same holds for common representation learning losses, such as InfoNCE and per-query AP surrogates. None of them considers whether similarities are comparable across queries, which any system with a single decision threshold relies on. Global Average Precision (gAP) does, by ranking all query-candidate pairs in one list and computing a single AP. We introduce gSAP, a differentiable surrogate of gAP. It needs only a similarity matrix and a binary matrix marking the positive pairs, the same input as existing losses, so it is a drop-in replacement for them and agnostic to the encoder, the modality, and the source of supervision. Since it considers all possible pairwise comparisons in the batch jointly, it also remains trainable at low temperatures, a regime where per-query surrogates run out of gradient. Swapping it into established recipes improves supervised metric learning, cross-modal alignment, and self-supervised pretraining, where, to our knowledge, it is the first ranking loss to replace the community standard InfoNCE in the latter two. Its similarities are more consistent across queries, which drives the gains under a universal threshold. gSAP retrieves up to four times as many positive pairs as the strongest AP surrogate at the same precision, and it degrades the least when queries with no positives in the database are added. Beyond thresholding, models trained with gSAP also learn better representations, with higher transfer, $k$NN and zero-shot classification accuracy.

</details>


### [104] [Bringing BNNs to Fast Event Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09873)
*Paul Longour,Julien Moreau,Franck Davoine*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将二值神经网络（BNN）应用于事件相机数据，提出二值事件表征PBEV，并证明跨模态预训练可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BNN与事件相机都注重高效部署，但二者结合尚未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 适配并评估现代深度BNN架构于事件数据；提出Polar-wise Binary Event Volume（PBEV）二值表征使事件数据可直接被BNN处理；并利用RGB数据跨模态预训练。

Result: 在N-Caltech101分类基准上，最优BNN达到90.58%准确率，运算量比全精度模型少7.5倍。

Conclusion: 跨模态预训练可提升BNN在神经形态数据集上的精度，PBEV是迈向全二值化事件视觉系统的一步。

Abstract: Binary Neural Networks (BNNs) enable efficient deep learning deployment on resource constrained devices with weights and activations compressed to one bit, substantially reducing model size and inference cost. Event cameras offer complementary advantages, including low latency, high dynamic range, and low power consumption, by capturing asynchronous streams of events rather than dense image frames. Despite their shared emphasis on efficiency, the combination of these technologies remains largely unexplored. This work aims at adapting and evaluating modern deep BNN architectures on event data. We also show that cross-modal pretraining from RGB data can improve the classification accuracy of BNNs on neuromorphic datasets. We introduce the Polar-wise Binary Event Volume (PBEV), a binary representation that enables event-camera data to be processed directly by BNNs and represents a step toward fully binarized event-based vision systems. Best evaluated BNN on N-Caltech101 classification benchmarks shows 90.58% accuracy with 7.5x less operations than their full-precision counterparts.

</details>


### [105] [SANet: Selective Attention Network for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09875)
*Yingmei Zhang,Wangtao Bao,Qin Xiao,Yong Yang,Weiguo Wan,Yitao Luo,Xueting Zou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性注意力网络SANet用于红外小目标检测，通过双路语义感知模块、空间通道注意力与选择性注意力融合模块，在三个公开数据集上取得领先的IoU等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测旨在复杂背景下准确定位暗弱小目标，服务于海上监视、军事搜救等应用。但红外目标尺寸小、对比度弱，导致检测精度与虚警率难以兼顾，亟需提升暗目标感知与背景抑制能力的方法。
