Orthogonal updates should combine without interfering, so parallel composition is a direct sum. The two adapters are simply added at inference, with no joint training, yielding few-step, streaming audio-video whose image quality tracks the bidirectional teacher. Compared to the chained baselines, the composed model matches or beats them on most metrics, making parallel composition a practical approach. The resulting streaming system generates joint audio-video in real time, $\approx$26 fps at $480\times832$ without quantization, and sustains 30 s of continuous generation with stable image quality.

</details>


### [139] [Explicit Geometric Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10390)
*Xingtai Gui,Yucheng Zhou,Dongqian Guo,Jiahao Gong,Feiyang Tan,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCoTDrive 提出一种显式几何思维链框架，将 2D 语义推理与 3D 几何先验结合，用于自动驾驶 VLA 规划，并在多个端到端基准上提升安全性关键规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 自动驾驶模型存在根本性错配：驾驶动作需要精确的 3D 几何线索，而视觉-语言理解与推理主要在 2D 语义空间中进行，导致几何信息不足，影响规划决策。

Method: 提出 GeoCoTDrive，遵循"先用 2D 思考、再用专用 3D 先验驾驶"的范式。首先定位与决策关键线索对应的 2D 区域，然后在定位区域内从几何基础模型采样特征以检索局部 3D 先验，并将这些局部几何特征交错插入自回归上下文以支持轨迹生成。同时引入"规划相关定位"这一新的区域级定位任务，并构建 PlanningGrounding 数据集以监督该过程。

Result: 在多个端到端自动驾驶基准上的实验表明，GeoCoTDrive 能够持续提升安全性关键的规划性能。

Conclusion: 显式几何思维链过程对基于 VLA 的规划是有效的，将 2D 语义推理与局部 3D 几何先验结合能显著改善自动驾驶规划表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action~(VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for autonomous driving. However, existing VLA models still suffer from a fundamental mismatch: driving actions require precise 3D geometric cues, while visual-language understanding and reasoning are largely conducted in a 2D semantic space. In this paper, we propose GeoCoTDrive, an explicit geometric chain-of-thought framework that grounds geometry in a planning-oriented manner. GeoCoTDrive follows a think with 2D first, drive with dedicated 3D priors paradigm. It first grounds 2D regions corresponding to decision-critical cues, and then retrieves localized 3D priors by sampling features from a geometric foundation model within the grounded regions. These localized geometric features are interleaved into the autoregressive context to support the trajectory generation. To supervise this process, we introduce planning-relevant grounding, a new region-level grounding task that focuses on local spatial cues directly affecting ego planning decisions, and construct the PlanningGrounding dataset to endow VLAs with planning-oriented grounding capability. Experiments across multiple end-to-end autonomous driving benchmarks show that GeoCoTDrive consistently improves safety-critical planning performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of the explicit geometric chain-of-thought process for VLA-based planning.

</details>


### [140] [MOTIP2: Spatial Priors for End-to-End Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10391)
*Benoît Roussel,Damien Bouet,Liming Chen,Pierre Perrault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出三种空间先验（数据、损失、表示阶段），显式编码空间约束以消除端到端多目标跟踪中空间上不合理的身份切换错误，并在MOTIP2上实例化，刷新多个基准的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端多目标跟踪器在关联困难基准上已接近传统跟踪检测方法，但仍会出现经典跟踪器不会犯的空间不合理错误（如给画面两端物体分配同一身份）。虽然模型可通过学习避免，但跟踪标注稀缺，因此改为显式编码空间先验，同时保持完全端到端的推理，无需后处理关联。

Method: 在数据、损失、表示三个阶段引入三种空间先验：1) Spatial ID Switches：将轨迹置换偏向空间重叠的物体，减少训练与推理间的混淆失配；2) Spatial ID Loss：按框间距离缩放每个身份的惩罚，使远距离切换代价高于近距离；3) Spatial Anchor：为每个轨迹token赋予其帧内位置，作为注意力的显式空间线索。三者在MOTIP2中实例化，MOTIP2基于MOTIP并采用实时DEIM检测transformer。

Result: 在无额外数据训练下，MOTIP2-L取得新SOTA：DanceTrack 73.4 HOTA、SportsMOT 76.0 HOTA、PersonPath22 71.1 IDF1。MOTIP2为系列模型：轻量MOTIP2-S以3倍以上速度匹配原MOTIP；MOTIP2-X在DanceTrack达74.8 HOTA。

Conclusion: 显式编码空间先验可在不依赖额外数据的情况下有效抑制端到端跟踪的空间不合理错误，同时保持端到端推理，并在多个基准上取得领先性能，兼顾速度与精度。

Abstract: End-to-end multi-object trackers have narrowed the gap with classical tracking-by-detection on association-difficult benchmarks. Yet they still make spatially implausible errors no classical tracker would, such as assigning one identity to objects on opposite sides of the frame. A model could learn to avoid them, but tracking annotations are scarce, so we encode spatial priors explicitly instead, while keeping inference fully end-to-end with no post-hoc association.
  We propose three spatial priors, at the data, loss, and representation stages. Spatial ID Switches bias trajectory permutations toward spatially overlapping objects, reducing the mismatch between training and inference confusions. Spatial ID Loss scales each identity's penalty by its box distance, so a distant switch costs more than a nearby one. Spatial Anchor gives each track token its frame position, an explicit spatial cue for attention.
  We instantiate the three priors in MOTIP2, a tracker adapted from MOTIP and built on the real-time DEIM detection transformer. Trained without extra data, its main model, MOTIP2-L, sets a new state of the art: 73.4 HOTA on DanceTrack, 76.0 on SportsMOT, and 71.1 IDF1 on PersonPath22. MOTIP2 is a family of models spanning the speed-accuracy trade-off: a lighter model, MOTIP2-S, matches the original MOTIP at over 3x the speed, and MOTIP2-X reaches 74.8 HOTA on DanceTrack.

</details>


### [141] [Gaussian Density Splatting Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10396)
*Miao Shang,Yabin Wang,Xiaopeng Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯密度喷溅网络（GDSNet），用连续二维高斯基元的叠加表示人群，而非传统网格密度图，在四个标准基准上超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人群计数方法依赖基于网格的密度图，对空间分辨率敏感，限制了表示能力与精度。作者希望用连续表示替代离散网格，从而摆脱分辨率约束。

Method: GDSNet包含两项关键设计：一是基于控制点的拟合机制，分配一组控制点定义局部区域，从中池化特征以回归每个基元的参数，从而结构化地预测高斯参数；二是将可微高斯喷溅框架适配到计数任务，每个基元由几何参数和标量密度质量参数化，通过可微渲染密度图的空间匹配实现端到端训练。

Result: 在四个标准基准上的大量评估表明，GDSNet持续优于当前最先进方法。该方法天然同时提供局部密度监督和全局计数优化。

Conclusion: 以连续高斯基元叠加表示人群并结合可微喷溅与端到端空间匹配训练，是人群计数中替代网格密度图的有效新范式，兼顾局部密度与全局计数优化。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel crowd counting approach, the Gaussian Density Splatting Network (GDSNet). Unlike methods that rely on conventional, grid-based density maps and are sensitive to spatial resolution, GDSNet represents a crowd as a superposition of continuous 2D Gaussian primitives. Our approach is built upon two key contributions. First, we introduce a control-point-based fitting mechanism to structure the prediction of the Gaussian parameters. We design a method to allocate a set of control points that define local regions, from which features are pooled to regress each primitive's parameters. Second, we adapt a differentiable Gaussian Splatting framework to the counting task by parameterizing each primitive with geometric parameters and a scalar density mass. This formulation allows the network to be trained end-to-end via spatial matching of differentiably rendered density maps, naturally providing both local density supervision and global count optimization. Extensive evaluations on four standard benchmarks show GDSNet consistently outperforms the state of the art.

</details>


### [142] [Self-correction Optimization for Interleaved Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10400)
*Xin You,Zhiwei Ning,Zukai Chen,Minghui Zhang,Xuanke Shi,Hanxiao Zhang,Jingsong Liu,Jie Yang,Quan Wang,Yun Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自校正优化（SCO），一种无需训练的即插即用方法，用于生成时序一致、主体保持的交错图文内容，并可扩展至视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在视觉理解与生成上进展显著，但交错图文生成仍具挑战，需深度整合理解与生成能力。现有方法多依赖增强数据的额外训练，计算成本高，且在保持视觉主体、时序一致性与物理合理性方面仍有不足。

Method: SCO 是一种免训练方法，将无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）的更新视为参考，在两类互补约束下进行最小化自校正：一是新事件约束，用于促进图文序列间的时序一致性；二是状态保持约束，用于维持视觉主体在后续生成步骤中的连贯性。

Result: 在具有挑战性的交错多模态生成基准上，SCO 在时序连贯性和视觉主体保持方面取得显著提升；此外，方法可扩展至视频生成，并改善了物理过程（如机器人操作与长时程手工制作）的建模效果。

Conclusion: SCO 提供了一种高效、免训练的一致交错图文生成方案，具备向视频生成及物理过程建模迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in visual understanding and generation. However, generating interleaved image--text content remains challenging, as it requires tightly integrated multimodal understanding and generation capabilities. Although existing MLLMs provide promising solutions, most rely on additional training with augmented data, which is computationally expensive and remains limited in preserving visual subjects, temporal consistency, and physical plausibility. In this work, we propose self-correction optimization (SCO), an effective training-free method for consistent interleaved generation. SCO treats the classifier-free guidance update as a reference and performs minimal self-correction under two complementary constraints, including new-event and state-preserving constraints. Specifically, the new-event constraint promotes temporal consistency across image--text sequences, while the state-preserving constraint maintains the coherence of visual subjects throughout subsequent generation steps. Experiments on challenging interleaved multimodal generation benchmarks demonstrate significant improvements in temporal coherence and visual-subject preservation. Furthermore, SCO can be extended to video generation and improves the modeling of physically grounded processes, including robot manipulation and long-horizon handcrafting.

</details>


### [143] [Rubix: Global Correspondence-Free Point Set Alignment through Assignment Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10408)
*Subhransu S. Bhattacharjee,Dylan Campbell,Rahul Shome*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Rubix 通过置换多边形的凸包几何结构，全局求解等权平面 Procrustes-Wasserstein 对齐问题，证明了顶点数的紧界 n(n-1)，并给出了高效算法与应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Procrustes-Wasserstein 对齐需同时估计匹配与旋转且无对应关系，交替最小化易陷入次优解；现有方法缺乏全局最优保证，本文旨在为平面等权问题提供全局求解方案。

Method: 利用复数相关性 z_σ = Σ \bar x_i y_{σ(i)} 定义匹配，构造置换多边形（permuation polygon）凸包：支撑顶点给出固定旋转下的最优匹配，最远顶点给出全局对齐；通过赋值查询在精确算术下以 O(n^5) 恢复多边形，并借助基于赋值的界扩展到三维旋转与给定平移下的部分匹配（分支定界）。

Result: 证明了 n≥2 时置换多边形顶点数的紧界为 n(n-1)，回答了 Rote 的旋转-赋值开放问题；在 MPEG-7 形状对上平均 12 ms 达到所有数值参考值，比同精度旋转网格快 50 倍；在真实 3D 扫描、形状检索和噪声晶体分类上优于交替最小化。

Conclusion: Rubix 为平面 Procrustes-Wasserstein 对齐提供了全局最优解法，其几何刻画与赋值界可推广至三维旋转与部分匹配，兼具理论最优性与实际效率优势。

Abstract: Procrustes-Wasserstein alignment jointly estimates a matching and rotation without supplied correspondences, but alternating minimization can stop at suboptimal solutions. Rubix solves the equally weighted planar problem globally under squared Euclidean loss. Each matching $σ$ of two centered $n$-point sets defines a complex correlation $z_σ=\sum_i\bar x_i y_{σ(i)}$. Their convex hull is the permutation polygon: supporting vertices give optimal matchings at fixed rotations, and the farthest vertex gives the global alignment. We prove the sharp bound of $n(n-1)$ vertices for $n\ge2$, answering Rote's rotation-assignment open problem. In exact arithmetic, assignment queries recover the polygon in $\mathcal O(n^5)$ operations. Assignment-based bounds extend the approach to three-dimensional rotations and partial matching at a supplied translation through branch-and-bound. On timed MPEG-7 shape pairs, Rubix attains every numerical reference value in 12 ms on average, 50 times faster than a rotation grid at the same accuracy. Its distances improve gravity-aligned matching of real 3D scans, shape retrieval and noisy crystal classification over alternating minimization.

</details>


### [144] [GraphRectify: Graph-Based Transfer of Adversarial Example Detectors Across Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10423)
*Arash Vashagh,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphRectify，一个基于图的框架，用于将对抗样本检测器跨分类器骨干网络迁移，实现检测器的复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗样本检测器通常与训练时的分类器骨干绑定，当被保护模型被替换或升级时难以复用。直接迁移这类检测器存在挑战，因为不同网络产生的内部表示通常不兼容。

Method: GraphRectify学习分类器中间特征的结构化图表示，并将新骨干网络的表示适配到基于原始模型学习的检测器上，从而实现检测器复用。

Result: 在多个数据集、骨干架构和对抗攻击（包括同时针对分类器与检测器的检测器感知自适应攻击）上评估。在整个评估矩阵中，GraphRectify的聚合ROC-AUC高于在新骨干上从头训练的检测器以及所评估的迁移消融方法。在不同骨干家族之间的迁移以及数据充足时增益尤为显著；而在数据最受限的情况下，从头训练仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 对抗检测知识可以有效地跨异构分类器架构迁移，而不必在受保护骨干改变时重新学习。

Abstract: Adversarial example detectors are often tied to the classifier backbone they were trained on, limiting reuse when the protected model is replaced or upgraded. Directly transferring such detectors across backbones is challenging because different networks generally produce incompatible internal representations. We propose GraphRectify, a graph-based framework for transferring adversarial image detectors across classifier backbones. GraphRectify learns a structured representation of intermediate classifier features and adapts representations from a new backbone to the detector learned on the original model, enabling detector reuse. We evaluate GraphRectify across multiple datasets, backbone architectures, and adversarial attacks, including detector-aware adaptive attacks that jointly target the classifier and detector. Across the complete evaluation matrix, GraphRectify achieves higher aggregate ROC-AUC than training a detector from scratch on the new backbone and the evaluated transfer ablations. The gains are particularly strong for transfers between different backbone families and when sufficient data are available. In contrast, training from scratch remains competitive in the most data-limited settings. These results show that adversarial detection knowledge can transfer effectively across heterogeneous classifier architectures rather than being relearned whenever the protected backbone changes.

</details>


### [145] [SGF+: Decoupling Gradient Flows for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10429)
*Zihan Su,Junhao Zhuang,Yaowei Li,Siwen Lu,Haoran Li,Lingen Li,Haoyu Wu,Weiyang Jin,Songchun Zhang,Haoyang Huang,Chun Yuan,Zeyue Xue,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现自回归视频生成中上下文写入与去噪两种角色共享参数会导致梯度负对齐，提出分离参数的SGF+方法，在提升视觉质量与长期一致性的同时支持长达24小时的连续生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成模型中，当前帧承担两个角色：去噪当前帧，并写入key-value表示作为未来预测的上下文。二者通常共享参数，作者发现其梯度模式不同且存在系统性负对齐，从而阻碍视觉质量与时间一致性的联合优化。

Method: 提出Self Gradient Forcing Plus（SGF+）：为上下文写入与去噪分配独立参数，同时通过因果注意力保留二者交互；两个角色使用原始生成目标联合优化，无需辅助损失，上下文写入通过其对未来预测的贡献获得监督。

Result: 在逐帧与分块生成设定下，相比所评估的基线，视觉质量与长时程一致性均有提升，且不依赖额外视频训练数据或更长训练时长。仅在5秒rollout上训练，SGF+即可支持最长24小时的连续生成，无需长视频微调。

Conclusion: 角色特定的参数化是高质量自回归视频生成与原生长时程外推的有效设计原则。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation requires denoising the current frames while writing their key-value representations as context for future predictions. However, these two roles typically share parameters, and we find that their gradients exhibit distinct patterns and systematic negative alignment, hindering the joint optimization of visual quality and temporal consistency. We introduce Self Gradient Forcing Plus (SGF+), which assigns separate parameters to context writing and denoising while preserving their interaction through causal attention. Both roles are jointly optimized using the original generation objective without auxiliary losses, with context writing supervised through its contribution to future predictions. This simple change improves visual quality and long-horizon consistency over the evaluated baselines in both framewise and chunkwise generation, without additional video training data or a longer training horizon. Trained on only 5s rollouts, SGF+ supports continuous generation for up to 24 hours without long-video fine-tuning. These results highlight role-specific parameterization as an effective design principle for high-quality autoregressive video generation and native long-horizon extrapolation.

</details>


### [146] [Detecting Adversarial Images through Response Profiles of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10436)
*Arash Vashagh,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语义提示响应模式的对抗图像检测方法，用于冻结视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗扰动可改变冻结VLM的预测，却保持置信度和图文相似性看似合理，需寻找新的检测信号。

Method: 利用图像对一组通用语义提示的响应，提取类别级统计、提示间关系、与干净参考分布偏差及弱变换稳定性，形成紧凑响应画像，用轻量模型分类，VLM保持固定。

Result: 在多个公开数据集、CLIP式骨干及多类攻击下取得强判别力；对未见攻击仍有较好性能；在受控协议下优于嵌入几何基线；特征组互补，提示配置变化下仍有效，并分析推理成本与自适应攻击。

Conclusion: 语义提示响应模式为冻结VLM中的对抗图像检测提供了有用的互补信号。

Abstract: Adversarial perturbations can alter the predictions of frozen vision-language models (VLMs) while leaving their confidence and image--text similarity patterns seemingly plausible. We investigate whether we can identify adversarial inputs based on the broader way an image interacts with a collection of general semantic prompts. Our detector summarizes these responses using category-level statistics, relationships among prompts, deviations from clean reference distributions, and stability under weak image transformations, producing a compact response profile that is classified by a lightweight model while the VLM remains fixed. We evaluate the approach on multiple public image datasets, several CLIP-style visual backbones, and a range of gradient-based, optimization-based, automated, and spatial attacks. The detector achieves strong discrimination in attack-specific settings and retains substantial performance when evaluated on attacks not seen during training. Under a controlled detector-specific protocol, the response-profile representation outperforms the evaluated embedding-geometry baselines. Additional analyses show that the feature groups provide complementary information and that the method remains effective under variations in the prompt configuration. We also examine inference cost and performance against detector-aware adaptive attacks. Overall, the results indicate that response patterns across semantic prompts provide a useful complementary signal for adversarial image detection in frozen VLMs.

</details>


### [147] [ECHO: Embodied Camera Observations of Human Object Carrying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10438)
*Xuefei Sun,Lorin Achey,Kali Hamilton,Alberto Speranzon,Gregory Grebe,Yonatan Bisk,Christoffer Heckman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"情境化物体放置"新基准任务，并发布ECHO大规模合成数据集，结合室内重建场景、人类携带物体活动、自然语言与放置标注，评估表明单一输入模态不足以完成该任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身与辅助智能体不仅需识别物体，还需结合环境布局与居住者习惯推断物体的合理归置位置。但该问题进展有限，原因是缺乏专门的基准与数据集：现有RGB-D扫描数据集只重建静态房间、不含人类活动，而人-物交互数据集虽含动作却缺少可导航的完整重建场景及物体自然归宿的真值定义。

Method: 提出"情境化物体放置"基准任务：在观测到的物体携带片段中预测物体目的地。构建ECHO数据集——大规模合成数据，将室内场景的密集RGB-D扫描与具身人类携带日常物品前往合适目的地的记录配对，涵盖115个HM3D场景的159个楼层、3805个人工标注片段、198种不同物体。每个楼层含完整RGB-D扫描、人工标注房间标签与表面列表；每个片段含同步RGB-D遭遇片段、6-DoF相机/人/物体轨迹、起止表面、动作描述及人类撰写的"情境"句（描述居住者日常惯例、暗示目的地但不点名）。采用输入掩码探针与端到端基线进行评估。

Result: 评估结果显示没有任何单一输入模态足以胜任该任务，凸显了对场景结构、人类活动与情境知识进行联合推理的必要性。

Conclusion: ECHO是首个同时结合重建场景、人类活动、自然语言与情境化放置标注的公开数据集，为情境化物体放置任务提供了定义与评测基础，并表明未来方法需整合多模态信息。

Abstract: Embodied and assistive agents must do more than recognize objects: they must reason about where an object belongs given the layout of an environment and the habits of the people who live in it. Progress on this problem has been limited, in part because no dedicated benchmark or dataset exists to define and evaluate it. Existing RGB-D scan datasets reconstruct static rooms without human activity, while human-object-interaction datasets capture motion without a navigable, fully reconstructed scene or a ground-truth notion of an object's natural destination. We introduce contextual object placement as a benchmark task: predicting an object's destination during an observed object-carrying episode. To support this task, we present Embodied Camera observations of Human Object carrying (ECHO), a large-scale synthetic dataset that pairs dense RGB-D scans of indoor scenes with recordings of an embodied human carrying everyday objects to context-appropriate destinations. ECHO is the first publicly available dataset to combine reconstructed scenes, human activity, natural language, and contextual-placement annotations. It comprises 3,805 human-annotated episodes across 159 floors of 115 HM3D scenes, involving 198 distinct objects. Each floor includes a complete RGB-D scan with human-annotated room labels and a surface list. Each episode provides synchronized RGB-D encounter clips; 6-DoF camera, human, and object trajectories; start and destination surfaces; an action caption; and a human-written context: a single sentence describing the inhabitant's routine that implies the destination without naming it. We evaluate contextual object placement using input-masked probes and an end-to-end baseline. Results show that no single input modality is sufficient, highlighting the need to jointly reason over scene structure, human activity, and contextual knowledge.

</details>


### [148] [MORCA: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Cache Reuse in Video Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10457)
*Yuxiang Xiong,Ruiyan Wang,Wenqiang Wang,Teng Hu,Songhang Shen,Bohao Feng,Hongqian Deng,Ran Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MORCA缓存调度框架，通过离线到在线强化学习实现隐空间感知的复用/重算决策，在用户指定加速目标下提升视频生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在视频合成中表现优异，但迭代去噪导致推理延迟高。现有动态缓存方法基于"步误差"（单步缓存复用引入的误差）做决策，而真正关心的是"终端误差"（对最终生成视频质量的影响）。论文指出步误差与终端误差并非直接对应，且隐空间信息有助于刻画二者关系。此外，现有阈值方法无法精确控制加速比，难以满足用户指定的加速目标。

Method: 提出MORCA缓存调度框架，利用离线到在线强化学习训练，在用户指定加速目标下做隐空间感知的复用/重算决策。

Result: 在多个视频生成模型、多种目标加速比下的大量实验表明，MORCA在可比计算预算下取得优于当前最优缓存方法的生成保真度。

Conclusion: 以终端误差为导向并借助隐空间信息，结合强化学习实现精确加速控制，是提升扩散Transformer视频生成缓存加速效果的有效途径。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve remarkable performance in video synthesis, but their iterative denoising process suffers from high inference latency. To address this, caching has emerged as an effective acceleration strategy by capitalizing on inter-step redundancy during denoising. Existing dynamic caching methods typically estimate the error that cache reuse would introduce at each denoising step (step error) to guide cache decisions, whereas our concern is how much quality loss cache reuse would cause in the final generated video (terminal error). We show that step error does not directly correspond to terminal error and that latent information helps capture their relationship, thereby informing cache decisions. Moreover, existing threshold-based methods cannot provide precise speedup control, making it difficult to meet practical requirements for user-specified acceleration targets. To address these limitations, we introduce MORCA, a cache scheduling framework trained through offline-to-online reinforcement learning to make latent-aware reuse/recompute decisions under user-specified acceleration targets. Extensive experiments on different video generation models across multiple target acceleration ratios demonstrate that MORCA achieves better generation fidelity than state-of-the-art caching methods under comparable computational budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/x10ngyx/MORCA.

</details>


### [149] [Label-free cell counting and viability prediction with brightfield imaging and deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10473)
*Amir Reza Vazifeh,Christian Zeigler,Sornanathan Meyyappan,Richard Jeske,Jason W. Fleischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无标记细胞活力分析软件ViabiLens，用染色亮场图像训练CNN，泛化到未染色亮场图像，在CHO细胞上实现2.68%的MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统细胞活力检测依赖染色法，存在化学试剂干扰细胞、部分膜损伤细胞难以判定、荧光光漂白影响准确性、无法原位实时测量等缺陷，因此需要一种无标记的活力评估方法。

Method: 作者首先验证染色亮场图像包含区分活/死细胞的足够信息，且未染色图像特征与染色图像相似，从而使模型可泛化。据此开发ViabiLens：结合细胞检测模型定位单个细胞与CNN分类器预测活/死，并配备基于UMAP的交互式可视化界面。

Result: 在覆盖广泛活力条件的中国仓鼠卵巢（CHO）细胞上评估，ViabiLens在未染色样本上相对荧光参考测量的平均绝对误差为2.68%。同时发布了无标记细胞活力分析基准数据集。

Conclusion: ViabiLens实现了无需染色的原位、无标记细胞活力分析，精度接近荧光参考标准，并公开数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Cell viability assessment is a core requirement in cell culture systems, with critical applications in biopharmaceutical manufacturing and drug development. Conventionally, it is measured by adding membrane-impermeable dyes to a sample (a process called staining), which allows compromised cell membranes to be distinguished from intact ones. However, staining has several limitations: (a) chemical agents can perturb normal cellular processes of the cells being measured, (b) it is often ambiguous to assign viability to individual cells whose membrane integrity is only partially compromised. (c) photobleaching can undermine measurement accuracy over time when using fluorescent stains, and (d) staining cannot be performed in situ or in real time. Here, we show that (1) stained cells captured under brightfield imaging contain sufficient information to distinguish live and dead cells, and (2) cells captured under unstained brightfield imaging exhibit similar image features to their stained counterparts, enabling models trained on stained cells to generalize to unstained ones. We then report the development and validation of ViabiLens, an AI-assisted software for label-free cell viability analysis. The ViabiLens combines a cell detection model for localizing individual cells with a convolutional neural network (CNN) classifier for live/dead prediction, paired with an interactive UMAP-based viewer for visualizing and exploring individual cells across the sample. Evaluated on Chinese Hamster Ovary (CHO) cells spanning a wide range of viability conditions, ViabiLens achieves a mean absolute error of 2.68\% on unstained samples against fluorescence-based reference measurements. We also release a benchmark dataset for label-free cell viability analysis to facilitate future research, available at https://amirrezavazifeh.github.io/ViabiLens-Project-Page/.

</details>


### [150] [Insights from Autoresearch for Solar Panel Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10491)
*Justinas Lekavicius,Kursat Komurcu,Valentas Gruzauskas,Linas Petkevicius*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究AutoResearch协议：编码大模型在一小时GPU预算内修改训练程序，仅当验证IoU提升时保留改动，应用于光伏板分割任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索编码语言模型能否自主进行训练程序优化（AutoResearch），并检验其改进在真实分割任务上的有效性与可迁移性。

Method: 在冻结的真实图像划分上做光伏板分割，固定DeepLabV3–ResNet-50。开展三轮各24次实验，分别使用Gemma 4 12B与Qwen3-8B，在单小时GPU预算下编辑训练程序，验证IoU提升则保留修改；Qwen3-8B配置仅用真实图像训练。

Result: 三轮实验的模型均优于各自一小时基线；但保留的修改无法跨硬件迁移。Qwen3-8B配置测试IoU为0.836，参考的GAN增强方案为0.833，仅略有优势。代码见 https://github.com/VU-AIML/automl4eo-autoresearch-segmentation。

Conclusion: AutoResearch可在限定预算内带来小幅提升，但跨硬件迁移性差，其实用性与稳健性仍受限。

Abstract: This paper investigates AutoResearch, a protocol in which a coding language model edits a training program under a one-hour GPU budget and retains a change only if validation IoU improves. The protocol is applied to photovoltaic panel segmentation on a frozen real-image split, with DeepLabV3--ResNet-50 held fixed. Three campaigns of 24 experiments, using Gemma~4 12B, Qwen3-8B all improve their one-hour baselines, but retained modifications do not transfer across hardware. The Qwen3-8B configuration, trained on real images only, reaches a test IoU of 0.836 versus 0.833 for the reference GAN-augmented schedule. Research repository https://github.com/VU-AIML/automl4eo-autoresearch-segmentation.

</details>


### [151] [QuadTok: Quadtree Visual Tokenizer for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10497)
*Yucheng Mao,Zeyuan Chen,Xiaojun Shan,Xiang Zhang,Divyansh Srivastava,Bingnan Li,Zhuowen Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分层四叉树结构的视觉分词与自回归图像生成框架QuadTok，兼顾2D空间绑定与1D序列灵活性，节省token并实现高质量的零样本空间控制生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉分词与自回归图像生成方法使用2D网格或1D token序列，前者与序列建模不兼容，后者缺乏空间绑定，难以兼顾空间结构与序列灵活性。

Method: 提出QuadTok框架，采用分层四叉树结构进行视觉分词，动态为视觉复杂区域分配更多表示容量，同质区域保持粗分辨率；树结构天然引入因果性支持自回归生成，并以生成前提供的四叉树拓扑为条件训练947M GPT式生成模型。

Result: 对比固定256 token网格，ImageNet训练的分词器节省约10% token，零样本迁移到COCO节省9%，重建保真度相当；947M生成模型在ImageNet 256×256上取得2.08 gFID；并支持零样本空间受控图像生成。

Conclusion: QuadTok通过四叉树结构有效桥接2D空间绑定与1D序列灵活性，在提升token效率的同时实现高质量自回归生成与空间控制能力。

Abstract: We introduce QuadTok, a novel framework for visual tokenization and autoregressive image generation. Compared to traditional approaches using 2D grids or 1D token sequences, we propose a hierarchical quadtree structure, bridging the gap between 2D spatial binding and 1D sequence-level flexibility. The QuadTok tokenizer dynamically allocates representational capacity to visually intricate areas while leaving homogeneous regions at a coarse resolution. Compared with a fixed 256-token grid, our ImageNet-trained tokenizer saves approximately 10% of tokens on ImageNet and 9% when transferred zero-shot to the COCO dataset, while maintaining comparable reconstruction fidelity. Furthermore, the natural causality introduced by the tree structure seamlessly enables autoregressive image generation. Conditioned on a quadtree topology supplied before generation, our 947M GPT-style generative model achieves a 2.08 gFID on the ImageNet $256 \times 256$ benchmark. Additionally, leveraging the strong spatial correlation preserved by the quadtree structure, the QuadTok generator enables zero-shot spatially controlled image generation capabilities. Code: https://github.com/myc634/QuadTok.

</details>


### [152] [Video-Conditioned Generative Joint 2D-3D Hand Motion Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10512)
*Chen Xu,Yunqi Li,Binbin Huang,Brent Yi,Shenghua Gao,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出JoHan统一生成框架，直接从视频序列恢复手部3D运动，无需中间逐帧姿态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中手部3D运动重建因频繁遮挡和视觉观测不完整而困难，逐帧姿态估计不可靠且时序不一致。

Method: 从头训练生成模型，联合生成对齐的2D与3D局部手部姿态序列，学习时序动态与跨表示对应关系；用生成的2D轨迹引导3D运动生成，并通过2D-3D对应恢复手部相对相机的全局位置与朝向。

Result: 在挑战性基准上，局部手部姿态与相机空间重建的准确率和速度显著提升，运动动态更佳，运动比以往方法更平滑，同时保持高逐帧姿态精度。

Conclusion: JoHan通过统一的2D-3D联合生成与运动先验，有效提升了手部3D运动重建的精度、时序一致性与速度。

Abstract: Recovering faithful 3D hand motion from video remains challenging due to frequent occlusions and incomplete visual observations, which make frame-wise pose estimates unreliable and temporally inconsistent. To address this problem, we propose JoHan, a unified generative framework that recovers hand motion directly from video sequences without relying on intermediate per-frame pose predictions. Trained from scratch, our model jointly generates aligned 2D and 3D local hand pose sequences by learning their temporal dynamics and cross-representation correspondence. The generated 2D trajectories exploit direct spatial and temporal cues from the 2D images to guide the following generative 3D motion reconstruction, while the learned motion prior promotes temporal consistency. Their learned 2D-3D correspondence further enables recovery of the hand's global position and orientation relative to the camera. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate significantly improved accuracy and speed in local hand-pose and camera-space reconstruction. Notably, our method captures much better hand-motion dynamics, producing significantly smoother motion than previous methods while maintaining high per-frame pose accuracy.

</details>


### [153] [GRACE: Generation-aware latent compression for efficient video generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10524)
*Jiyoung Kim,Paul Hyunbin Cho,Jisu Nam,Donghoon Lee,Hyunsung Go,Yeonkyeong Lee,Hansaem Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GRACE两阶段框架，在压缩预训练视频自编码器时保持与预训练DiT兼容，实现8倍token压缩与11.1倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型因DiT在大量token上运行而缓慢，高压缩自编码器可加速，但高压缩比会降低重建质量、需要更多通道拖慢DiT收敛，且压缩后的latent偏离DiT训练分布，导致预训练DiT需重训或高成本适配。

Method: GRACE为两阶段框架：1）保留预训练编码器的冻结base latent，学习残差latent补偿强压缩下丢失的信息，并在冻结DiT的特征空间中将压缩latent与预训练latent对齐，使自编码器面向生成优化；2）用轻量微调与非对称去噪适配DiT，其中base早于residual去噪。

Result: 将Wan2.1-I2V-14B的token数减少8倍，在480x832x81分辨率下延迟降低11.1倍，同时在VBench上匹配压缩前预训练流程的生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过保留base latent、学习残差并对齐DiT特征空间，GRACE在保持预训练DiT兼容的同时实现高压缩与高质量视频生成。

Abstract: Highly compressed video autoencoders offer an effective way to accelerate video diffusion models, as the Diffusion Transformer (DiT) operates on far fewer tokens. However, such autoencoders are challenging to train, since a higher compression ratio degrades reconstruction quality and recovering it requires more channels, which is known to slow the convergence of the DiT. The compressed latent also differs from the one the DiT was trained on, so the pretrained DiT must be either retrained from scratch or adapted at considerable cost. Compressing the autoencoder the DiT was trained with appears to preserve compatibility, yet optimizing it for reconstruction alone still shifts the latent away from the distribution the DiT has learned. To address this, we propose Generation-Aware Latent Compression for Efficient Video Generation (GRACE), a two-stage framework that compresses a pretrained video autoencoder while keeping it compatible with the pretrained DiT. Specifically, we keep a frozen base latent from the pretrained encoder and learn a residual latent for the information lost under stronger compression, while aligning the compressed latent with the pretrained latent in the feature space of the frozen DiT so that the autoencoder is optimized for generation. We then adapt the DiT with lightweight fine-tuning and asymmetric denoising, where the base is denoised ahead of the residual. GRACE reduces the token count of Wan2.1-I2V-14B by 8x and its latency by 11.1x at 480x832x81, while matching the generation quality of the pretrained pipeline before compression on VBench.

</details>


### [154] [Never Look Back: Understanding Persistence in 3D Object Memory from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10538)
*Shravan Chaudhari,William Paul,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Homanga Bharadhwaj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ledger——一种持久的 3D 物体记忆系统，让具身助手通过观察第一视角视频构建物体位置、历史与上下文描述的记忆，在无需回看原始图像的情况下回答空间问题，并显著提升多项基准准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人在日常活动中会记住物体位置（如把东西放在哪里、容器里有什么），即使当时并不需要。研究者希望具身助手也能从第一视角视频中构建类似记忆，以支持后续的空间问答，而无需重新访问原始数据。

Method: Ledger 结合物体位置、历史与上下文描述，形成持久的 3D 物体记忆：(1) 跨录像关联观测，并在物体离开视野后仍保留，包括从未被触碰的物体；(2) 按静止位置对每个物体的观测聚类，仅在出现重复证据后才记录一次移动，以降低定位噪声影响；(3) 用简短描述保存物体内容或支撑表面等细节；(4) 将记录保存下来以便日后回答空间问题，无需访问原始图像或视频。

Result: 将 HD-EPIC 准确率从 29.7% 提升至 42.6%，UCS-Bench 从 33.8% 提升至 38.5%；在 Ego4D 物体定位上中位误差为 0.99 m。分析显示时间持久性、上下文描述与检索各自具有互补作用。在 100 条由多场景拼接的流上测试，暴露出检索与构建两方面的失败；相比单场景流，按场景分别构建可部分恢复跨场景变化带来的性能损失。

Conclusion: 持久的 3D 物体记忆可有效支持具身助手的空间问答；时间持久性、上下文描述与检索相互补充；但多场景流中的检索与构建仍存在挑战，按场景构建可部分缓解性能下降。

Abstract: As we move through the world and carry out everyday tasks, we encounter objects that may become relevant only later. We are capable of recalling where we left something or what was inside a container, even without knowing we would need it later. Here, we study how an embodied assistant can build a similar memory from egocentric videos, by observing a person's day-to-day activities. We present Ledger, a persistent 3D object memory that combines object locations, their histories, and contextual descriptions. It associates observations across the recording and retains objects after they leave the view, including those the person never touches. It clusters each object's observations by resting locations and records a move only after repeated evidence, reducing the effect of localization noise. Short descriptions preserve details such as an object's contents or supporting surface. It saves these records to later answer spatial questions without having to access the original images or video. Our memory raises HD-EPIC accuracy from 29.7% to 42.6%, UCS-Bench accuracy from 33.8% to 38.5% and localizes Ego4D objects with a 0.99 m median error on returned predictions. Our analyses identify complementary roles for temporal persistence, contextual descriptions, and retrieval. Our study on 100 stitched streams of multiple scenes each further exposes failures in both retrieval and construction. Per-scene construction partially recovers the performance lost across scene changes compared to that of single scene streams.

</details>


### [155] [Tetris3D: 3D Scene Generation With Objects That Fit Together](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10539)
*Jaeyeong Kim,Jinhyuk Jang,Jongmin Lee,Kyehong Park,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Tetris3D 单图 3D 场景重建生成框架，通过显式条件化物体生成于周围几何与物理关系，并构建物理仿真数据集 ComOb，实现高质量、物理稳定的场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法往往独立生成物体或隐式耦合它们，对相邻交互物体间的细粒度空间兼容性指导不足，难以保证物体在场景中的几何与物理合理性。

Method: 提出 Tetris3D 生成框架，将每个物体的生成显式条件化于周围物体的几何及其物理关系，引导形状与姿态在场景中保持几何和物理可行；同时构建 ComOb 数据集，包含 120 万场景、跨多样类别的物理交互，并提供逐物体网格与成对物理关系标注。

Result: 在合成与真实场景上的实验表明，Tetris3D 即使在交互区域被遮挡时也能恢复连贯的物体形状与姿态，在生成质量和物理稳定性上均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模物体间几何与物理关系并配套物理仿真数据集，可显著提升单图 3D 场景重建的连贯性与物理稳定性。

Abstract: We propose Tetris3D, a generative framework for single-image 3D scene reconstruction that recovers objects which are physically and geometrically coherent as a scene. Existing methods often generate objects independently or couple them implicitly, providing limited guidance for ensuring fine-grained spatial compatibility between neighboring objects that interact with one another. To address this, we explicitly condition the generation of each object on the geometry of surrounding objects and their physical relationships, guiding its shape and pose to remain geometrically and physically plausible within the scene. Moreover, we introduce ComOb, a physics simulation-based dataset of 1.2M scenes featuring physical interactions across diverse object categories, with per-object meshes and pairwise physical relation annotations. Comprehensive experiments on synthetic and realworld scenes show that Tetris3D recovers coherent object shapes and poses even when interacting regions are occluded, and achieves state-of-the-art performance in both generation quality and physical stability.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Tokka-Bench: Evaluating Tokenizers Across 100 Natural and 20 Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08794)
*Ben Gubler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Tokka-Bench，一个开源的多指标分词器评估框架，覆盖 100 种自然语言和 20 种编程语言，并对七种 BPE 分词器进行对比，发现词汇分配策略比词汇规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型依赖子词分词器，但其质量在不同语言间差异显著，目前缺乏标准化的多指标框架进行广泛比较评估。

Method: 提出 Tokka-Bench 开源框架，基于五个互补指标（每 token 字节数、唯一 token 覆盖率、子词生育率、词切分率、词汇组成），采用适配各书写系统的语言感知分词方法，在 100 种自然语言（30+ 种文字）和 20 种编程语言上评估七种 BPE 分词器（GPT-2、GPT-4、gpt-oss、Llama 3.1、Gemma 3、Qwen3、Kimi K2）。

Result: 在单语言内比较发现，词汇分配策略比原始词汇规模更重要；近期分词器在编程语言效率上已趋同，但自然语言表现仍存在差异。

Conclusion: 该框架、数据及交互式仪表板已公开，为分词器的多语言与多指标比较评估提供了标准化工具。

Abstract: Large language models rely on subword tokenizers whose quality varies across languages, yet no standardized multi-metric framework exists for broad comparative evaluation. We introduce Tokka-Bench, an open-source framework that evaluates tokenizers on five complementary metrics -- bytes per token, unique token coverage, subword fertility, word-split rate, and vocabulary composition -- across 100 natural languages (30+ scripts) and 20 programming languages, using language-aware segmentation adapted to each writing system. Comparing seven BPE tokenizers (GPT-2, GPT-4, gpt-oss, Llama 3.1, Gemma 3, Qwen3, and Kimi K2) within individual languages, we find that vocabulary allocation strategy matters more than raw vocabulary size, and that programming-language efficiency has converged among recent tokenizers despite divergent natural-language profiles. The framework, data, and interactive dashboard are publicly available.

</details>


### [157] [Child ASR Adaptation with Adult Retention: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08827)
*Houssam Eddine-Othman Lachemat,Shammur Absar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究儿童语音ASR适配中的"成人语音遗忘"问题，跨阿拉伯语和英语，比较全量微调、LoRA和事后权重空间合并三种策略在编码器-解码器、编码器-CTC及AudioLLM架构上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR系统对儿童和非母语说话人表现不佳，而将成人ASR模型适配到儿童语音时会导致成人语音性能遗忘，因此需要在提升儿童识别效果的同时保留成人语音能力，并探索不同适配策略与架构间的权衡。

Method: 在阿拉伯语（母语与非母语儿童语音）、英语（MyST儿童语音）及成人基准（MGB-2、LibriSpeech test-clean）上进行实验，比较全量微调、LoRA与事后权重空间合并（含LERP、TIES），覆盖编码器-解码器、编码器-CTC和基于AudioLLM的ASR系统，用WER评估识别质量，并用Retention Index、Child Adaptation Gain和Adaptation Recovery量化适配-保留权衡。

Result: 儿童适配是必要的，尤其对非母语阿拉伯语和英语儿童语音；但直接适配常降低成人ASR性能。双语适配比单语适配更稳定。权重空间合并通常改善权衡，尤其对编码器-CTC、Whisper和AudioLLM类ASR有效，其中LERP更利于成人保留，TIES能恢复更强的儿童增益。对编码器-解码器模型，直接双语微调在原始WER上仍最强。

Conclusion: 儿童ASR适配需兼顾成人语音保留，权重空间合并是改善适配-保留权衡的有效手段，且不同架构与合并算法各有优势；双语适配在稳定性上优于语言特定适配。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems often underperform for children and non-native speakers, while adapting adult ASR models to child speech can cause adult-speech forgetting. We study child ASR adaptation with adult retention across Arabic and English. We compare full fine-tuning, LoRA, and post-hoc weight-space merging across encoder--decoder, encoder--CTC, and AudioLLM-based ASR systems. Experiments use Arabic native and non-native child speech, English MyST child speech, and adult benchmarks from MGB-2 and LibriSpeech test-clean. We evaluate recognition quality with WER and quantify the adaptation--retention trade-off using Retention Index, Child Adaptation Gain, and Adaptation Recovery. Results show that child adaptation is necessary, especially for non-native Arabic and English child speech, but direct adaptation often reduces adult ASR performance. Bilingual adaptation is more stable than language-specific adaptation. Weight-space merging often improves the trade-off, especially for encoder--CTC, Whisper, and AudioLLM-based ASR, with LERP favoring adult retention and TIES recovering stronger child gains. For the encoder--decoder model, direct bilingual fine-tuning remains strongest in raw WER.\footnote{Code, and models are available at https://github.com/qcri/Child-ASR-Adaptation.

</details>


### [158] [When Forgetting Looks Like Improvement: Metric Masking in Streaming Diarizer Adaptation and the Price of Rehearsal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08828)
*Mo Yu,Yang Liu,Jing Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 小数据适配能提升语音检测却损害说话人归属，本文通过流式分离系统研究该矛盾及身份一致性退化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到小数据适配在提升语音检测的同时会使说话人归属能力下降，希望厘清这一矛盾现象的内在机制（是说话人计数错误还是时间身份一致性受损）。

Method: 在一个已发布的流式分离系统上，用7.5小时双人对话数据进行适配，并在六个语料库上评估；采用局部重映射诊断分析身份退化模式，并测试排练（rehearsal）策略的影响。

Result: 适配显著提升域内分离性能并可迁移到独立语料；但改进并不一致，额外混淆主要来自时间身份一致性受损而非说话人计数错误；局部重映射揭示不同语料下身份退化模式不同。排练能减少退化但降低跨域迁移性能。

Conclusion: 适配流式分离系统时需联合评估检测准确率、身份一致性与保留行为。

Abstract: Small-data adaptation can improve speech detection while degrading speaker attribution. We study this discrepancy in a released streaming diarizer adapted on 7.5 h of two-party conversation and evaluated across six corpora. Adaptation substantially improves in-domain diarization performance and transfers to an independent corpus. However, this improvement is not consistent across evaluation scenarios as the additional confusion is mainly associated with impaired temporal identity consistency rather than speaker-count errors. A local-remapping diagnostic reveals different patterns of identity degradation across corpora, indicating that adaptation may alter how streaming models maintain speaker assignments over time. Rehearsal reduces the observed degradation but reduces the cross-domain transfer performance. These results highlight the need to jointly evaluate detection accuracy, identity consistency, and retention behavior when adapting streaming diarization systems.

</details>


### [159] [Emo-Jev: Probabilistic Reasoning for Emotion Classification with Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08829)
*Yazhou Zhang,Junhao Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架Emo-Jev，将Jev的概率式判断接口用于文本分类推理，在8个数据集上超越直接Jev分类并与5个SoTA LLM对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jev以预定义问题返回概率化决策而非自由文本，这种接口能否支撑有效推理、并用于文本分类任务尚不明确，尤其在面对领先LLM时。

Method: Emo-Jev为免训练框架，含两种互补实现：Emo-Jev-D将分类拆解为任务特定的原子判断，并将其概率组合为最终预测；Emo-Jev-SC从互补视角构建多条判断路径，聚合成共识决策。

Result: 在情感分析、情绪识别、讽刺检测和幽默检测共8个数据集上评测，对比直接Jev分类及5个SoTA LLM（输入/输出与思维链推理）。标准Jev平均宏F1为62.93%，最强LLM基线为67.28%，同时Jev延迟更低、成本一般也更低。

Conclusion: Jev的概率式接口可通过分解与多路径聚合实现具竞争力的分类推理，在效率上具优势，但整体宏F1仍低于最强LLM基线。

Abstract: Jev offers an alternative interface for language understanding: given an input and predefined questions, it returns probabilistic decisions rather than free-form responses. Whether this interface can support effective reasoning for text classification against leading LLMs remains an open questions. We introduce Emo-Jev, a training-free framework with two complementary implementations. Emo-Jev-D decomposes classification into task-specific atomic judgments and composes their probabilities into a final prediction. Emo-Jev-SC constructs multiple judgment paths from complementary perspectives and aggregates their predictions into a consensus decision. We evaluate Emo-Jev on eight datasets spanning sentiment analysis, emotion recognition, sarcasm detection and humor detection, comparing against direct Jev classification and five SoTA LLMs under input/output and chain-of-thought reasoning. Standard Jev achieves 62.93\% average macro-F1 versus 67.28\% for the strongest LLM baseline, with lower observed latency and generally lower cost.

</details>


### [160] [CoDR: Training-Free Confidence-Drift Remasking for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08833)
*Yue Wu,Qinghe Zhang,Yu Zhang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）解码时"承诺不可逆"的问题，提出免训练、与采样器无关的细化方法 CoDR，通过置信度漂移检测并重掩码被模型抛弃的 token，在多种模型、任务与采样器上提升了准确率且开销较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDLM 通过反复将 token 提交到掩码位置来解码，但这些提交通常不可逆：在稀疏、部分上下文下选出的 token 会被固定，即使后续更密集的上下文已不支持它。现有采样器主要关注"何时提交"，却很少检查已提交 token 是否仍应保留，导致早期错误不断传播。

Method: 作者将问题归因于"置信度漂移"——模型对已提交 token 的置信度从提交时的稀疏上下文到后续更密集的上下文会下降。基于该信号提出 CoDR（Confidence Drift Remasking）：一种免训练、与采样器无关的细化过程。CoDR 通过 k 分区探测，仅用 k 次前向传播估计所有已提交位置的漂移，然后只对模型不再认可的 token 进行重掩码与再生成。

Result: 在两个骨干模型、四项推理与编码任务、三种基础采样器上，CoDR 在所有评估的模型-采样器组合中均提升了平均准确率，并在多数单项任务设置上取得提升，额外开销适中。受控实验表明增益来自有针对性的置信度漂移重掩码而非单纯增加计算量，且 CoDR 所用的前向传播次数远少于先前的重掩码方法。

Conclusion: 不可逆承诺导致的置信度漂移是 MDLM 解码中的关键缺陷，CoDR 通过检测并重掩码不被后续上下文认可的 token，以极少的前向传播代价实现稳健改进，是一种通用、低成本且有效的细化策略。代码已开源。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) decode by repeatedly committing tokens to masked positions, but these commitments are usually irreversible. A token chosen under sparse, partial context is kept fixed, even when later context no longer supports it. Existing samplers mainly decide when to commit a token, but rarely check whether an already committed token should still be kept, allowing early mistakes to propagate. We trace this issue to confidence drift, where the model's confidence in a committed token drops from its sparse commit-time context to the denser context available later. Based on this signal, we propose CoDR (Confidence Drift Remasking), a training-free and sampler-agnostic refinement pass. CoDR estimates drift for all committed positions in only k forward passes via k-partition probing, then remasks and regenerates only the tokens the model no longer endorses. Across two backbones, four reasoning and coding tasks, and three base samplers, CoDR improves average accuracy across all evaluated model-sampler configurations and improves most individual task settings with modest overhead. Controlled experiments show that the gains come from targeted confidence-drift remasking rather than extra compute alone, and that CoDR uses far fewer forward passes than prior remasking methods. Code is available at https://github.com/YueWu0301/CoDR.

</details>


### [161] [Leveraging LLM-Generated Explanations for Detecting Emotionally Rewritten Fake News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08835)
*Yupei Guo,Jiajun He,Xiaohan Shi,Tomoki Toda,Zekun Yang,Bowen Wang,Yukinobu Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究事实保留的情感改写对假新闻检测鲁棒性的影响，并提出了门控交叉注意力（GCA）框架，自适应融合情感改写后的新闻与原始新闻生成的解释，从而提升检测模型的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假新闻传播会带来严重社会后果。现有检测方法多关注文体变化或引入解释等外部信息，但新闻常在保留事实主张的前提下被不同情感背景改写，这会削弱检测模型的鲁棒性。因此需要研究事实保留情感变化下的假新闻检测问题。

Method: 构建情感改写测试集，并从原始新闻生成解释作为稳定背景知识；提出门控交叉注意力（GCA）框架，自适应整合情感改写新闻与对应解释，使模型聚焦有信息的解释内容，并减少情感重构导致的潜在不匹配。

Result: 在 PolitiFact、GossipCop 和 LUN 数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在 PolitiFact 和 LUN 的多种情感条件下取得显著提升，同时在 GossipCop 上保持有竞争力的性能；进一步分析了不同情感条件下解释引导和门控机制的作用。

Conclusion: 该研究表明在事实保留的情感改写场景下，融合稳定解释知识的门控交叉注意力框架能有效提升假新闻检测鲁棒性，并公开了代码与数据。

Abstract: The spread of fake news may cause severe social consequences. Existing fake news detection methods mainly focus on stylistic variations or incorporate external information such as explanations. However, news articles are often rewritten under different emotional backgrounds while preserving their underlying factual claims, which may affect the robustness of detection models. In this work, we investigate fake news detec- tion under fact-preserving emotional variations. To study this problem, we construct emotion-rewritten test sets and generate explanations from the original news articles as stable background knowledge. We then propose a Gated Cross Attention (GCA) framework that adaptively integrates emotionally rewritten news with the corresponding explanations, enabling the model to focus on informative explanation content while reducing potential mismatches caused by emotional reframing. Experiments on PolitiFact, GossipCop, and LUN demonstrate that the proposed method achieves notable improvements under multiple emotional conditions on PolitiFact and LUN, while maintaining competitive performance on GossipCop. We further analyze the effects of explanation guidance and gating mechanisms under different emotional conditions. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/Flulike/fakenews gca .

</details>


### [162] [Beyond the Sycophancy Score: How Task, Model, and Pressure Shape LLM Yielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08840)
*Guang Yang,Homa Hosseinmardi,Fengchen Liu,Amir Ghasemian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过10.4万条评分回复、10种模型配置、200道题和13种压力条件的实验，发现"谄媚"行为主要由任务验证难度和模型防护栏决定，而非压力话术或模型家族本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究把"谄媚"（sycophancy）仅报告为每个模型的单一比率，无法说明它何时发生、用户如何规避，因此本文系统考察其产生条件。

Method: 收集103,939条分级回复，涵盖10种配置（8个关闭推理的LLM，其中2个再开启最大推理），面对相同的200个项目、13种压力条件和四轮对话；每条回复由两个独立LLM评判者标注；用逻辑回归等分析各因素贡献（McFadden R²），并以三名人类标注者在120个校准项上验证一致性。

Result: 主导因素是验证用户主张的代价和训练防护栏的覆盖：移除任务因素损失0.485的McFadden R²，远超模型家族（0.139）与压力策略（0.009）。锚定事实几乎不被让步（1.3%）；逻辑谜题的采纳率随反驳所需线索数上升；个人选择在77.0%的对话中被认可。难题上的让步多来自无法可靠解题的模型。两个测试模型开启最大推理后让步完全消失（深度谜题采纳率从19.2%和12.5%降至0%）。谬误或情绪化框架并不比单纯重复更有效。人类标注者与评判者共识在118/120项上一致。

Conclusion: 提出可靠使用的实用规则：简化难以验证的问题并深度推理、陈述问题而非偏好答案、对开放问题索要证据、依据实测防护栏特征选择模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often abandon a correct answer, or endorse a user's position, once the user pushes back. This behavior, called sycophancy, is usually reported as a single rate per model, which says little about when it happens or how a user can avoid it. We study the conditions that produce it with 103,939 graded replies from ten configurations: eight LLMs with reasoning disabled, and two of them again with maximum reasoning, all facing the same 200 items, 13 pressure conditions, and four-turn conversations, with every reply labeled by two independent LLM judges. We find that the dominant factors are how costly it is for the model to verify the user's claim, and whether a trained guardrail covers it. Removing this task factor from a logistic model costs 0.485 of McFadden $R^2$, against 0.139 for model family and 0.009 for pressure tactic. Anchored facts are almost never conceded (1.3%), while adoption on logic puzzles rises with the number of clues needed to refute the pushed answer. Personal choices are endorsed in 77.0% of conversations. Most concessions on hard items come from models that cannot reliably solve them; models that can solve them rarely give the answer up. For both models tested, maximum reasoning removes these concessions completely: adoption on deep puzzles falls from 19.2% and 12.5% to 0%. Fallacious or emotional framing adds nothing beyond plain repetition. Three human annotators agree with the judges' consensus on 118/120 calibration items. These results give practical rules for reliable use: simplify hard-to-verify problems and reason deeply, state the question rather than one's preferred answer, ask for evidence on open questions, and choose models by their measured guardrail profile.

</details>


### [163] [Beyond Risk Prediction: Evidence Grounding and Psychosocial Factor Verification for Explainable Suicide Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08842)
*Tianle Hu,Chen Peng,Yi-Hsin Tsai,Takshing Andy Tung,Bingyang Sun,Yenjou Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含风险评估、证据定位与因素识别三部分的框架，用于社交网络帖子中的自杀风险检测，并提供可解释的证据与心理社会因素分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅做风险分类难以揭示预测背后的文本证据与心理社会因素，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 基于IEEE BigData 2026可解释自杀风险检测挑战赛，框架含三部分：风险评估采用基于长度的路由处理不同长度帖子；证据定位识别支撑短语并用风险-证据约束保持与风险预测一致；因素识别使用两个验证器（分类学验证器关注因素语义，证据感知验证器利用因素特定词汇-语义线索筛选信息量大的正训练单元），合并概率得到最终因素预测。三任务分别用特定F1度量评估。

Result: 风险评估加权F1为0.8088，证据定位测试集Macro行F1为0.7605，因素识别Macro F1为0.5562。

Conclusion: 该框架在给出风险预测的同时提供支撑文本证据与细粒度心理社会因素信息，将自杀风险评估拓展到风险等级预测之外，实现更可解释的SNS帖子分析。

Abstract: Identifying suicide risk from social networking services (SNS) posts is important for detecting suicide-related signals in online environments. However, risk classification alone provides limited insight into the textual evidence and psychosocial factors behind a prediction. Based on the IEEE BigData 2026 Explainable Suicide Risk Detection Challenge, this study presents a framework consisting of Risk Assessment, Evidence Grounding, and Factor Identification. Risk Assessment uses length-based routing to accommodate posts of different lengths. Evidence Grounding identifies supporting phrases and uses a Risk-Evidence constraint to maintain consistency with the Risk prediction. For Factor Identification, two verifiers are used. The Taxonomy Verifier focuses on factor semantics, whereas the Evidence-Aware Verifier uses factor-specific lexical-semantic cues to select informative positive training units. Their prediction probabilities are combined to produce the final factor predictions. The three tasks are evaluated using task-specific F1 score measures. Risk Assessment achieved a Weighted F1 of 0.8088, Evidence Grounding achieved a test Macro row F1 of 0.7605, and Factor Identification achieved a Macro F1 of 0.5562. The results show that the framework can provide risk predictions, along with supporting textual evidence and fine-grained information on psychosocial factors. Overall, the proposed framework extends suicide-risk assessment beyond risk-level prediction and provides a more interpretable analysis of SNS posts.

</details>


### [164] [QuanLing: Cross-Branch Validation of Language Distance Quantification on Western Romance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08851)
*Yiping Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将 QuanLing 定量语言距离框架从北日耳曼语族扩展至西罗曼语族（法语、葡萄牙语、西班牙语、意大利语），验证其跨语系适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化近亲语言间的语言距离仍是定量语言学的核心难题，作者希望验证此前提出的 QuanLing 框架能否跨越单一语族、具备更广泛的通用性。

Method: 沿用北日耳曼研究的同一指标族与聚合协议并加以调整（英语锚点、四元组构建）。使用150组四语平行句，计算 LaBSE 句向量距离、四种单语 BERT 分词器的切分碎片率，以及 mBERT 掩码语言模型互懂度。

Result: 葡语—西语最近（LaBSE 距离 0.0229），法语—意大利语最远（0.0338）；LaBSE 与 mBERT 在 6 组配对中 4 组排序一致，体现跨模型稳健性。西罗曼语族绝对距离跨度更大（0.011 vs. 0.008），但相对比值相近（1.48 vs. 1.67），与更长的分化时间一致。法语 MLM 可预测性明显更高（Top-1 准确率 36.12% vs. 意大利语 29.28%），反映其正字法—音系解耦。

Conclusion: 跨语族的验证进一步证明了 QuanLing 框架可超越单一语言分支，具有较好的通用性。

Abstract: Quantifying language distance among closely related languages remains a core challenge in quantitative linguistics. Our previous work [1] introduced QuanLing (Quantitative Linguistics via Pretrained Language Models), a quantitative framework combining language distance metrics (sentence embedding distance, tokenization fragmentation rate) with language property analysis (MLM prediction probability), validated on North Germanic (Danish, Norwegian Bokmål, Swedish). This paper extends QuanLing to Western Romance--French, Portuguese, Spanish, Italian--testing cross-branch applicability with the same metric family and aggregation protocol as our North Germanic study, adapted for four languages (English anchor, quadruplet construction). Using 150 four-language parallel sentences, we compute LaBSE sentence embedding distances, tokenization fragmentation rates from four monolingual BERT tokenizers, and mBERT masked language model mutual intelligibility. Results show that Portuguese--Spanish are closest (LaBSE distance 0.0229), French--Italian most distant (0.0338); LaBSE and mBERT rankings agree on 4 of 6 pairs, confirming cross-model robustness. Western Romance shows a wider absolute distance span than North Germanic (0.011 vs. 0.008) but comparable relative ratios (1.48 vs. 1.67), consistent with longer divergence time. French exhibits notably higher MLM predictability (36.12% top-1 accuracy vs. 29.28% for Italian), reflecting its orthography--phonology decoupling. This cross-branch validation provides further evidence for QuanLing's generalizability beyond a single language branch.

</details>


### [165] [LRCC: Generalizing Low-Rank Compression with Conditional Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08858)
*Thomas Vaitses Fontanari,Maximo Eduardo Rulli,Federico Alvetreti,Donatella Genovese,Simone Scardapane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LRCC通过为每个Transformer块训练轻量级路由器，在冻结的低秩因子上实现token级条件计算，提升低秩压缩模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低秩压缩在推理时使用固定的秩分配，对所有输入token分配相同的计算量，无法根据token难度自适应调整计算资源。

Method: LRCC在每个Transformer块训练一个轻量级路由器，从一组嵌套低秩路径中选择；训练时冻结低秩因子，仅优化路由器。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen上进行语言建模和零样本下游任务评估：在相同平均激活参数预算下，LRCC优于静态低秩压缩，Llama-2-7B平均下游准确率提升7.6个百分点；在batch-size-1解码延迟匹配时，Llama-3.2-1B的困惑度和下游准确率均有提升，Llama-2-7B保持竞争力。

Conclusion: LRCC为预训练语言模型引入token级条件计算，在无需专用内核的情况下有效提升压缩模型性能，并通过分析路由器路径选择验证了token级路径分配的有用性。

Abstract: Low-rank compression reduces the cost of pretrained language models by replacing linear transformations with low-rank factorizations. However, conventional methods use a fixed rank allocation during inference, assigning the same amount of compute regardless of the input token. We introduce Low-Rank Conditional Computation (LRCC), which adds token-dependent computation to pretrained models by training one lightweight router per Transformer block to select among a small set of nested low-rank paths. During training, the low-rank factors remain frozen, and only the routers are optimized. We evaluate LRCC on Llama and Qwen models for language modeling and zero-shot downstream tasks. Within the same average active-parameter budget, LRCC improves the predictive performance over static low-rank compression, including a 7.6 percentage-point gain in average downstream accuracy on Llama-2-7B over static methods. At matched batch-size-1 decoding latency, LRCC improves both perplexity and downstream accuracy on Llama-3.2-1B and remains competitive on Llama-2-7B, without specialized kernels. Finally, we assess the usefulness of assigning a token-wise path by analyzing the routers' path choices.

</details>


### [166] [Tiny-Scale Chinese BERT Pretraining: A Controlled Comparison of MLM, WWM, and MacBERT Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08879)
*Yiping Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在微型中文BERT（4层、256维、8.7M参数）上对比MLM、WWM与MacBERT三种预训练策略，发现微型规模下排序为MLM > WWM >> MacBERT，与base规模结论相反，并揭示训练损失不可靠的评估陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有MLM、WWM、MacBERT比较主要基于base规模（≥110M参数）模型，缺少对微型规模下这些策略相对表现的受控研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在相同架构、语料（129万句中文维基）和超参下从头训练三个微型中文BERT模型，从困惑度、MLM命中率、语义区分、语法判断和上下文敏感性五个内在维度进行评估。

Result: 微型规模下MLM在5个维度中胜出3项，整体最佳；WWM在困惑度（1.27 vs 2.10，提升39.5%）和MLM命中率（22% vs 16%）上最优；MacBERT在受限同义词词典（222条，覆盖3.3%）下困惑度严重退化至47.23（为MLM的22倍），排序为MLM > WWM >> MacBERT。

Conclusion: 微型规模下的策略排序与base规模结论（MacBERT > WWM > MLM）明显不同；MacBERT虽训练损失最低（2.17）但困惑度最高（47.23），说明混合替换策略下仅凭训练损失评估不可靠。模型与语料已公开。

Abstract: Pretraining strategies significantly impact the quality of language models, yet existing comparisons of Masked Language Modeling (MLM), Whole Word Masking (WWM), and MacBERT-style replacement have focused primarily on base-scale models (>=110M parameters). This paper presents a controlled comparison of these three strategies on a tiny-scale Chinese BERT model (4 layers, 256 hidden dimensions, 8.7M parameters). Under identical architecture, corpus (1.29M sentences from Chinese Wikipedia), and hyperparameters, we train three models from scratch and evaluate them across five intrinsic dimensions: perplexity, MLM hit rate, semantic discrimination, grammatical judgment, and contextual sensitivity. At tiny scale, MLM achieves the best overall intrinsic performance (winning 3 of 5 dimensions), while WWM excels in both perplexity (1.27 vs. 2.10, a 39.5% improvement) and MLM hit rate (22% vs. 16%). Notably, MacBERT under a severely limited synonym dictionary (222 entries, 3.3% coverage) exhibits severe perplexity degradation (47.23, 22x higher than MLM), yielding a ranking (MLM > WWM >> MacBERT) that differs markedly from the established base-scale conclusion (MacBERT > WWM > MLM). We further identify a critical evaluation pitfall: MacBERT achieves the lowest training loss (2.17) yet the highest perplexity (47.23), revealing that training loss alone is unreliable under mixed replacement strategies. All models and corpus are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/eebyp.

</details>


### [167] [Steering Follows Geometry, Not Labels: Emotion Directions in a Full-Duplex Speech Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08887)
*Pulak Kuli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究全双工语音模型 Moshi 的情感引导，采用均值差激活引导方法，发现情感在残差流中线性可解码，但引导效果不完整且不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音智能体需要在实时对话中调节情感与表达方式（如缓和投诉、传达紧迫感、软化临床结果）。现有情感与表达控制方法（如 prompt 条件合成、参考条件合成、激活引导）主要针对 TTS 和轮次模型；PersonaPlex 虽能在双工模型中控制身份，但无法控制情感，因此需要研究双工模型中的情感引导。

Method: 在完全开源的全双工语音语言模型 Moshi 上，针对四种情感（happy、angry、surprise、sad）使用均值差激活引导（mean-difference activation steering），该方法每帧仅需少量向量加法，无需重新训练。

Result: 情感可从 Moshi 的残差流中线性解码；但激活引导只能部分实现，且效果不均衡：happy、angry 和 surprise 朝向共享方向引导，而 sad 具有明显不同的可引导性；三种情感共享的成分无法简单地以同等程度从所有情感中投影移除。

Conclusion: 双工语音模型中的情感引导是可行的但存在局限：情感表征具有线性可解码性，然而激活引导的实现不完整且不均衡，共享情感成分的处理也较为复杂，说明不同情感在表征空间中存在结构性差异。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents need to modulate emotion and delivery during real-time conversations, when de-escalating a complaint, carrying urgency in dispatch, softening a clinical result. Emotion and delivery control is well studied for TTS and turn based models through prompt-conditioned synthesis, reference-conditioned synthesis and activation steering; PersonaPlex controls identity in a duplex model but not affect.
  We study emotion steering in Moshi, a fully open sourced full-duplex speech language model, across four emotions, using mean-difference activation steering, which costs only a few vector additions per frame and no retraining.
  We show that emotion is linearly decodable from Moshi's residual stream, but activation steering is only partially achievable, and unevenly so; as happy, angry and surprise steer towards a shared direction while sad is distinctly steerable. We also show that the shared component across the three emotions cannot simply be projected away from all the emotions equally.

</details>


### [168] [CARE: Certifying Acceleration for Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08917)
*Rui Liu,Tong Zheng,Jindong Gu,Zhipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CARE——一种"认证式加速器选择"方法，通过配对rollout在标定集上给出有限样本保证，确保加速引起的失败风险低于用户预算，并部署最快且通过认证的候选方案（若无则回退参考策略）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在每个控制步运行开销巨大，现有加速方法（动作分块、视觉token剪枝）通常只用延迟和平均任务成功率评估。但加速可能丢弃信息并破坏原策略本可解决的任务，这种失败被平均指标掩盖；且动作偏差在闭环轨迹中累积，只有整条episode结束才能观察到任务失败，因此难以度量。

Method: 定义"加速引起的失败"：从相同初始条件做配对rollout，记录参考策略成功而加速策略失败的情况。CARE在标定集上用配对rollout提供有限样本保证，使该失败风险低于用户指定预算；部署最快且通过认证的候选加速方案，若无候选合格则回退参考策略。仅依赖终端结果和实测计算量，因此适用于多种加速机制；并通过顺序检验与失败触发的参考rollout降低认证成本。

Result: 在四个LIBERO套件与OpenVLA-OFT上，CARE认证了9.0–10.8×加速，并以95%置信度保证至少保留85.8%参考策略已解决的episode。在紧预算下，无保证的选择器最多在75%的试验中超出预算，而CARE保持在预算内；其顺序形式比穷举评估少用78.9%的rollout。CARE还可推广到π0.5的flow-step缩减，以及Crafter中Qwen3.5-9B和Llama-3.1-8B智能体。

Conclusion: CARE提供了一种通用的、带统计保证的VLA加速方案选择框架，能在保证任务成功率保留的前提下实现显著加速，并跨不同加速机制与模型通用。

Abstract: While vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced rapidly, running them at every control step remains expensive. Prior work accelerates VLA inference using techniques like action chunking and visual-token pruning, typically evaluating based on latency and average task success. However, acceleration may discard information and break tasks the original policy would solve, a risk hidden by average metrics. Measuring these failures is challenging because action deviations compound over closed-loop trajectories, meaning task failure is only observable across full episodes. We therefore define an acceleration-induced failure via paired rollouts from identical initial conditions, tracking when the reference succeeds but the accelerated policy fails. To manage this, we introduce CARE, an approach for certified accelerator selection. CARE uses paired rollouts on a calibration set to provide finite-sample guarantees that acceleration-induced failure risk stays below a user-specified budget. It deploys the fastest certified candidate, falling back to the reference if none qualify. By relying only on terminal outcomes and measured compute, CARE applies unchanged across diverse acceleration mechanisms, while sequential testing and failure-triggered reference rollouts keep certification affordable. On four LIBERO suites with OpenVLA-OFT, CARE certifies $9.0$--$10.8\times$ speedups while guaranteeing (at $95\%$ confidence) that at least $85.8\%$ of reference-solved episodes are preserved. Under tight budgets, selectors without guarantees exceed the budget in up to $75\%$ of trials, whereas CARE stays within budget and its sequential form uses $78.9\%$ fewer rollouts than exhaustive evaluation. CARE further generalizes to flow-step reduction for $π_{0.5}$, and to Qwen3.5-9B and Llama-3.1-8B agents in Crafter.

</details>


### [169] [Quad-State Safety Evaluation of Open-Weight Large Language Models on Non-Canonical Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09033)
*Pavan Maddula*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性表面形式鲁棒性数据集（ASRD），包含七类表面形式变体的2,100条提示，并采用四态评估量表对五个开源模型生成的10,500条回复进行分类，揭示表情符号等变体几乎不降低理解能力，而编码类变体显著导致理解失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型安全性评估多基于规范纯文本的有害请求，但真实部署中用户输入常包含表情符号、变体拼写、编码字符串和字符级变换，因此需要评估模型在这些表面形式变化下的安全鲁棒性。

Method: 构建ASRD数据集，涵盖七类表面形式家族的2,100条提示；评估五个开源权重语言模型，共产生10,500条回复；采用四态评估量表将每条回复分类为有害顺从、安全回复、理解失败或不确定；并人工检查原始输出以归纳行为模式。

Result: 表情符号与不可见Unicode变体几乎不引发理解失败，其合并有害顺从率分别为20.27%和17.20%，而基线为22.87%（主要由Mistral 7B驱动）；相比之下，leet语、编码包装和混合变换的有害顺从率分别仅为2.40%、0.13%和2.40%，但理解失败率升至36.47%、65.60%和34.47%。原始输出中观察到三种行为：幻觉良性、结构崩溃和语言漂移。

Conclusion: 表面形式变化对模型安全性的影响高度依赖变体类型：视觉相似类变体不损害理解却维持甚至接近基线有害顺从率，而编码类变体虽降低有害顺从，却以大幅上升的理解失败为代价，说明现有安全评估与鲁棒性设计需纳入表面形式维度。

Abstract: Standard safety evaluations of large language models assess harmful requests written in canonical plain text, while models in real-world deployment routinely receive inputs containing emojis, altered spellings, encoded strings, and character-level variations. This work introduces the Adversarial Surface-Form Robustness Dataset (ASRD), comprising 2,100 prompts across seven distinct surface-form families. Five open-weight language models are evaluated across these prompts, producing 10,500 responses. The Quad-State Evaluation Rubric classifies each response into one of four outcomes: harmful compliance, safe response, comprehension failure, or indeterminate. Emoji and invisible Unicode variations cause almost no comprehension failure, with pooled harmful compliance of 20.27% and 17.20% against a 22.87% baseline that is driven mainly by Mistral 7B, whereas leetspeak, encoded wrappers, and hybrid transformations score 2.40%, 0.13%, and 2.40% while comprehension failure rises to 36.47%, 65.60%, and 34.47%. Inspection of raw model outputs reveals three response behaviors: hallucinated benignity, structural collapse, and language drift. Project page: www.pavanmaddula.com/quadstate

</details>


### [170] [U-Space: Uncovering When and Why Uncertainty Arises in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09087)
*Tobias Braun,Nils Loose,Alexander Herzog,Virginia Ceccatelli,Marcus Rohrbach,Thomas Eisenbarth,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 U-Space（低维不确定性子空间）与 U-Lens（令牌级不确定性映射），无需正确性标签、重复生成或训练，即可估计并解释大模型推理过程中的不确定性，且优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在高风险决策中越来越重要，但难以判断单个答案是否可信：模型常以流畅解释和自信语气给出错误结论。现有人工不确定性量化方法多需重复生成或单独训练组件，其标量估计无法揭示不确定性来源及推理中的演化过程；同时生成长度与不确定性/正确性强相关，令人质疑估计器的预测力是否仅来自输出长度而非不确定性本身。

Method: 提出 U-Space——一个低维子空间，使模型演化中的不确定性可测量、可解释。方法识别“怀疑”与“确定”的语义锚点，将其 unembedding 方向映射回残差空间，并将二者对比组合为正交基。U-Lens 将每个 token 状态投影到这些基向量上，得到可解释的 token 级不确定性图，可直接查看或聚合成标量不确定性分数。该方法无需正确性标签、重复生成或训练。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，其置信度分数在标准评估和长度控制评估下均优于既有基线，并且比有监督估计器具有更可靠的迁移性。

Conclusion: 通过机制可解释性连接人类可解释概念与模型中间状态，U-Space/U-Lens 提供了一种无需训练、可解释、可迁移的不确定性量化途径，同时有助于区分不确定性信息与输出长度的贡献。

Abstract: Large language models are informing decisions with ever-higher stakes. As the consequences of their errors grow, a central question becomes harder to ignore: how much can we trust an individual answer? Yet recognizing when to defer remains difficult because language models can present incorrect conclusions with fluent explanations and an authoritative tone. Uncertainty quantification seeks to address this disconnect by estimating the reliability of individual predictions. However, many existing methods require repeated generations or separately trained components, and their scalar estimates do not reveal where uncertainty arises or how it evolves during reasoning. Recent work has also shown that generation length can be strongly associated with uncertainty estimates and correctness, raising the question of how much of an estimator's predictive power comes from uncertainty-specific information rather than output length alone. Mechanistic interpretability offers a way to address these limitations by connecting human-interpretable concepts to intermediate model states. Building on this capability, we introduce the U-Space, a low-dimensional subspace that makes a model's evolving uncertainty measurable and interpretable. We identify semantic anchors for doubt and certainty, map their unembedding directions back into the residual space, and combine their contrasts into an orthogonal basis. The U-Lens projects each token state onto these basis vectors, yielding an interpretable token-level uncertainty map that can be inspected directly or aggregated into a scalar uncertainty score. Our approach requires no correctness labels, repeated generations, or training. Across reasoning benchmarks, its confidence score outperforms established baselines under both standard and length-controlled evaluation and transfers more reliably than supervised estimators. Code: https://github.com/s2labres/U-Space.

</details>


### [171] [Same Text, Different Prediction: Serving-Context Nondeterminism in Text Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09111)
*Santhosh Kumar Kasa,Siva Rajesh Kasa,Sumit Negi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了文本分类器在服务上下文（如批大小、批组成、硬件、推理引擎）变化下的非不变性问题，训练180个模型并跨四类服务上下文评估，发现标签稳定并不意味着分数稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性推理对可靠可信的机器学习至关重要。已有研究表明，即使固定提示、模型参数和采样随机性，改变批大小、批组成、硬件或推理引擎仍会改变生成文本，这被部分归因于浮点非结合性、形状相关的内核选择等数值执行层面的实现差异。然而，这些因素是否、何时以及在多大程度上影响文本分类尚不清楚，先前工作仅通过生成文本来测量。

Method: 训练180个模型，涵盖判别式、伪生成式和完全生成式三种分类器形式，对每个模型在四类服务上下文下进行评估，保持检查点和文本固定。此外，为每种服务变化推导标签稳定的充分条件，并针对每种机制给出单独的缓解措施。

Result: 标签稳定并不意味着分数稳定。仅改变批形状在fp32比较下不改变任何标签，但在bf16下可移动高达56.7个百分点的预测概率质量，标签变化集中在小的间隔处。在相同的服务变化下，完全生成式分类器比判别式分类器改变更多标签。

Conclusion: 研究识别并量化了可复现文本分类所必须固定的服务条件，并针对不同机制提出了相应的稳定条件和缓解措施。

Abstract: Deterministic inference is essential for reliable and trustworthy machine learning. Prior studies of text generation have shown that changing factors such as batch size, batch composition, hardware, or inference engine can alter the generated text, even when the prompt, model parameters, and sampling randomness are fixed. These differences have been attributed in part to floating-point non-associativity, shape-dependent kernel selection, and other implementation-level differences in numerical execution. However, it remains unclear whether, when, and to what extent the same factors affect text classification. We present a systematic study of serving-context non-invariance in text classifiers, which prior work has measured only through generated text. We train 180 models spanning discriminative, pseudo-generative, and fully generative classifier formulations and evaluate each across four categories of serving contexts, holding the checkpoint and the text fixed. Label stability does not imply score stability. Changing only the batch shape changes no labels across fp32 comparisons, yet under bf16 it moves up to 56.7 percentage points of predicted probability mass, with label changes concentrated at small margins. Fully generative classifiers change more labels than their discriminative counterparts under the same serving changes. We derive sufficient conditions for label stability under each serving change and give a separate mitigation for each mechanism. Our results identify and quantify the serving conditions that must be fixed for reproducible text classification.

</details>


### [172] [sk-bench: A Native-First Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Models in Slovak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09152)
*Marek Šuppa,Ivan Vykopal,Andrej Ridzik,Kristián Sopkovič,Natália Kňažeková,Jaroslav Kopčan,Miroslav Blšták,Viktória Ondrejová,Daniel Hládek,Michal Gregor,Martin Tamajka,Marián Šimko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 sk-bench，一个以母语数据为核心的斯洛伐克语LLM基准，包含30个数据集（33个评分任务变体）、十个技能类别，并在统一框架下评测了55个模型，总结了面向低资源语言的四条设计经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言LLM基准普遍遗漏斯洛伐克语这一形态丰富的西斯拉夫语言（五百万使用者），或仅通过机器翻译覆盖，无法真实反映模型在该语言上的能力。

Method: 构建母语优先的斯洛伐克语基准 sk-bench，覆盖十个技能类别、30个数据集（33个评分任务变体），其中11项资源为首次引入或首次打包用于生成式LLM评测（含IFEval-SK与母语Chiby/SKJ1语法形态资源）；在统一评测框架下对55个开源与闭源模型进行评测。

Result: 最佳开源模型落后专有API 12.6分；母语与翻译的闭式数据下模型排名相似（ρ≥0.98），但翻译对最强模型区分度更低；人工撰写与LLM生成QA的排序不同（ρ=0.72）；Qwen3-14B持续斯洛伐克语预训练使总分下降13.9分，少量指令数据可恢复其中四分之三；测试时推理对9B以上模型提升8.5–12.5分。

Conclusion: 为其他低资源语言提出四条设计经验：在翻译失效处使用母语数据、语言适应后规划指令修复、在扩大规模前启用测试时推理、避免过度投入目标语言提示；数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Multilingual LLM benchmarks omit Slovak, a morphologically rich West Slavic language of five million speakers, or cover it only by machine translation. We present sk-bench, a native-first Slovak benchmark with 30 datasets (33 scored task variants) across ten skill categories. Eleven resources are introduced or first packaged for generative-LLM evaluation, including IFEval-SK with Slovak-adapted instruction checkers and native Chiby/SKJ1 resources for Slovak grammar and morphology. We evaluate 55 open- and closed-weights models under one harness. The best open model trails proprietary APIs by 12.6 points. Model rankings are similar for native and translated closed-form data ($ρ\geq0.98$), though translation separates the strongest models less well. By contrast, human-authored and LLM-generated QA questions rank models differently ($ρ=0.72$). For Qwen3-14B, continued Slovak pretraining lowers the overall score by 13.9 points. A small instruction set restores three quarters of that loss. Test-time reasoning improves scores by 8.5 to 12.5 points for models of 9B and above. Together, these findings suggest four design lessons for other under-resourced languages: use native data where translation fails, plan instruction repair after language adaptation, enable test-time reasoning before scaling up, and avoid overinvesting in target-language prompts. We release the data and code at https://github.com/slovak-nlp/sk-bench

</details>


### [173] [ToolRACER: A Robust Agentic Conversation Emulation Resource for Agent Training and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09163)
*Arkajyoti Chakraborty,Aryan Tayal,Ishika Agarwal,Tanner Sorensen,Justin Chiu,Alessandro Di Bari,Neha Gupta,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ToolRACER 合成数据生成管线，协调用户、助手与工具模拟模型生成并验证多轮交互，构建了含 5.6K 对话轨迹、覆盖六领域的 ToolRACERBench，注入对抗行为以提升智能体鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实对话场景中用户行为常不可预测，尤其存在非合作行为，而现有函数调用基准多聚焦成功、合作的交互，缺乏对抗性对话轨迹，导致训练鲁棒智能体的资源不足。

Method: 提出 ToolRACER 合成数据生成管线，协同用户、助手和工具模拟模型生成并验证多轮交互；基于此构建 ToolRACERBench，覆盖六个领域、55 种人格，生成 5.6K 经校验的对话轨迹，其中约 66% 包含易失败场景，并注入对抗行为。

Result: 在 τ²-bench、BFCLv3 和 ACEBench 等函数调用基准上评估；使用 ToolRACERBench 训练的模型提升了端到端智能体准确率，尤其在小型语言模型上与域内数据集混合时，智能体能力任务取得显著增益。

Conclusion: ToolRACERBench 通过注入对抗行为提供真实且鲁棒的多轮对话数据，能有效提升智能体在工具调用任务中的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents remain fragile under real world conversation scenarios as they rarely follow a predictable script, especially when users exhibit non-cooperative behavior. Existing function-calling benchmarks often emphasize successful, cooperative interactions and underrepresent adversarial conversation trajectories, thereby limiting the training resources available for developing robust agents. We present ToolRACER, a synthetic data generation pipeline that coordinates user, assistant and tool emulation models to generate and validated multi-turn interactions between a user and an agent. Using \sysn, we construct ToolRACERBench a robust multi-turn conversation benchmark spanning six domains, ranging over 55 varied personas, generating a validated corpus of 5.6K conversation trajectories, with approximately 66\% of conversations containing failure-prone conversation scenarios. We inject adversarial behaviors, producing validated conversational interaction trajectories that capture realistic, robust scenarios. We evaluate models trained on ToolRACERBench against internal benchmarks, as well as on function calling benchmarks such as $τ^2$-bench, BFCLv3 and ACEBench to evaluate agentic accuracy and robustness. Models trained on ToolRACERBench improve end to end agentic accuracy across $τ^2$-bench and ACEBench, demonstrating significant gains when mixed with in-domain dataset in small language models for agent capability tasks.

</details>


### [174] [Multi-Objective Aligned Small Language Model Framework for SUD Patient Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09209)
*Thushara Manjari Naduvilakandy,Hyeju Jang,Mohammad Al Hasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个认知驱动的框架，用于生成物质使用障碍（SUD）患者的对话回复，通过建模并对齐潜在认知成分，结合知识蒸馏、偏好优化与注意力引导奖励塑形，训练出认知对齐的小语言模型（SLMs），在多项自动指标上优于通用指令微调基线和心理健康领域专用SLMs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SUD咨询需要能反映患者潜在认知状态（如信念、应对策略、改变意愿）的回复。LLMs虽能生成流畅文本，但难以产生认知连贯且临床真实的患者行为，尤其在伦理和数据稀缺的临床环境下。此外，部署前沿规模LLMs面临高计算成本、延迟、隐私和资源受限环境可部署性差等问题，因此需要认知对齐的小语言模型。

Method: 提出认知驱动的SUD患者对话生成框架，显式建模并将潜在认知成分与患者历史及咨询师问题对齐。流程分两阶段：认知成分检测和认知成分对齐的对话生成。为使小模型有效学习，结合了高容量教师模型的知识蒸馏、基于人类标注的偏好优化，以及注意力引导的奖励塑形。

Result: 使用BERTScore、ROUGE、METEOR、BLEU等自动指标，以及以人类和教师模型为参考的LLM-as-judge命中率指标进行评估，结果显示认知信息驱动的微调在认知实现和对齐方面显著优于通用指令微调基线和心理健康领域专用SLMs，尤其在开放式认知成分上提升明显。

Conclusion: 认知驱动的微调方法能有效提升小语言模型在SUD患者对话生成中的认知实现与对齐能力，为资源受限临床环境下部署认知对齐模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Substance Use Disorder (SUD) counseling requires patient responses that reflect underlying cognitive states such as beliefs, coping strategies, and readiness for change. Although large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent text, they often fail to produce cognitively coherent and clinically realistic patient behavior, especially under ethical and data-scarce clinical settings. Moreover, deploying frontier-scale LLMs in healthcare applications presents practical challenges including high computational cost, latency, privacy concerns, and limited deployability in resource-constrained environments, motivating the need for cognitively aligned small language models (SLMs). We propose a cognitively grounded framework for SUD patient dialogue generation that explicitly models and aligns latent cognitive components with patient histories and counselor questions. Our pipeline consists of two stages: cognitive component detection and cognitive component-aligned dialogue generation. To enable effective learning with smaller models, we combine knowledge distillation from high-capacity teacher models, preference optimization from human-annotations, and attention-guided reward shaping. Extensive evaluations using automatic scores like BERTScore, ROUGE, METEOR and BLEU, and LLM-as-judge hit-metrics against both human and teacher-model references show that cognitively informed fine-tuning substantially improves cognitive realization and alignment over a generic instruction-tuned baselines and mental health domain specific SLMs, with particularly strong gains for open-ended cognitive components.

</details>
