The corpus aligns spoken interaction with synchronized behavioral data, including gaze, head and body movement, facial behavior, hand and finger tracking. Its reference transcripts and linguistic annotations provide a reliable interface between this multimodal spoken interaction and predominantly text-based NLP methods. This interface is important because automatically transcribing speech can distort linguistically relevant information, while downstream models trained on existing resources may additionally face transfer challenges when applied to transcribed spoken data. InterView-C therefore provides word-timed and manually post-edited verbatim transcripts for all 54 recordings, interview-item timings, questionnaire responses and negation cue and scope annotations for 1,422 sentences, 1,398 of them doubly annotated (α=0.87 for cues; α=0.81 for scopes). We demonstrate both challenges empirically: nine open-weight ASR systems disproportionately misrecognize short closed answers and number words, while negation models trained on existing corpora show lower and highly variable performance on our transcribed interviews than a model trained on the InterView-C annotations. InterView-C thus enables linguistic analyses of spoken interaction while retaining their alignment with rich multimodal behavior.

</details>


### [211] [HySPE: Positional Encoding via Symplectic Dual Shears](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10154)
*Zhongping Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出双曲辛位置编码（HySPE），利用非紧致辛变换刻画位置注意力，在数值稳定性和长序列外推上显著优于RoPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准旋转位置编码（RoPE）仅等价于辛群Sp(2,R)的紧致椭圆分支（旋转），缺乏对双曲（非紧致）分支的利用。朴素绝对分解会带来指数级表示漂移，因此需要一种能同时保证长序列外推能力与数值稳定的位置编码方案。

Method: HySPE在Sp(2,R)的双曲分支上通过双剪切（dual shears）的阻尼对称复合实现共形辛收缩，每个通道对有两个谱衰减率；为消除指数表示漂移，将算子在不变特征基中对角化，并引入分块坐标重基（blockwise coordinate rebasing）与自适应中心化执行。

Result: 方法保证与长度无关的数值界，前向延迟与缓存版RoPE相当（RTX 4090上7.21 ms）。TinyShakespeare上HySPE-UltraLong在16×零样本外推（L=4096）下困惑度稳定为4.810，而RoPE退化至131.198；在WikiText-103的51M参数子词Transformer（L_train=512）上域内性能与RoPE相当，且可稳健外推至8192，尾部困惑度较RoPE降低83.9%。

Conclusion: 受控实验表明HySPE具备优异的外推鲁棒性与数值稳定性，但其在大型基础模型上的扩展行为仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce Hyperbolic Symplectic Positional Encoding (HySPE), grounding positional attention in non-compact symplectic transformations. While canonical Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) parameterizes the compact, elliptic branch of $\Sp(2,\R)$ via rotations, HySPE operationalizes its hyperbolic branch via a damped symmetric composition of dual shears, yielding a conformally symplectic contraction with two spectral decay rates per channel pair. To eliminate the exponential representation drift inherent to naive absolute factorizations, we diagonalize the operator in its invariant eigenbasis and introduce blockwise coordinate rebasing with adaptive centered execution. This guarantees length-independent numerical bounds while matching cached RoPE forward latency (7.21\,ms on an RTX 4090). On TinyShakespeare, HySPE-UltraLong maintains an invariant perplexity of 4.810 up to $16\times$ zero-shot extrapolation ($L=4096$), whereas RoPE degrades to 131.198. Scaled to a 51M-parameter subword Transformer on WikiText-103 ($L_{\text{train}}=512$), HySPE closely matches RoPE in-domain while robustly extrapolating to length 8192, reducing tail perplexity by 83.9\% over RoPE. While these controlled experiments establish HySPE's extrapolation robustness and numerical stability, evaluating its scaling behavior on large-scale foundation models remains an important direction for future investigation.

</details>


### [212] [Beyond Outcome Rewards: Constructing and Assigning Retrieval Credit for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10179)
*Wenyu Huang,Xinyu Hou,Pavlos Vougiouklis,Ruofei Lai,Jeff Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究中间监督如何改进搜索智能体的强化学习，通过奖励塑形与信用分配策略提升多步搜索轨迹的学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索智能体让大语言模型能迭代检索信息以应对复杂多跳问题，而带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）是后训练此类智能体的有效途径，但其依赖稀疏的、基于结果的整体监督，导致信用分配困难、学习效率受限。

Method: 系统探究中间监督的作用，研究一系列奖励塑形与信用分配策略，从中间检索步骤提供学习信号；在此基础上提出一个训练框架，将中间信号与最终结果奖励相结合，以改进多步搜索轨迹的学习。实验在多个基准、匹配训练条件下进行，并比较不同中间信号类型及其信用分配位置的影响。

Result: 在匹配训练条件下，多个基准实验显示搜索智能体的总体性能得到提升；同时表明中间信号的选择及其信用分配的位置均会影响训练行为。

Conclusion: 奖励设计与信用分配是训练高效搜索智能体的重要设计维度。

Abstract: Search agents enable Large Language Models (LLMs) to iteratively retrieve and use information for complex multi-hop questions. Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) offers a promising approach for post-training such agents, but its reliance on sparse, outcome-based supervision can make credit assignment difficult and limit learning efficiency. In this paper, we systematically investigate how intermediate supervision can improve reinforcement learning for search agents. We study a range of reward-shaping and credit-assignment strategies that provide learning signals from intermediate retrieval steps. Building on these insights, we develop a training framework that combines intermediate signals with final outcome rewards to improve learning from multi-step search trajectories. Experiments across multiple benchmarks under matched training conditions demonstrate improvements in aggregate search-agent performance and show that both the choice of intermediate signal and where its credit is assigned affect training behaviour. These findings show that reward design and credit assignment are important design dimensions for training effective search agents.

</details>


### [213] [LLM Persuasion Is in the Eye of the Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10232)
*Kamile Dementaviciute,Julija Vaitonyte,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对九种自动化说服力评测方法进行统一复现，在相同十五个LLM上运行并比较排名一致性，发现方法间仅弱相关（平均Spearman ρ=0.25），主因是模型拒答行为与任务类型差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的 persuasiveness 已可与人类专家相比，既有益于教育与健康传播，也可能被用于操纵与误导，因此评测需求上升。但现有评测碎片化：各研究对"说服"定义不一，广泛结论常建立在狭窄情境评估上。自动化方法可规模化、可覆盖难以或不宜在人类身上测试的高风险说服形式，因此适合用来直接比较这些评估。

Method: 将九种已发表的自动化说服评测方法适配到统一设置，在相同的十五个LLM上运行，检验其模型排名是否一致及原因。分析中考察模型拒答行为（直接或间接）对一致性的影响，以及通用能力与理性说服（非操纵）方法、操纵类方法之间的关系。

Result: 方法间一致性仅为弱相关（平均Spearman ρ=0.25）。对部分任务拒答而另一些不拒答的模型使一致性下降约四分之一，且拒答主要出现在操纵类任务上。通用能力方面，多数理性说服方法能追踪能力水平，而多数操纵类方法则不能。

Conclusion: 一致性更多取决于方法设定的任务类型，而非其打分方式（但受限于仅八种方法可分析，此结论仅为指示性）。更广泛地看，说服力得分同时混合了模型"能否说服"与"是否愿意说服"，单一分数只对其自身设定有信息量，难以反映模型跨任务的整体说服力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have already been shown to match or exceed human experts in persuasion. While their persuasive capabilities hold promise for beneficial uses such as education and health communication, they can also be used to manipulate and misinform, making their evaluation a growing priority for developers and regulators. That evaluation, however, remains fragmented: studies differ in what they treat as persuasion, and broad claims often rest on narrow, situation-specific assessments. Automated methods, often modelled on human studies, offer a way to compare such assessments directly, as they can be run on the same models at scale and can include high-risk forms of persuasion that would be difficult or unethical to test on people. In this study, we adapt nine published automated methods to a shared setup, run them on the same fifteen LLMs, and ask whether their rankings agree and why. We find that the methods agree only weakly (mean Spearman $ρ= 0.25$). Our analyses point to two contributing factors. Models that refuse some tasks but not others, directly or indirectly, lower agreement by about a quarter, and these refusals fall mostly on manipulation tasks. General capability also plays a part: most rational persuasion (non-manipulative) methods track it, whereas most manipulation methods do not. Together, these findings suggest that agreement depends more on the task a method sets than on how it scores persuasion, although this pattern is only indicative given the eight methods available for analysis. More broadly, our results suggest that persuasion scores combine a model's ability to persuade with its willingness to do so. A single score is therefore informative about its own setting, but says little about a model's persuasiveness across tasks.

</details>


### [214] [Nobody Truly Agrees on Sentiment: Humans, Bespoke Tools, and LLMs Struggle with Social Media Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10318)
*Himarsha R. Jayanetti,Sivakanesan Dhanushkanda,Shuai Hao,Michael L. Nelson,Michele C. Weigle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三种情感分析工具与三种大语言模型在100条推文上与六位人工评分者的一致性，发现即使人工之间也仅为中等一致，Twitter-roBERTa-base与人工评分最吻合，说明领域微调与人工评估对社交媒体情感分析仍然关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体情感分析工具被广泛使用，但其局限性常被忽视，尤其在标注可靠性方面缺乏系统评估，因此需要考察自动化工具与人工评分者之间的一致性水平。

Method: 对100条推文，以六位人工评分者为基准，评估TextBlob、VADER、Twitter-roBERTa-base三种情感分析工具以及Qwen3-32B、GPT-OSS-120B、Llama-4-Maverick-17B三种大语言模型，采用Cohen's kappa（两两比较）与Fleiss' kappa（多评分者）衡量一致性，并区分二元与三类情感分类。

Result: 人工评分者之间仅为中等（fair）一致，体现情感标注的主观性；二元分类（负面vs非负面、正面vs非正面）的一致性高于三类分类。Twitter-roBERTa-base与人工评分对齐最好，尤其在区分负面与非负面时优于其他工具和LLM；LLM彼此间一致性较高，与人工为中等至较高一致，在正面与非正面分类上表现更好。

Conclusion: 领域特定的微调对可靠的社交媒体情感分析仍不可或缺，以人为中心的评估仍是建立金标准标签的必要途径。

Abstract: Social media is a rich source of real-time public sentiment, but widely used sentiment analysis tools are often applied without understanding their limitations. In this study, we evaluate the inter-rater reliability of three bespoke sentiment analysis tools (TextBlob, VADER, and Twitter-roBERTa-base) and three large language models (LLMs: Qwen3-32B, GPT-OSS-120B, Llama-4-Maverick-17B) against six human raters across 100 tweets. We measured agreement using two statistical measures: Cohen's kappa for pairwise comparisons and Fleiss' kappa for multiple raters. Even among the human raters, our results showed only fair agreement, highlighting the subjectivity of sentiment analysis. Higher agreement was observed under the binary sentiment classification (negative vs. non-negative and positive vs. non-positive) than under the three-class classification across both humans and automated tools. The Twitter-roBERTa-base model showed the strongest alignment with human ratings, outperforming both bespoke sentiment tools and LLMs, particularly in distinguishing negative versus non-negative sentiment. LLMs showed substantial agreement among themselves and moderate to substantial alignment with humans, performing better in positive vs. non-positive classifications. Our findings underscore that domain-specific fine-tuning remains crucial for reliable social media sentiment analysis, and human-centered evaluation remains essential for establishing gold-standard labels.

</details>


### [215] [Learning to Act with Task Progress: Distilling Small Agents from Compact Teacher Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10332)
*Wenxi Gan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务进度蒸馏（TPD），用简短的任务阶段标签替代完整推理过程来监督小模型智能体，在ALFWorld上验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用大模型示范训练小智能体可以避免每步都调用大模型。关键问题是：教师轨迹中包含推理、动作和任务进度信息，究竟应保留哪些内容来训练学生模型。

Method: 提出Task-Progress Distillation（TPD）离线方法：为每个示范动作配一个描述当前任务阶段的短标签，学生模型学习这些紧凑目标，通过对可允许的"阶段-动作"对联合打分来选择动作，并由确定性执行器在环境中执行。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，1.7B学生模型用404条示范达到72.4%平均未见任务成功率（TPD或仅动作监督），而基于推理训练、受限动作选择的学生仅48.3%。在200条示范时，显式阶段带来额外收益：成功率从48.0%提升到67.7%。示范更多时，仅动作监督的学生追平差距，两者在808条示范时均达76.9%。共享历史分析表明，TPD的局部优势部分来自子目标切换时（尤其从物体获取到处理）的更好决策。

Conclusion: 紧凑监督可以训练出有效的小型任务智能体；显式任务进度在中等示范预算下提供额外指导价值。

Abstract: Learning from large-model demonstrations offers a way to train small agents that can complete recurring tasks without calling a large model at every step. A central design choice is what to retain from teacher trajectories that contain reasoning, actions, and information about task progress. We introduce Task-Progress Distillation (TPD), an offline approach that pairs each demonstrated action with a short label describing the current task stage. The student learns these compact targets and selects actions by jointly scoring admissible stage--action pairs, which a deterministic harness executes in the environment. On ALFWorld, a 1.7B student trained with 404 demonstrations achieves 72.4\% mean unseen task success with either TPD or action-only supervision, compared with 48.3\% for a reasoning-trained student using constrained action selection. Explicit stages provide an additional benefit at 200 demonstrations, improving success from 48.0\% to 67.7\% over action-only supervision. With more demonstrations, the action-only student closes the gap, and both approaches reach 76.9\% at 808 demonstrations. Shared-history analyses link part of TPD's local advantage to better decisions when moving between subgoals, particularly from object acquisition to processing. These results show that compact supervision can train effective small task agents, while explicit task progress provides additional guidance at an intermediate demonstration budget.

</details>


### [216] [Document-Level Text Simplification in Estonian Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10378)
*Meeri-Ly Muru,Eduard Barbu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统评估五种多语言大模型在爱沙尼亚语文档级简化中的表现，比较三种提示策略，并提出新的文档连贯性指标与公开资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 句子级简化在高资源语言已有进展，但文档级简化涉及语篇连贯、指代消解与跨段落一致性，在形态丰富、低资源语言（如爱沙尼亚语）中几乎未被探索。

Method: 评估五种先进多语言大模型，采用三种提示策略：单次生成、基于流水线的模块化智能体、以及指南增强的流水线；评价框架结合可读性、语义保持与语篇连贯的自动指标，并配合结构化人工标注协议。

Result: Gemini-2.0与LLaMA-3.3的输出接近母语流畅度且语义保持良好，其他模型存在明显语法与语义不足。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了新的文档级连贯性指标、基于证据的提示策略以及可复现的公开资源，推动低资源语言文档级简化研究。

Abstract: Document-level text simplification involves transformations that go beyond sentence-internal edits, addressing discourse coherence, anaphora resolution, and cross-paragraph consistency. Despite advances in sentence-level simplification for high-resource languages, document-level simplification in morphologically rich, low-resource languages such as Estonian remains largely unexplored. This study presents a comprehensive evaluation of five state-of-the-art multilingual large language models (LLMs) for document-level simplification in Estonian. Three prompting strategies are examined: single-pass generation, pipeline-based modular agents, and guideline-augmented pipelines. The evaluation framework integrates automatic metrics assessing readability, semantic preservation, and discourse coherence, alongside a structured manual annotation protocol. The findings indicate that Gemini-2.0 and LLaMA-3.3 produce outputs with near-native fluency and strong meaning preservation, whereas other models display notable grammatical and semantic limitations. This work contributes novel document-level coherence metrics, evidence-based prompting strategies, and publicly available resources for reproducibility.

</details>


### [217] [CoTrace: Data Recipes for Training Terminal Agents with Harness-Model Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10426)
*Jixuan Chen,Jiaxin Zhang,Qinyuan Ye,Yada Pruksachatkun,Haoxiang Zhang,Jingming Zhuo,Yifan Zhang,Yutong Dai,Juntao Tan,Xiangyu Peng,Silvio Savarese,Zeyuan Chen,Lianhui Qin,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出交替式协同演化框架与CoTrace方法，强调轨迹价值取决于生成它的运行时装置（harness），通过按装置匹配的轨迹路由与课程刷新提升终端智能体能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体的能力同时取决于模型权重与运行时装置（负责提示格式化、工具绑定、错误恢复）。现有harness-model协同演化方法虽同时改进二者，却常把harness搜索中产生的轨迹当作无差别的回放缓存，忽视了轨迹对模型训练的价值取决于其生成时所处的harness，因此需要系统分析这一接口。

Method: 建立交替式协同演化框架，通过组件级晋升决策解耦harness搜索与策略训练；在此框架下提出CoTrace，一种harness感知的数据配方，显式管理轨迹路由、来源匹配与课程刷新。循环出现的执行失败引导harness合成，策略训练严格以经校验、且与所采用运行时匹配的rollout为条件：SFT使用匹配轨迹，RL使用新鲜在线交互。

Result: 在Tmax晋升划分上，CoTrace使Qwen3.5-9B在SFT下从78提升至88个已解任务，在线强化学习变体达到90。紧凑的harness匹配语料以远低于跨同类harness汇聚的大语料的算力，带来稳定增益。在Terminal-Bench 2.1与SWE-bench Lite上的评测显示，分布外迁移根本上取决于harness兼容性，保持训练与评测运行时一致可避免在异质脚手架下出现的流程性执行崩溃。

Conclusion: 轨迹训练价值与生成它的harness强相关；采用harness感知的数据路由与来源匹配，可用更小语料与更低算力获得更稳健的提升，且训练-评测运行时的一致性对分布外迁移至关重要。

Abstract: Terminal-agent capability depends jointly on model weights and the runtime harness that formats prompts, binds tools, and handles error recovery. Existing harness-model co-evolution approaches improve both components, yet often treat trajectories produced during harness search as an undifferentiated replay buffer. This practice overlooks that a trajectory's value for model training depends on the harness under which it was generated. To systematically analyze this interface, we establish an alternating co-evolution framework that decouples harness search and policy training through component-wise promotion decisions. Within this framework, we introduce CoTrace, a harness-aware data recipe that explicitly governs trajectory routing, provenance matching, and curriculum refresh. Under CoTrace, recurring execution failures guide harness synthesis, while policy training is strictly conditioned on verified rollouts matched to the adopted runtime for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or fresh online interactions for reinforcement learning (RL). On the Tmax promotion split, CoTrace advances Qwen3.5-9B from 78 to 88 solved tasks under supervised fine-tuning while an online reinforcement variant reaches 90. Specifically, a compact harness-matched corpus produces steady model gains at substantially lower compute than much larger corpora pooled across sibling harnesses. Furthermore, evaluations on Terminal-Bench 2.1 and SWE-bench Lite show that out-of-distribution transfer depends fundamentally on harness compatibility, where maintaining consistency between training and evaluation runtimes prevents procedural execution breakdowns observed under foreign scaffolds.

</details>


### [218] [PHRBench: A Behavioral Evaluation of Post-Hallucination Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10455)
*Linghao Meng,Feng He,Xuan Yang,Junyuan Mao,Pinze Ren,Deqing Mu,Hesen Yang,Qiankun Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PHRBench基准，从行为层面结构化分析LLM在后幻觉推理（PHR）中如何解决幻觉前提，覆盖4个领域和18个模型，并发现成功恢复较罕见但与更频繁的信念更新相关，同时幻觉提示属性对成功恢复具有较强预测力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉信息会在多阶段LLM系统中传播并成为后续推理的上下文。现有后幻觉推理研究主要刻画最终结果变化和总体推理动态，对模型在响应层面如何解决幻觉前提的理解不足，因此需要更细粒度的行为分析视角。

Method: 提出PHRBench，一个受控基准，覆盖4个领域和18个大型语言模型。该基准独立于最终答案正确性，通过三个行为维度刻画每条推理轨迹：幻觉顺从（Hallucination Compliance）、幻觉规避（Hallucination Avoidance）和启发式纠正（Heuristic Correction），并定义"有洞察力的轨迹"为最终能到达正确答案的成功纠正。

Result: 在4820个受控实例上，发现成功恢复相对罕见，且与推理轨迹中更频繁的信念更新相关；此外，幻觉提示的属性对成功恢复具有显著预测信号，轻量级预测器达到0.847的AUROC。

Conclusion: 研究提供了后幻觉推理的行为视角，刻画了LLM如何解决错误上下文以及成功恢复可能发生的条件，为理解多阶段LLM系统中的幻觉传播与纠正提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: Hallucinated information can propagate through multi-stage LLM systems and become part of the context for subsequent reasoning. Existing studies of post-hallucination reasoning (PHR) mainly characterize changes in final outcomes and aggregate reasoning dynamics, leaving how models resolve hallucinated premises at the response level insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce PHRBench, a controlled benchmark for behaviorally structured PHR across four domains and 18 large language models. PHRBench characterizes each reasoning trajectory independently of final-answer correctness through Hallucination Compliance, Hallucination Avoidance, and Heuristic Correction, and defines an insightful trajectory as successful correction that ultimately reaches the correct answer. Across 4820 controlled instances, we find that successful recovery remains relatively rare and is associated with more frequent belief updates along the reasoning trajectory. We further find that properties of the hallucinated prompt contain substantial predictive signal for successful recovery, with a lightweight predictor achieving an AUROC of 0.847. These findings provide a behavioral view of post-hallucination reasoning, characterizing how LLMs resolve erroneous context and when successful recovery is likely to occur.

</details>


### [219] [Your Prompt Should Do More: Effects of Retrieval Instructions in Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10508)
*Amanda Myntti,Jenna Kanerva,Veronika Laippala,Filip Ginter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究指令如何影响非对称检索中查询的向量表示，发现模型在查询侧存在干扰项时难以遵循简单任务指令，而针对性地加入查询侧干扰项微调可显著改善该问题且对其他任务影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前提示式嵌入模型在检索中被广泛关注，新数据集与研究表明现有模型往往无法可靠地遵循检索指令。作者希望厘清指令究竟如何影响检索查询的表示，并探究模型不遵循指令的原因。

Method: 在非对称检索任务中考察指令对查询表示的影响机制；在评测中引入查询侧干扰项以观察模型对指令的遵循情况；基于对训练设置与评测方式的分析提出假设，并通过加入查询侧干扰项进行微调来验证。

Result: 一旦评测中包含查询侧干扰项，模型即使面对简单任务指令也可能无法遵循；采用加入查询侧干扰项的微调方式后，模型表现获得显著提升，同时对其他任务的影响很小。

Conclusion: 模型不遵循指令的行为主要源于当前嵌入模型的训练设置与评测方式，通过在训练中引入查询侧干扰项可以针对性缓解该问题，且具有良好的任务间泛化代价平衡。

Abstract: Prompted embedding models have recently received increasing attention, particularly for retrieval, where detailed retrieval instructions are provided as part of the retrieval prompt. Several new datasets and studies have examined this setting, showing that the current embedding models often struggle to follow such instructions reliably. In this paper, we study the mechanism of how instructions actually affect the representations of retrieval queries in asymmetric retrieval tasks. We show that models can fail to follow even simple task instructions when query-side distractors are included in the evaluation. We hypothesize that this behavior is driven by the training setup of current embedding models and their evaluation, and show that fine-tuning with added query-side distractors leads to substantial improvements, with minimal effect on other tasks.

</details>


### [220] [EngramEdit: Decoupled Knowledge Updates in LLMs through Conditional Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10533)
*Hongru Cai,Ran Wei,Wenjie Wang,Chengfa Wu,Ning Song,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EngramEdit，通过条件记忆（如 DeepSeek Engram 的 n-gram 嵌入查找）实现解耦的知识更新，在保持 Transformer 主干固定的前提下近乎完美地编辑事实知识，并能泛化到未见表达和多跳推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件记忆架构（如 DeepSeek Engram）以有限额外计算扩展 LLM 容量，并展现出将事实知识存储与通用计算解耦的潜力，从而可在固定 Transformer 主干的情况下更新事实知识。但实现该潜力存在挑战：同一事实的不同表达会激活不同的 n-gram 嵌入，而更新共享嵌入又可能无意中改变模型对其他事实的预测。

Method: EngramEdit 首先计算目标记忆表示，使模型在多种表达下都能预测更新后的事实；然后联合更新共享的 n-gram 嵌入，使这些嵌入在多种表达和编辑任务中匹配目标表示，并对被频繁复用的嵌入施加更强惩罚，以保护无关知识。

Result: 实验表明，EngramEdit 能够通过条件记忆实现独立的事实知识更新，编辑成功率近乎完美。修订后的知识可用于未见表达和多跳推理，在思维链（CoT）提示下准确率约为最强基线的三倍。即使事实更新不断累积，无关知识和通用能力也基本得以保留。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明，EngramEdit 将条件记忆转变为可编辑的知识接口，使其作用超越模型扩展，支持解耦的知识更新。

Abstract: Conditional memory architectures such as DeepSeek Engram use input n-grams to look up learned embeddings, expanding the capacity of large language models (LLMs) with limited additional computation. Beyond model scaling, this architecture has demonstrated the potential to decouple factual knowledge storage from general-purpose computation, offering a promising route to updating factual knowledge while keeping the Transformer backbone fixed. Realizing this potential is challenging because different expressions of a fact may activate different n-gram embeddings, while updating shared embeddings can unintentionally change the model's predictions about other facts. We propose EngramEdit for decoupled knowledge updates through conditional memory. EngramEdit first computes target memory representations that make the model predict the updated fact across multiple expressions. It then jointly updates the shared n-gram embeddings to match these targets across expressions and edits, penalizing updates to frequently reused embeddings more strongly to preserve unrelated knowledge. Experiments show that EngramEdit enables independent factual knowledge updates through conditional memory, achieving near-perfect editing success. Revised knowledge is usable across unseen expressions and in multi-hop reasoning, with nearly three times the strongest baseline's accuracy under chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting. Unrelated knowledge and general capabilities are largely preserved even as factual updates accumulate. These findings show that EngramEdit turns conditional memory into an editable knowledge interface, extending its role beyond model scaling to support decoupled knowledge updates.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Mitigating Uncertainty Interactions in GenAI-based Adaptive Systems: Vision, Challenges and Preliminary Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08881)
*Karthik Vaidhyanathan,Norha Villegas,Raffaela Mirandola,Nicolás Cardozo,Ivana Dusparic,Marin Litoiu,Ralf Reussner,Gabriel Tamura*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概念框架，用于刻画和缓解GenAI软件密集系统中不确定性的交互，并强调须贯穿全部软件生命周期进行缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI组件（含LLM与智能体子系统）带来跨系统层次的新型与叠加不确定性，其概率性输出、幻觉、上下文窗口限制和记忆陈旧等特性要求重新审视既有不确定性框架，尤其是不确定性发生与表现之间的交互。

Method: 作为立场论文，提出初步概念框架与指导原则；引入“缓解函数”，将不确定性交互类别、涉及的不确定性类型、系统设计特征、关键性及缓解性架构策略，映射到受影响的质量属性及相应权衡。

Result: 通过三个示例用例——LLM服务、AI驱动的软件运维、智能体云管理——对框架进行了概念验证。

Conclusion: 缓解须覆盖需求分析、设计时决策与运行时自适应，因而无法与执行期的监控和分析解耦。

Abstract: Modern software-intensive systems increasingly incorporate GenAI components, including LLM and agentic subsystems, which introduce novel and compounding sources of uncertainty across system layers. The self-adaptive systems community has made significant strides in understanding and managing uncertainty. The intrinsic characteristics of GenAI, including probabilistic outputs, hallucinations, context window limitations, and memory staleness, demand a re-examination of existing frameworks and mitigation strategies for dealing with uncertainty, and especially, the interactions among uncertainty occurrences and manifestations. This position paper posits an initial conceptual framework that establishes preliminary guidelines for characterizing and mitigating uncertainty interactions in GenAI-based software-intensive systems. We argue that mitigation must be considered across the full software lifecycle, encompassing requirements analysis, design-time decisions, and run-time adaptations, which implies the unfeasibility of decoupling it from monitoring and analysis at execution. We introduce the notion of a mitigation function that maps uncertainty interaction categories, involved uncertainty types, system design characteristics, criticality, and mitigating architectural tactics to impacted quality attributes and corresponding tradeoffs. As a proof of concept, we apply the framework to three illustrative use cases: LLM serving, AI-driven software operations, and agentic cloud management.

</details>


### [222] [Psychological Safety in Software Engineering Teams: A Systematic Mapping Study of Team Processes and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08896)
*Adnan Manzoor,Muhammad Azeem Akbar,Jussi Kasurinen,Muhammad Hamza,Aakash Ahmad,Abdul Razzaq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文通过系统映射研究分析了2006年至2026年间112篇软件工程领域心理安全感相关文献，揭示其多维性、情境依赖性及与团队绩效和过程的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理安全感是软件开发团队中支持协作、知识共享、人际风险承担与学习的重要人为和行为因素，但软件工程领域现有证据分散于团队过程、绩效结果、情境条件与障碍等不同方面，缺乏系统整合。

Method: 遵循已有软件工程指南开展系统映射研究，对2006年至2026年6月间发表的112篇原始研究进行描述性统计与主题分析，围绕六个研究问题展开。

Result: 心理安全感被识别为一种多维、情境依赖的团队层面构念，其特征包括人际风险承担、开放沟通、协作、学习、信任与支持性互动；主要与生产力、软件质量、创新、团队满意度、协作、沟通与决策相关联，并受领导力、组织文化、工作环境、信任及社会文化与个体条件影响；常见障碍包括沟通问题、组织约束、领导力问题、多样性与包容性挑战及工作负荷压力；相关实践虽多样，但有效性与长期可持续性证据有限。

Conclusion: 心理安全感是多维、情境依赖的团队层面构念，与团队互动和绩效相关；研究强调需要在软件开发环境中开展纵向、情境敏感的干预研究。

Abstract: Context: Psychological safety is an important human and behavioral factor in software development teams, supporting collaboration, knowledge sharing, interpersonal risk-taking, and learning. However, evidence in software engineering remains fragmented across team processes, performance outcomes, contextual conditions, barriers. Objective: This study maps literature on psychological safety in software engineering, examining its conceptualization, associations with team performance and processes, contextual influences, barriers, and strengthening strategies. Method: We conducted a systematic mapping study following established software engineering guidelines. We analyzed 112 primary studies published between 2006 and June 2026 using descriptive statistics and thematic analysis across six research questions. Results: Psychological safety emerges as a multidimensional, context-dependent, team-level construct characterized by interpersonal risk-taking, open communication, collaboration, learning, trust, and supportive interaction. It is predominantly associated with productivity, software quality, innovation, team satisfaction, collaboration, communication, and decision-making. These relationships are shaped by leadership, organizational culture, work environment, trust, and sociocultural and individual conditions. Recurring barriers include communication problems, organizational constraints, leadership issues, diversity and inclusion challenges, and workload pressures. Although diverse practices are reported, evidence of their effectiveness and long-term sustainability remains limited. Conclusion: Psychological safety is a multidimensional, context-dependent team-level construct associated with team interaction and performance. This study highlights the need for longitudinal, context-sensitive research on psychological safety interventions in software development environments.

</details>


### [223] [Evaluating Change Point Detection Methods for Software Performance Regression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09023)
*Diego Elias Costa,Michele Tucci,Luca Traini,Daniele Di Pompeo,Thomas Bach,Francois Farquet,David Daly,Simon Eismann,Petr Tůma,Vittorio Cortellessa,André van Hoorn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对12种变化点检测（CPD）方法在真实软件性能测量数据上的有效性进行了大规模实证评估，涵盖了数据采集、人工标注一致性分析及方法精度对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能问题是关键的软件质量议题，可能导致用户信任流失、违反服务等级协议并影响业务效率。软件性能工程因此转向尽早检测性能回归。CPD方法虽已广泛用于金融、医疗等领域，但其在软件性能测量中的有效性尚未被充分评估。

Method: 首先从三个大型软件系统采集性能测量数据，刻画性能时间序列的独特属性；随后进行大规模人工标注，并评估标注者识别潜在性能变化的一致性；最后评估12种不同CPD方法检测潜在性能变化的准确性。

Result: 研究刻画了软件性能时间序列的独特属性，获得了人工标注一致性结果，并对比得出12种CPD方法的检测准确性及其在软件性能回归分析中的适用性与有效性洞察。

Conclusion: 该研究为软件性能回归分析中CPD方法的适用性与有效性提供了实证依据与指导性见解。

Abstract: Performance issues in software systems are a critical quality issue that can erode user trust, violate service-level agreements, and ultimately affect business efficiency. Consequently, software performance engineering has shifted its focus to developing robust techniques to detect performance regressions as early as possible in the development cycle. Performance regression analysis often relies on time series of performance measurements to detect significant changes in performance behavior. Change Point Detection (CPD) methods have been widely used to automate the identification of such changes in various domains, including finance, healthcare, and performance monitoring. However, the effectiveness of these methods for software performance measurements has not been thoroughly evaluated. In this paper, we present a comprehensive study to evaluate the effectiveness of various CPD methods on real-world software performance measurement datasets. We start by collecting performance measurement data from three large software systems and characterizing the unique properties of performance time series data. Then, we undertake a large-scale effort to annotate and evaluate the consistency of human annotators' identification of potential performance changes. Thereafter, we evaluate the accuracy of twelve distinct CPD methods in detecting potential performance changes, providing insights into their applicability and effectiveness in software performance regression analysis.

</details>


### [224] [Large-scale Repository Engineering via Agent-Native Reusable Code Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09079)
*Haibo Jin,Peng Kuang,Xucheng Yu,Jerry Wang,Dehao Wu,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"代码原语"（Code Primitives，带接口契约、依赖闭包与验证测试的智能体原生可复用组件）、CodeFace 库与 LEGO 框架，实现仓库级代码构建，并在 522 项任务的 LEGO-REPO 基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型已具备开发环境，代码生成从函数级走向仓库级，但构建完整仓库仍很困难：模块、接口、配置、测试与依赖必须协同工作，现有方法缺乏可复用、可验证、可适配的组件化支撑。

Method: 提出 Code Primitives（含接口契约、依赖闭包、验证测试与出处信息），每个原语由常驻 LLM 评估相关性并适配目标仓库的实现、接口与依赖；在 CodeFace 中组织 1,424 个经验证原语；提出 LEGO 框架：激活任务相关原语、将其适配实现与任务专用代码集成并解决跨组件约束、依据执行测试迭代修改；构建 LEGO-REPO 基准（522 个可执行重建任务、7 个领域、22 条能力轨道、5 个难度等级，按原生测试套件在空包下限与原源码上限之间评分）。

Result: 13 个被测基座中最佳交付分为 0.318，41.0% 任务得零分；LEGO 使 13 个基座平均提升 0.1474，将 GPT-5.6-terra 从 0.3180 提升至 0.5134（+61.4%）。受控对比中，适配原语优于以上下文提供的检索代码或原样引入的代码；该效果在独立仓库智能体、三个外部基准及重新挖掘的不相交 CodeFace 上依然成立；使用 GPT-OSS-20B 做适配与诊断可保留 95.1% 的同构得分，成本降低 24.0%。

Conclusion: 以智能体原生可复用原语为核心的仓库构建范式能显著提升仓库级代码生成的成功率与可执行性，优于检索式上下文与直接复用方案，且具备跨模型、跨基准的稳健性与成本效率。

Abstract: Large language models equipped with development environments have moved code generation toward repository-scale construction, yet building complete repositories remains difficult because interacting modules, interfaces, configurations, tests, and dependencies must work together. We introduce Code Primitives, agent-native reusable executable components with interface contracts, dependency closures, validation tests, and provenance. Each primitive uses a resident LLM to assess relevance and adapt its implementation, interfaces, and dependencies to the target repository, and we organize 1,424 validated primitives in CodeFace, a searchable library for repository construction. We introduce LEGO (Large-scale repository Engineering via aGent-native reusable cOde primitives), which activates task-relevant primitives, integrates their adapted implementations with task-specific code while resolving cross-component constraints, and revises the result against executed tests. To measure construction end to end, we build LEGO-REPO, a benchmark of 522 executable reconstruction tasks spanning seven software domains, 22 capability tracks, and five difficulty levels, scored against native test suites between an empty-package floor and original-source ceiling. The strongest of 13 evaluated backbones reaches a delivery score of 0.318 and scores zero on 41.0% of tasks; LEGO improves all 13 by 0.1474 on average and raises GPT-5.6-terra from 0.3180 to 0.5134 (+61.4%). In controlled comparisons, adapted primitives outperform retrieved code supplied as context or vendored unchanged. The effect persists against independent repository agents, across three external benchmarks, and with a disjointly re-mined CodeFace; GPT-OSS-20B for adaptation and diagnosis retains 95.1% of the homogeneous score at 24.0% lower cost.

</details>


### [225] [Who Broke Me? Execution-Guided Repair of Behavioral Dependency Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09267)
*Juntao Chen,Tingting Bi,Yanlin Wang,Patanamon Thongtanunam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出BBCFixer，通过运行失败测试、对比新旧库版本返回值差异来定位根API，再据此过滤库diff为LLM提供上游证据，修复行为性破坏变更。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖升级即使不改变库接口也可能破坏下游项目，即行为性破坏变更。这类问题难以修复：失败测试往往不指向导致破坏的根API；现有基于LLM的修复方法依赖编译器反馈或库文档获取证据，但行为性破坏既无编译器反馈也常无文档记录，导致代理难以自行检索上游证据，多数修复失败未能识别根API。

Method: BBCFixer：在旧版和新版库下运行失败测试，对返回值不同的调用进行排序以识别候选根API，再用该候选过滤库diff，从而为LLM代理提供相关的上游证据。同时构建BBCBench基准，包含Python和JavaScript中的100个行为性依赖破坏案例。

Result: 在BBCBench上，BBCFixer相比Pure Agent（无升级证据的代理）平均通过率提升最高达17%，每次成功修复所需代理步数减少最高达27%；并与通过挖掘库文档的BDUpdater方法进行对比。

Conclusion: BBCFixer提供了一种定位行为性破坏背后上游证据的方法，使LLM代理能更有效且更高效地修复行为性破坏变更。

Abstract: Dependency upgrades can break downstream projects without changing the library interface. Such behavioral breaking changes are difficult for developers to fix, because the failing test does not always point to the root API, the upstream API that causes the break. Existing LLM-based repair methods obtain evidence for the repair from compiler feedback or library documentation. However, a behavioral break produces no compiler feedback and is often undocumented. Agents that receive no upgrade evidence also usually do not retrieve upstream evidence themselves, and most of their failed repairs do not identify the root API.
  The library diff provides useful upstream evidence, but the root API must first be identified to select the relevant part of the diff. We present BBCFixer, a repair method that runs the failing test under the old and new library versions, ranks the calls whose return value differs to identify a candidate root API, and filters the library diff with that candidate. To evaluate repairs of behavioral breaks, we further introduce BBCBench, a benchmark of 100 behavioral dependency breaks in Python and JavaScript.
  We compare BBCFixer on BBCBench with two baselines: (1) Pure Agent, an agent that receives no upgrade evidence, and (2) BDUpdater, a method that mines library documentation. BBCFixer raises the average pass rate by up to 17% relative to Pure Agent and needs up to 27% fewer agent steps per successful repair. BBCFixer thus provides a way to select the upstream evidence behind a behavioral break, so that an LLM agent can repair behavioral breaking changes more effectively and efficiently.

</details>


### [226] [Beyond FAIR: A Fitness Function Framework for Sustainable Research Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09580)
*Irdin Pekaric,Philipp Zech*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在既有面向FAIR的可持续科研软件适应度函数框架基础上，新增环境与安全两组适应度函数，将科研软件可持续性扩展为包含FAIR合规、环境责任与长期安全的多维属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研软件可持续性常以FAIR原则评估，但FAIR未覆盖全部可持续性关切。软件还应具备环境责任与长期安全性：过度资源消耗会提高环境成本，不安全软件会带来维护负担、技术债务并阻碍复用。因此需要将评估范围从FAIR合规扩展到更完整的可持续性视角。

Method: 扩展此前提出的可持续科研软件适应度函数框架，使其超越FAIR。新增两组适应度函数：一是面向资源效率与执行足迹的环境函数；二是面向依赖健康、漏洞暴露与安全配置的安全函数。

Result: 新增的环境与安全适应度函数与原有FAIR相关函数共同构成扩展框架，使持续软件评估能够从FAIR合规扩展到环境责任与长期安全等维度。

Conclusion: 该框架将科研软件可持续性视为包含FAIR、环境责任与长期安全的多维属性，从而实现对科研软件更全面的持续评估。

Abstract: Research software sustainability is often assessed through the FAIR principles: findability, accessibility, interoperability and reusability. While important, FAIR does not cover all relevant sustainability concerns. Research software should also be environmentally responsible and secure over time. Excessive resource consumption increases environmental cost, while insecure software creates maintenance overhead, technical debt and barriers to reuse. To this end, in this paper, we extend a previously proposed fitness function framework for sustainable research software beyond FAIR. We introduce two additional sets of fitness functions: environmental functions targeting resource efficiency and execution footprint, and security functions targeting dependency health, vulnerability exposure and secure configuration. Together, these functions broaden continuous software assessment from FAIR compliance to a more complete view of sustainability. The resulting framework treats research software sustainability as a multidimensional property that includes FAIR, environmental responsibility and long-term security.

</details>


### [227] [GRAML: Graph-Grounded Reasoning and Multi-Task Learning for LLM-Based Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09605)
*Ruohan Li,Miaoqing Tian,Haipeng Qu,Ruobing Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GRAML 提出结合图证据、漏洞描述生成与多任务训练，提升 LLM 漏洞检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在软件漏洞检测中表现受限，主要因控制流与数据流信息利用不足。

Method: 对 C/C++ 程序做静态分析，提取关键源码行与带类型行关系作为结构证据；用该证据引导 GPT-5 通过 ToT-VR 生成漏洞描述；再与检测、定位、评估样本构建统一四任务训练数据集。

Result: 在 1 个分布内测试集和 6 个分布外数据集上，F1 平均达 66.67%–68.70%，超越最优基线最高 30.92%；消融显示 ToT-VR 与图引导描述优于标准 CoT 和原始 CPG 序列化。

Conclusion: 为构建更可靠、更安全的 LLM 软件工程系统提供实践指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely applied to software vulnerability detection. However, their performance is often limited by insufficient use of control-flow and data-flow information. In this paper, we propose GRAML, a framework that combines graph evidence, vulnerability description generation, and multi-task training. GRAML first performs static analysis on C/C++ programs to extract critical source lines and typed line relations as structural evidence. It then uses this evidence to guide GPT-5 through the Tree-of-Thought-guided Vulnerability Reasoning (ToT-VR) process and generate vulnerability descriptions. These descriptions are further combined with Detection, Localization, and Assessment samples to build a unified four-task training dataset. We evaluate GRAML on an in-distribution (ID) test set and six out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets. The results show that GRAML achieves average F1 scores ranging from 66.67% to 68.70%, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines by up to 30.92%. Ablation experiments further show that ToT-VR and graph-guided vulnerability descriptions improve detection performance compared with standard Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning and raw Code Property Graph (CPG) serializations. These findings provide practical guidance for building more reliable and secure software engineering systems with large language models.

</details>


### [228] [A Chat Assistant for Software Exploration in a 3D Software Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09901)
*Malte Hansen,Karim Issa,Wilhelm Hasselbring*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个嵌入Exploviz 3D软件可视化工具中的聊天助手，基于大语言模型，支持用户通过自然语言提问并触发改变可视化的操作，并进行了包含十一名参与者的实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在探索性程序理解任务中，用户交互开销较大，需要一种更自然、低负担的方式来查询和操作软件系统的可视化。

Method: 基于大语言模型构建聊天助手，集成于Exploviz前端（使用CopilotKit库），将概率性LLM与确定性的、基于工具的操作相结合（类似MCP实现），并支持通过自然语言增删改可视化中的软件系统结构；随后开展十一人参与的实证实验，评估感知理解支持与触发的工具调用。

Result: 参与者认为助手生成的摘要和解释基本正确；对高亮实体、创建新配色主题等操作评价出高可用性；但开放式聊天辅助的软件重构结果好坏参半，表明需要更强的LLM约束机制和更好的用户反馈整合。

Conclusion: 该助手整体被认为可用，并在减少探索性程序理解任务中的交互开销方面具有前景；作者提供了使用视频和评估所用软件系统的复现包。

Abstract: We present a chat assistant for interactive software exploration, embedded in the 3D software visualization tool ExplorViz. The assistant builds upon current Large Language Models (LLMs) and enables users to ask questions about the currently visualized software system and trigger actions that change the visualization through natural language. We integrate the chat assistant in our ExplorViz frontend using the CopilotKit libraries such that probabilistic LLMs are combined with deterministic and tool-based actions similar to implementations using the Model Context Protocol (MCP). The chat assistant is also enabled to restructure the software system by adding, removing, or modifying parts of the software system in the visualization. An empirical experiment with eleven participants evaluated both perceived comprehension support and the tool calls that were triggered by the chat assistant. Participants rated the assistant's generated summaries and explanations as largely correct. They also reported high usability for actions like highlighting entities in the visualization and the creation of new color themes. In contrast, open-ended chat-assisted software restructuring in the visualization showed mixed results. This suggests a need for stronger guardrails for the employed LLM and better incorporation of user feedback. Overall, the assistant was perceived as usable and promising for reducing interaction overhead during exploratory program comprehension tasks. We provide a video presenting the chat assistant's use and a reproduction package of the software system that was used in our evaluation.

</details>


### [229] [Analysis and Visualization of the Linux Kernel's Software Evolution Using the City Metaphor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09910)
*Malte Hansen,Lukas Damerau,Moritz Bugla,David Moreno-Lumbreras,Wilhelm Hasselbring*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种使用软件可视化工具ExplorViz对Linux内核进行可视化的方法，采用3D城市隐喻展示其结构与代码演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核代码规模庞大（超4000万行），理解其内部结构和评估软件演化面临巨大挑战，需要有效的可视化手段。

Method: 通过自定义分析服务解析Linux Git仓库中的提交记录；Web前端采用3D城市隐喻可视化软件结构；为每个文件收集行数等指标并累加至目录和提交；计算出的指标可映射到建筑物尺寸或以热力图展示；提供多种可视化、搜索和过滤选项以支持交互式探索。

Result: 呈现了内核仓库中所有文件的可视化，以及对Linux内核中Rust文件代码演化的可视化分析。

Conclusion: 该可视化方法能够支持对Linux内核这样超大规模软件系统的结构理解与演化分析，具有交互式探索能力。

Abstract: The Linux kernel is one of the largest and longest-maintained open source projects in existence. With more than 40 million lines of code, understanding the kernel's internal structure and assessing its software evolution is a great challenge. In this paper, we present an approach to visualize the Linux kernel using our software visualization tool ExplorViz. We analyze commits from the Linux Git repository using a custom analysis service. The web-based frontend utilizes the 3D city metaphor for visualization of the software structure. Metrics such as the number of lines are collected for each file and are accumulated for directories and commits. Calculated metrics can be mapped to the building's dimensions or be displayed via a heat map. A wide range of visualization, search, and filter options enable the interactive exploration of the visualized data. We present both a visualization of all files in the kernel repository and a visual analysis of the code evolution of Rust files in the Linux kernel. Demo video: https://youtu.be/3ODnERqD8g0

</details>


### [230] [AgentTracer: Tracing Indirect Prompt Injection Attack through Fine-Grained Intention-Execution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09935)
*Zitong Yao,Jiangrong Wu,Yixi Lin,Anrui Huang,Luoyun Zhang,Yuhong Nan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentTracer，一种意图感知的追踪框架，将间接提示注入（IPI）视为任务意图漂移，通过重建工具调用间的隐式决策依赖来还原攻击链并定位注入源与注入点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体需与外部资源交互以完成复杂任务，因而面临间接提示注入风险。由于 IPI 在真实环境中难以防御，事后溯源变得至关重要，但现有方法主要捕捉显式控制流与数据流依赖，忽略了由恶意指令驱动的工具调用之间的隐式关系，导致难以完整重建攻击链。

Method: AgentTracer 将 IPI 视为任务意图漂移。它恢复工具调用之间的隐式决策依赖，构建意图驱动执行图，将分散的工具调用按任务意图连接；结合用户请求与操作知识库构建用户意图授权空间，识别意图漂移的工具调用；从受审计的异常工具调用出发，执行基于目标的剪枝与反向追踪，重建攻击链并定位注入源和注入点。

Result: 作者将 AgentDyn 与 InjecAgent 中成功 IPI 攻击的执行日志，与包含 1,800 个用户请求和 9,000 个无 IPI 后台工具调用的正常执行日志相结合进行评测。端到端实验中，AgentTracer 达到 94.17% 的注入点准确率和 93.56% 的路径精确率；对比实验中，其注入点准确率比现有方法提升 18% 至 54%。

Conclusion: AgentTracer 通过意图感知的追踪方法有效解决了 IPI 事后溯源中隐式依赖难以捕捉的问题，在含正常任务噪声的真实场景下仍能高精度定位注入点并重建攻击链，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents interact with external resources to complete complex user tasks, exposing them to indirect prompt injection (IPI), where malicious instructions redirect agents toward attacker-intended tasks. Since IPI is difficult to defend against in real-world environments, post-incident tracing is essential for locating the injection source and reconstructing the attack chain. However, existing tracing methods primarily capture explicit control-flow and data-flow dependencies, overlooking the implicit relationships among tool calls driven by the malicious instruction. These tool calls may lack explicit dependencies and be interleaved with legitimate operations, making complete attack-chain reconstruction difficult. In this paper, we present AgentTracer, an intent-aware tracing framework that treats IPI as task intent drift. AgentTracer recovers implicit decision dependencies among tool calls to construct an Intent-Driven Execution Graph that connects dispersed tool calls by task intent. It combines the user request with an operation knowledge base to construct a user intent authorization space and identify intent-drift tool calls. Starting from an anomalous tool call under audit, AgentTracer performs target-based pruning and backward tracing to reconstruct the attack chain and locate the injection source and injection point. To evaluate AgentTracer in the presence of noise from normal tasks, we combine execution logs constructed from successful IPI attacks in AgentDyn and InjecAgent with normal execution logs containing 1,800 user requests and 9,000 background tool calls without IPI. In end-to-end experiments, AgentTracer achieves 94.17 percent injection-point accuracy and 93.56 percent path precision. Comparative experiments show that AgentTracer improves injection-point accuracy over existing methods by 18 to 54 percent.

</details>


### [231] [Designing Collaborative AI-Driven Workflows for Scientific Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09995)
*Akash Dhruv,Max Knobbe,Pedro Machado,Anshu Dubey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文主张智能体AI系统应嵌入"人机协作团队"而非全自动模式：领域专家负责规格与计划，智能体在确定性编排下写代码，每阶段以数值对比与人工审核把关。以大型高能物理Fortran到C++翻译为案例，14次实验显示简单的"作者—审阅者"循环在成本约为多智能体方案三分之一的情况下完成了相近数量的文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI可承担软件工程与科学研究中的多种任务（代码编写、翻译、数据分析与可视化工作流）。但在科学计算场景下，真正的难点在于验证智能体生成的代码是否既正确又能被跨专业背景的团队成员理解，因此完全自动化并不可取。

Method: 提出人机协作的工作流：领域专家编写规格与计划，智能体在确定性编排模式（deterministic orchestration pattern）下生成目标代码；每个阶段结束都与参考代码进行数值比对，并须经人工审查批准后才能进入下一阶段。以将大型高能物理应用从Fortran翻译为C++为评测任务，在实验中变化编排器、设计模式与模型。

Result: 共进行十四次实验。采用带有强制限制的简单"作者—审阅者"循环，所完成的文件数量与多智能体工作流相当，而每文件成本约为后者的三分之一。

Conclusion: 在科学计算类代码生成中，结构简单、有人工把关且成本可控的协作工作流优于复杂多智能体自动化方案；智能体应作为团队协作的一部分，而非取代人的全自动工具。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence systems can carry out a broad range of tasks in software engineering and scientific research, from writing and translating code to running workflows for data analysis and visualization. In scientific computing, the difficulty is verifying that agent-generated code is both correct and understandable to teams whose members bring different areas of expertise. We therefore argue that these systems are best used within collaborative team structures rather than as full automation. In the workflows we propose, domain experts write the specification and plan, and agents operate under a deterministic orchestration pattern to write the target code. Each stage ends with a numerical comparison against the reference code and requires human review and approval before the next begins. We evaluate these workflows on the translation of a large high-energy physics application from Fortran to C++, running the same task under different orchestrators, design patterns, and models. Across fourteen experiments, a simple author--reviewer loop with enforced limits completed a comparable number of files to a multi-agent workflow at about one-third of the cost per file.

</details>


### [232] [AdaT$^2$: Adaptive Test Transformations for Black-Box Boundary Testing of Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10141)
*Liting Lin,Boxi Yu,Qinghua Xu,Yuzhong Zhang,Lionel Briand,Emir Muñoz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AdaT$^2$ 通过探索式对话提取策略条件的陈述，并结合语句与测试变换指令对生成边界测试，用自适应选择（新颖度+Bayes-UCB）提升测试质量与故障检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的对话代理必须遵守策略，而策略中的每个条件都在用户请求空间上划定边界，代理需在边界两侧表现不同。现有测试方法难以系统、有效地生成覆盖这些边界的测试，因此需要自动化的边界测试生成与选择方法。

Method: AdaT$^2$ 让LLM扮演用户在探索式对话中与代理交互，从代理回复中抽取语句；每条语句描述一个条件及其成立时的期望行为。方法包括：由单语句引导的普通测试，以及由“语句+测试变换指令”（如“省略一个必需输入”）引导的变换测试；指令可将测试移动到该语句边界的另一侧或另一边界。由于语句与指令组成的配对数量远超可执行数量且许多不适用，采用自适应配对选择：按新颖度选择语句，并用Bayes-UCB bandit算法选择指令，学习信号来自早期配对是否产生测试以及代理是否通过LLM评判。基准对每个边界的两侧分别计数，并区分代理提示/工具代码/知识库显式定义的边界与LLM从领域知识推断的边界。

Result: 在$\tau^3$-bench的四个领域中，AdaT$^2$生成的测试有62.7%到83.3%是有效的边界测试且期望行为被显式定义，每个领域均高于AgentEval的47.7%到68.1%。变换测试额外发现了普通测试遗漏的13到46个显式定义边界。作为回归测试，AdaT$^2$的测试套件在航空领域检测出全部8个植入策略故障，在零售领域检测出8个中的4个；而AgentEval的测试套件测试数量不到其三分之一，分别只检测出5个和2个。

Conclusion: AdaT$^2$通过从探索式对话中提取策略条件陈述，并用语句–变换指令配对与自适应选择（新颖度+Bayes-UCB）生成边界测试，显著提高了有效边界测试比例、覆盖被遗漏的显式边界，并增强了对策略故障的回归检测能力。

Abstract: Conversational agents based on large language models (LLMs) must comply with policies. Each condition in a policy draws a boundary between user requests, and the agent must behave differently on its two sides. We present AdaT$^2$, which extracts statements from the agent's replies in exploratory conversations with an LLM acting as the user, and uses the statements to guide boundary test generation. Each statement describes one condition and the behavior expected when the condition holds. Besides plain tests guided by single statements, AdaT$^2$ writes transformed tests guided by pairs of a statement and a test transformation instruction, such as "omit one required input". The instruction of a pair can move a test to the other side of the statement's boundary or to another boundary. The statements and instructions form far more pairs than a run can try, and many pairs are not applicable. Adaptive pair selection therefore chooses the statement of each pair by novelty and the instruction with the bandit algorithm Bayes-UCB, which learns from whether earlier pairs yielded a test and whether the agent passed it according to an LLM judge. Our benchmark counts the two sides of each boundary separately and distinguishes boundaries explicitly defined by the agent's prompt, tool code, or knowledge base from boundaries that the agent's LLM infers from domain knowledge. On four domains of $τ^3$-bench, 62.7% to 83.3% of AdaT$^2$'s tests are valid boundary tests whose expected behavior is explicitly defined, higher in every domain than AgentEval's (47.7% to 68.1%). Transformed tests add 13 to 46 explicitly defined boundaries that plain tests miss. As regression tests, AdaT$^2$'s test suites detect all eight seeded policy faults in the airline domain and four of eight in the retail domain, and AgentEval's test suites, with fewer than a third as many tests, detect five and two, respectively.

</details>


### [233] [Why Software Engineering Is Indispensable in the Age of Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10226)
*Alfonso Fuggetta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文论证：具备能力的 AI 编程代理兴起，反而使软件工程学科与软件工程师变得不可或缺，因为 LLM 的三个结构性属性造成结构性真空，必须由软件工程方法、领域知识、设计选择与流程选择四个知识杠杆填补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应"AI 是否会让软件工程学科过时、让软件工程师失业"这一争议性问题，指出当前 AI 辅助开发产出貌似合理却不可验证、不可信的软件，其缺失的基础正是软件工程与软件工程师。

Method: 以概念论证为主：从大语言模型的三个结构性属性——概率式生成（probabilistic generation）、不可知性（agnosticism）、语义无状态性（semantic statelessness）——出发，推导出无法通过训练消除的结构性真空，并提出填补该真空的四个知识杠杆。

Result: 论证指出单一训练手段无法消除该结构性真空；填补需要方法知识、领域知识、设计选择、流程选择四类知识杠杆，且四者都必须被固化为持久性工件（persistent artifacts）。

Conclusion: AI 不会使软件工程学科过时，也不会使软件工程师冗余；软件工程师需承担方法学家、中介者与守护者三重角色，成为 AI 辅助开发可信性的基础保障。

Abstract: Can AI make Software Engineering (SE) -- the discipline -- obsolete? And can it make software engineers -- the professionals -- redundant? This paper argues that the rise of capable AI coding agents makes SE and software engineers essential, not obsolete: the missing foundation without which AI-assisted development produces misleadingly plausible, unverifiable, and ultimately untrustworthy software. Three structural properties of large language models (probabilistic generation, agnosticism, and semantic statelessness) create a structural vacuum that no amount of training can eliminate. Filling it requires four knowledge levers: methodological knowledge, domain knowledge, design choices, and process choices. All four must be reified as persistent artifacts, and each requires the software engineer as methodologist, mediator, and custodian.

</details>


### [234] [TestGRAD: Evolving Test Suites via Failure Pattern Momentum for SWE-Agent Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10242)
*Pengfei He,Jiayuan Zhou,Shaowei Wang,Ruiqi Pan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TestGRAD将集成补丁选择形式化为测试空间优化，通过差分损失、全CRUD梯度与失败模式动量实现自动测试优化，在SWE-bench Verified上以4智能体集成达到84.2% Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-agent集成通过组合不同智能体的互补补丁提升问题解决率，核心难点在于基于测试的选择：需生成测试、执行候选补丁并挑选最优补丁。然而现有测试生成方法优化能力有限，缺乏针对集成选择的显式损失，优化方向不完整（仅新建或删除测试），且为一次性生成，无法利用重复失败的反馈。

Method: 受带动量梯度下降启发，提出TestGRAD自动测试优化框架，包含三个核心概念：差分损失（differential loss）以执行定义显式目标，使有用测试能按行为区分候选补丁；全CRUD梯度（full CRUD gradients）将更新方向从仅创建/删除扩展到读取现有测试基础设施、新建测试、更新过时断言、仅删除废弃测试；失败模式动量（Failure Pattern Momentum）从记忆中挖掘高频失败序列，避免重复的非判别性方向并压缩失败历史上下文。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，TestGRAD使用4智能体集成取得84.2% Pass@1，较最强基线（80.6%）绝对提升3.6个百分点，同时将失败历史上下文压缩超过100倍。

Conclusion: 将测试选择建模为测试空间优化，并以差分损失、全CRUD梯度与失败模式动量构建优化器，可显著提升集成补丁选择效果并大幅降低上下文开销，为SWE智能体集成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: SWE-agent ensembles improve issue resolution by combining candidate patches from different agents with complementary strengths. The central problem is therefore test-based selection: generate tests, execute candidate patches, and identify the best patch. We formulate this process as test-space optimization: evolving an executable repository test suite until it distinguishes competing patches. Existing test-generation methods are limited optimizers. They usually lack an explicit loss for ensemble selection, optimize through incomplete directions that mostly create new tests or delete old ones, and perform one-off generation without feedback from repeated failures. Inspired by gradient descent with momentum, we introduce TestGRAD, a framework for automatic test optimization. TestGRAD centers on three concepts. Differential loss gives the optimizer an explicit execution-defined target: useful tests should separate candidate patches by behavior. Full CRUD gradients expand the update direction from merely creating or deleting tests to reading existing test infrastructure, creating new tests, updating stale assertions, and deleting only obsolete tests. Failure Pattern Momentum mines frequent failure sequences from memory, allowing the optimizer to avoid repeated non-discriminative directions while compressing the failure-history context. On SWE-bench Verified, TestGRAD achieves 84.2% Pass@1 with a 4-agent ensemble, outperforming the strongest baseline (80.6%) by an absolute improvement of 3.6 percentage points, while compressing failure-history context by over $100\times$.

</details>


### [235] [QuSema: Detecting Silent Bugs in Quantum Libraries via Quantum-knowledge-enhanced Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10258)
*Yujin Song,Kaining Zhang,Qixin Zhang,Shuai Wang,Pingchuan Ma,Yuxuan Du*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 QuSema，一个面向量子库的自主测试智能体，利用量子语义与文档约束作为源码级语义预言机，检测不会崩溃的"静默缺陷"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子库已成为量子算法开发的关键基础设施，但现有测试方法主要依赖失败型或对比型预言机（执行失败、违反运行时检查或与其他实现不一致时才暴露缺陷）。当缺乏合适的执行型预言机时，这类方法失效，静默缺陷被漏检，进而污染实验结论、仿真研究与算法设计。

Method: QuSema 以量子语义与文档中的约束构成源码级语义预言机，判断实现逻辑是否会把合法输入映射为非法输出。它运行在智能体循环中：反复查看库的 API 文档与源代码，推理量子操作的预期行为，识别潜在语义偏差，并通过库 API 生成可执行测试进行验证；借助量子领域推理把高层次行为不一致转化为可被用户触发的具体缺陷报告。

Result: 在 Qiskit 与 PennyLane 上实现。在包含 20 个历史静默缺陷的基准上，平均缺陷复现（relocation）数量高于 Claude Code 与 Codex，其中 DeepSeek 配置的成本低于 Claude Code；此外还发现 40 个开发者确认的新缺陷，其中 30 个为静默缺陷。

Conclusion: 基于量子语义的源码级语义预言机与智能体循环，可有效弥补执行型预言机的空白，发现现有工具难以捕获的静默缺陷，并具备较高的性价比与实用价值。

Abstract: Quantum libraries are now critical infrastructure for quantum algorithm development, yet their correctness remains difficult to test. Existing testing techniques mainly rely on failure-based or comparison-based oracles, exposing bugs only when executions fail, violate runtime checks, or disagree with another implementation. Their applicability is limited when suitable execution-based oracles are unavailable, leaving some silent bugs undetected. Such missed bugs can produce incorrect results that propagate into experimental conclusions, simulation studies, and algorithmic designs. Here we present QuSema, an autonomous testing agent for finding silent bugs in quantum libraries. QuSema uses constraints from quantum semantics and documentation as a source-level semantic oracle to assess whether implementation logic can produce invalid outputs from valid inputs. It operates through an agentic loop that repeatedly inspects library API documentation and source code, reasons about the intended behavior of quantum operations, identifies potential semantic deviations, and validates them by generating executable tests through library APIs. Guided by quantum-domain reasoning, QuSema turns high-level behavioral mismatches into concrete, user-triggerable bug reports, enabling it to uncover non-crash defects. We implement QuSema for Qiskit and PennyLane. On a benchmark of 20 historical silent bugs, QuSema achieves higher mean bug relocation counts than Claude Code and Codex, with the DeepSeek configuration costing less than Claude Code. QuSema also discovers 40 previously unknown bugs confirmed by the developers, including 30 silent bugs.

</details>


### [236] [Using Small Language Models to Reverse-Engineer Machine Learning Pipelines Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10261)
*Nicolas Lacroix,Frederic Precioso,Mireille Blay-Fornarino,Sebastien Mosser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估小型语言模型（SLM）能否借助代码理解与分类能力，从源码中识别ML流水线阶段，以克服现有分类器在领域多样性上的局限，并更可靠地理解ML实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从源码中提取ML流水线各阶段（如数据预处理、建模）有助于理解ML实践，但ML算法与数据集的持续演化带来多样性，使该任务困难。现有方法要么依赖不可扩展的人工标注，要么使用无法恰当支持领域多样性的分类器，因而需要更可靠的方案。

Method: 采用验证性研究，基于两篇代表当前局限的参考工作。先用Cochran's Q检验比较多个SLM，再通过两个McNemar检验将表现最好的模型与参考研究对比；另用Cochran's Q检验考察分类法定义变化对SLM表现的影响；最后用拟合优度检验将SLM分类所得的ML实践洞见与既有研究结果进行比较。

Result: 第一，分类法的措辞显著影响分类性能；第二，表现最好的SLM取得良好结果，但并未超越其他分类器；第三，三种分类方法在探索数据科学家实践时得出显著不同的洞见，且效应量各异。

Conclusion: 现有分类方法的局限会偏倚我们对ML实践的理解。当前SLM在无需预先微调的情况下展现出潜力，但仍存在常见局限，同时推理成本高，制约其在大规模研究中的适用性。

Abstract: Context: Once defined a taxonomy of stages structuring Machine Learning (ML) pipelines (e.g. Data Preprocessing, Modeling...), extracting these stages from source code is key for better understanding ML practices. However, the diversity caused by the constant evolution of ML (e.g., algorithms, datasets) makes this task challenging. Existing approaches either rely on non-scalable manual labeling or on classifiers that do not properly support domain's diversity. These limitations call for more reliable solutions.
  Objective: We evaluate whether Small Language Models (SLMs) can leverage their code understanding and classification abilities to address these limitations, and enhance our understanding of practices in ML.
  Method: We conduct a confirmatory study based on two relevant reference works representing current limitations in the state-of-the-art. We first compare several SLMs using Cochran's Q test, then evaluate the best-performing model against reference studies via two McNemar's tests. An additional Cochran's Q test examines how taxonomy definition variations affect the SLM performance. Finally, goodness-of-fit tests compare ML practice insights from SLM classification with those from prior studies.
  Results: First, we found that the taxonomy wording significantly impacts classification performance. Second, the best performing SLM yielded good results, yet, without outperforming other classifiers. Third, the three classification methods led to significantly different insights, with varying effect sizes, when exploring practices of data scientists.
  Conclusions: Limitations of existing classification methods bias our understanding of ML practices. While current SLMs show promising results without prior fine-tuning, they still exhibit common limitations, in addition to inference high costs challenging their applicability in large-scale studies.

</details>


### [237] [When Sub-Agents Work in Parallel: The Promises and Pitfalls of Dynamic Concurrency in Long-Horizon Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10263)
*Han Li,HanHaoNing Li,Ziqian Jiang,Yiling Lou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对照实验研究前沿编码智能体（Codex、Claude Code、Kimi Code）在长周期开发任务中启用/禁用动态并发执行策略的效果，覆盖354个任务、2124次执行，识别出13种并发特有失效模式与28个可观察模式，并刻画其优势条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体正从有限软件工程任务走向长周期开发，动态并发有望扩展复杂开发任务的规模。短任务中模型能力主导结果，而长周期开发使编排（orchestration）成为任务完成的核心。现有工作多关注短任务失败或预定义多智能体工作流中的协作，对前沿智能体在不同任务复杂度下的动态并发缺乏认识。

Method: 将动态并发作为一种执行策略，对匹配的Codex、Claude Code和Kimi Code执行进行启用与禁用的受控对比。在354个任务、2124次执行上，覆盖不同任务复杂度与执行时长，评估端到端效果与调度行为，并通过匹配轨迹分析并发特有失效模式与可观察模式。

Result: 在354个任务、2124次执行中评估了动态并发的端到端效果与调度行为；识别出13种并发特有失效模式、28个可观察模式，并刻画了动态并发带来优势的条件。

Conclusion: 动态并发的收益取决于任务复杂度与执行时长的匹配：短任务中模型能力更关键，长周期开发中编排能力成为任务完成的核心；并发并非普遍有利，其优势存在特定适用条件。

Abstract: As coding agents advance from bounded software engineering tasks toward long horizon development, dynamic concurrency offers a promising way to scale complex development tasks. Under this policy, agents decide during execution whether and how to spawn concurrent sub-agents. Model capability largely determines outcomes on shorter tasks, whereas long horizon development makes orchestration central to task completion. Existing work, focused on coding agent failures on shorter tasks or collaboration in predefined multiagent workflows, offers little insight into dynamic concurrency in frontier agents across task complexity. We study dynamic concurrency as an execution policy through controlled comparisons of matched Codex, Claude Code, and Kimi Code executions with the policy enabled or disabled. Across 354 tasks and 2,124 executions spanning a range of task complexities and execution horizons, we evaluate its end to end effects and scheduling behavior, and analyze matched trajectories to characterize 13 concurrency specific failure modes, 28 observable patterns, and the conditions under which it provides an advantage.

</details>


### [238] [TaoD2C-Bench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Industrial UI Code Generation Beyond Visual Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10374)
*Chengwei Shi,Yunnong Chen,Tingting Zhou,Qiang Lu,Shiyu Yue,Xinyuan Hu,Jianfang Ru,Liuqing Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TaoD2C-Bench，用于评估多模态大模型在工业设计转代码（D2C）中生成满足实现需求 UI 代码的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型的关键挑战已从视觉识别转向约束感知的跨模态推理，而工业级设计转代码（D2C）正是这一挑战的典型场景：模型需结合设计图像与杂乱的图层元数据，推断组件与布局实现需求，并在目标代码库约束下实现。但现有评估在真实工业场景中仍不充分，故需填补此空白。

Method: 构建 TaoD2C 数据集，包含来自 17 个商业平台的 2,861 个生产设计，涵盖四类共 97,652 条专家标注：Component、Group、Alignment、Position，并区分必需约束与可允许的实现选择；基于此定义三个任务：端到端 UI 代码生成、需求推断、需求实现；评估八个 MLLM。

Result: 八个 MLLM 在生成满足实现需求的 UI 代码上存在显著差距，在需求推断与需求实现上呈现不同性能特征；且视觉重建能力并不必然意味着能生成满足需求的代码。

Conclusion: 发布 TaoD2C 以支持工业级 UI 代码生成研究。

Abstract: A key challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is moving beyond visual recognition to constraint-aware cross-modal reasoning. This involves combining visual cues with information from other modalities to understand elements' relationships under domain-specific rules. This challenge is acutely evident in industrial design-to-code (D2C), which converts user interface (UI) designs into code and requires MLLMs to connect design images with disorganized layer metadata, infer component and layout implementation requirements, and realize them in code under target-library constraints. However, these capabilities remain insufficiently evaluated in realistic industrial settings. To fill this gap, we present TaoD2C-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs' ability to generate UI code that satisfies implementation requirements in industrial applications. The TaoD2C dataset consists of 2,861 production designs from 17 commercial platforms with 97,652 expert annotations across four categories: Component, Group, Alignment, and Position. These annotations distinguish required constraints from permitted implementation choices. TaoD2C-Bench defines three tasks: end-to-end UI code generation, requirement inference, and requirement realization. Evaluating eight MLLMs reveals substantial gaps in generating UI code that satisfies implementation requirements, alongside distinct performance profiles in inference and realization. We further show that MLLMs' visual reconstruction ability does not necessarily imply an ability to generate code that meets these requirements. We release TaoD2C to support research on industrial UI code generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Adversarial RL for Port-Scan Evasion: Attacker Feature Visibility in Edge-Deployed IDS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08864)
*Logan Andrew North,Priya Sanjay Kaluskar,Shasi Kumar Ramachandran Prabhu,Peilong Li,Suman Saha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究针对部署在Raspberry Pi 3B+上的ML入侵检测系统的自适应端口扫描规避攻击，使用DQN对抗智能体在黑盒、灰盒、白盒特征可见性下学习规避策略。传统端口扫描检测率达91.1%–99.8%，但DQN规避率达61.9%–98.3%，且更高特征可见性并不单调提升规避效果，效果依赖于模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML-based IDS increasingly used in resource-constrained IoT, but robustness is often evaluated against static attacks rather than adversaries adapting to detection feedback. This paper investigates adaptive port-scan evasion against ML-based IDS deployed on a Raspberry Pi 3B+.

Method: 实现基于Zeek的实时IDS流水线，训练XGBoost、多层感知机（MLP）和1D卷积神经网络（1D-CNN）三种模型，使用TON_IoT遥测数据；采用Deep Q-Network（DQN）对抗智能体，在黑盒、灰盒、白盒三种特征可见性设置下学习探测时序、TCP标志和载荷大小的规避组合；并使用Double DQN对代表性条件进行抽查验证。

Result: 部署的IDS模型对常规端口扫描检测率为91.1%–99.8%，而DQN最后50回合规避率为61.9%–98.3%。更高特征可见性并未单调提升规避效果，且效果依赖模型：对XGBoost，黑盒智能体规避率为92.9%，灰盒和白盒分别为61.9%和76.9%；对1D-CNN，白盒访问下最脆弱，规避率达98.1%。Double DQN抽查中灰盒条件仍不稳定，无证据表明仅过估计偏差能解释所观察的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 有限的特征知识仍可实现对静态边缘部署IDS模型的有效自适应规避，这凸显了为IoT边缘环境设计更鲁棒防御的必要性。

Abstract: Machine learning-based intrusion detection systems (IDS) are increasingly used in resource-constrained Internet of Things (IoT) environments, yet their robustness is often evaluated against static attacks rather than adversaries that adapt to detection feedback. This paper investigates adaptive port-scan evasion against ML-based IDS models deployed on a Raspberry Pi 3B+. We implement a live Zeek-based IDS pipeline with XGBoost, a multi-layer perceptron, and a 1D convolutional neural network trained on TON_IoT telemetry, and use a Deep Q-Network (DQN) adversary to learn evasive combinations of probe timing, TCP flags, and payload size under black-box, gray-box, and white-box feature-visibility settings. Although the deployed IDS models detect conventional port scans at 91.1--99.8%, DQN final-50-episode evasion rates range from 61.9% to 98.3% across feature-visibility settings. Greater feature visibility does not monotonically improve evasion, and its effect is model-dependent: against XGBoost, the black-box agent achieves 92.9% evasion, compared with 61.9% and 76.9% for gray-box and white-box agents, respectively, whereas 1D-CNN is most vulnerable under white-box access at 98.1%. Because standard DQN can overestimate action values, we additionally spot-check representative conditions using Double DQN. The gray-box condition remains unstable in this check, providing no evidence that overestimation bias alone explains the observed instability. These results show that limited feature knowledge can still enable effective adaptive evasion against static edge-deployed IDS models, motivating more robust defenses for IoT edge environments.

</details>


### [240] [A Deployment-Aware Feasibility Framework for Machine Learning-Based IoT Intrusion Detection Across Edge, Fog, and Cloud Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08867)
*Shaker Nawasra,Munther Abualkibash*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对基于机器学习的物联网入侵检测方法进行了面向部署的定量分析，提出“部署可行性评分（DFS）”模型，将ML技术按算力、内存与时延需求映射到边缘、雾/网关、云三层架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网环境快速增长且高度异构，传统基于规则和签名的入侵检测系统存在根本性局限；既有综述多聚焦检测精度，忽视了真实架构下的部署约束。

Method: 从实验与系统级研究中提取代表性定量部署能力包络，构建结构化DFS模型，采用加权序数打分机制，依据计算需求、内存占用和时延敏感性将ML技术映射到不同架构层。

Result: 轻量级统计与线性模型最适合边缘部署；集成与聚类方法适配雾/网关环境；深度模型与优化驱动模型最适合云基础设施。

Conclusion: 通过定量支撑与结构化评估形式化部署可行性，为真实架构约束下选择入侵检测方案提供实用指导，支持更具部署意识的物联网安全设计。

Abstract: The rapid growth and heterogeneity of Internet of Things (IoT) environments have exposed fundamental limitations in traditional rule-based and signature-based intrusion detection systems. This paper presents a quantitative deployment-aware analysis of machine learning (ML)-based intrusion detection approaches across edge, fog/gateway, and cloud architectures. Unlike prior surveys that primarily emphasize detection accuracy, this work defines representative quantitative deployment capability envelopes extracted from experimental and system-level studies and introduces a structured Deployment Feasibility Score (DFS) model. The proposed framework maps ML techniques to architectural layers based on computational demand, memory footprint, and latency sensitivity using a weighted ordinal scoring mechanism. The analysis demonstrates that lightweight statistical and linear models are most suitable for edge deployment, ensemble and clustering-based methods align with fog/gateway environments, while deep and optimization-driven models are best suited for cloud infrastructures. By formalizing deployment feasibility through quantitative grounding and structured evaluation, this work provides practical guidance for selecting intrusion detection solutions under real-world architectural constraints, supporting more informed and deployment-conscious IoT security design.

</details>


### [241] [Visible-Spectrum Optical Covert Channels in Commodity Smart Lighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08868)
*David Noever*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 未改装的智能灯泡（Philips WiZ A19）可用极微小的颜色与亮度变化，把二进制数据隐蔽地传给相机接收端，短消息"hi"成功解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究智能灯泡发出的可见光能否作为隐蔽的数字数据传输信道，即在人眼难以察觉的微小变化下实现通信；这类联网设备本身产生携带信息的输出，存在被用作低速率隐蔽信道的可能。

Method: 使用未经改装的Philips WiZ A19智能灯泡，以相机为接收端。用程序将二进制数据编码为几乎相同的颜色与亮度变化（颜色覆盖1600万色阶，差异极小）。由于信号微弱，接收端周期性测量已知参考状态，以补偿相机漂移与房间背景光照变化。颜色法以感知色距E=0.5发送短消息"hi"；亮度法在最小非零整数差（调光设置56与54之间交替）下测试；并设置把两种二进制符号映射到同一亮度的对照实验。

Result: 颜色法成功以E=0.5发送"hi"，接收端恢复99%的比特且完整消息通过完整性校验；亮度法在最小非零整数差下仍可解码，两轮平均比特准确率96.2%；对照实验（两符号同亮度）准确率降至约随机水平。颜色实验的色差低于常规人眼颜色辨别阈值。

Conclusion: 普通智能灯泡的可见光输出无需硬件改造即可作为低速率数据信道。局限在于：相机颜色漂移会淹没微弱信号，除非传输中重新校准；环境光突变会干扰解码；更长消息需要更强的纠错。亮度法对应的感知极限尚未经实验确定。

Abstract: Smart light-emitting diode (LED) bulbs are networked devices that produce information-carrying output. This study asks whether that light can carry digital data through changes small enough to be difficult for a person in the room to notice. To explore this covert communication channel, we tested an unmodified smart bulb (Philips WiZ A19) using a camera as the receiver. Binary data were encoded programmatically in nearly matched color and brightness changes. Because the differences were deliberately small across 16 million color steps, the receiver periodically measured known reference states and used them to compensate for changes in the camera and background room illumination. The color method successfully transmitted the short message 'hi' using a color difference (E) of 0.5, a standard measure of perceptual color distance. The receiver recovered 99% of the transmitted bits, and the complete message passed an integrity check. The brightness method remained decodable at the smallest nonzero integer difference tested, alternating between dimming settings 56 and 54, with 96.2% mean bit accuracy across two runs. When both binary symbols were assigned to the same brightness in a control experiment, accuracy fell to approximately chance. These results show that the visible output of an ordinary smart bulb can serve as a low-rate data channel without hardware modification. Camera color drift can overwhelm small signals unless the receiver is recalibrated during transmission, sudden changes in ambient light can disrupt decoding, and longer messages require stronger error correction. The color experiment operated at a difference below a conventional threshold for human color discrimination. The corresponding perceptual limit for the brightness method has not yet been established experimentally.

</details>


### [242] [CredLeakBench: Evaluating Credential Leakage and Recovery in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08871)
*Rafid Ahmed,Joseph Fioresi,Mubarak Shah,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 CredLeak-Bench 基准，评估语言模型代理在钓鱼攻击下的信息泄露与正常任务效用的权衡，发现所有被测模型均存在泄露风险，且现有防御难以兼顾安全与效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型代理被广泛用于自动化日常数字事务（如邮件、社交媒体、银行与账单管理），其可脱离用户监督运行，这使敏感信息暴露于钓鱼攻击风险。安全执行需区分恶意与合法请求而非简单拒绝。但该问题研究不足，尚不清楚现有代理或防御手段能否实现这一目标。

Method: 提出 CredLeak-Bench 综合基准，评估代理在钓鱼与身份验证情境下自动化工作流的安全性与有效性。基准覆盖用户指令式认证与自主收件箱监控（无显式登录指令）两类场景；系统性地变化欺骗线索，将钓鱼场景与合法对应场景配对，从而联合评估信息泄露与正常任务效用。在沙箱环境中，泄露以实际信息提交而非代理自我报告的行为来度量。

Result: 所有被测模型均易受泄露影响；在自主收件箱监控中代理也会披露敏感信息，表明无需用户请求认证即可由钓鱼诱发披露；多数可减少泄露的缓解措施同时损害正常任务表现，暴露出安全与效用的权衡。

Conclusion: 仅减少泄露并不足够，有效防御必须在防止未授权披露的同时保持合法任务完成。CredLeak-Bench 提供了同时度量这两个目标的受控框架，用于评估迈向安全且有用代理的进展。

Abstract: Language model agents are increasingly deployed to automate everyday digital chores from managing emails and social media to handling banking and bills allowing users to step away from supervision. However, this capability also exposes sensitive information to phishing. Safe execution requires distinguishing malicious requests from genuine ones without simply refusing to act. Despite its practical importance, this problem remains underexplored and it is unclear whether current agents or existing defenses can achieve it. To study this problem, we first propose CredLeak-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate how effectively and securely agents automate human workflows when confronted with phishing and identity verification. The benchmark covers both user-directed authentication and autonomous inbox monitoring, where agents are not explicitly instructed to log in. It systematically varies deceptive cues and pairs phishing scenarios with legitimate counterparts, enabling joint evaluation of information leakage and utility on genuine tasks. Within a sandboxed environment, leakage is measured through actual submissions of information rather than agents' self-reported behavior. Our evaluation reveals that all tested models are vulnerable to leakage. Agents also disclose sensitive information during autonomous inbox monitoring, demonstrating that phishing can induce disclosure without a user request to authenticate. Furthermore, most evaluated mitigations that reduce leakage also impair performance on genuine tasks, exposing a security utility trade off in existing defenses. These findings show why reducing leakage alone is insufficient: effective defenses must prevent unauthorized disclosure while preserving legitimate task completion. CredLeak-Bench provides a controlled framework for measuring both objectives and evaluating progress toward secure, useful agents.

</details>


### [243] [Towards Verifying Neural Networks Against Multi-Parameter Bit-Flip Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08876)
*Hai Duong,Thanh Le,Ho Nguyen,ThanhVu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: mBFV是一个高效验证框架，可在不显式枚举组合的情况下证明量化神经网络对多参数同时位翻转的鲁棒性，并成功扩展到115万参数网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件故障会翻转量化神经网络存储权重中的比特，可能破坏其预测。此类故障通常同时影响多个参数，但现有验证器因大型网络中翻转位置组合爆炸，仅能处理单参数扰动。

Method: mBFV采用一种新颖的多参数边界传播技术，直接聚合m个最坏情况贡献；并引入针对扰动位置的分支定界机制，将潜在翻转划分为更小的神经元组以收紧边界。

Result: 在625个实例上，mBFV成功验证293个，显著优于单参数验证器（38个）和精确混合整数线性规划基线（0个）。基线仅限小网络（最多1.3万参数）的单参数翻转，而mBFV可验证最多115万参数网络、最多四个参数同时翻转。

Conclusion: mBFV通过边界传播与分支定界，在不枚举组合的前提下实现了对多参数同时位翻转的高效鲁棒性验证，并能扩展到大规模网络。

Abstract: Hardware faults can flip bits in the stored weights of a quantized neural network, potentially compromising its predictions. While such faults typically affect multiple parameters simultaneously, existing verifiers are limited to single-parameter perturbations due to the combinatorial explosion of possible flip locations in large networks. We present mBFV (m-BitFlip Verifier), an efficient verification framework that proves robustness against simultaneous bit flips across multiple parameters without explicitly enumerating these combinations. mBFV achieves this via a novel multi-parameter bound propagation technique that directly aggregates the m worst-case contributions. To further tighten these bounds, mBFV employs a branch-and-bound mechanism over perturbation locations, partitioning the potential flips to smaller groups of neurons. Evaluated on 625 instances, mBFV successfully verifies 293, significantly outperforming a prior single-parameter verifier (38 verified instances) and an exact mixed-integer linear programming baseline (0 verified instances). Notably, while these baselines are restricted to single-parameter flips on small networks (up to 13k parameters), mBFV scales to verify networks with up to 1.15M parameters against up to four simultaneous parameter flips.

</details>


### [244] [Contextualization of Third-Party Cloud Security Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08895)
*Leon Goldberg,Gal Engelberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"情境化"方法，用深度研究智能体在跨信号资产图上对第三方云安全发现重新推导严重性，并在8个真实生产环境的9967条厂商HIGH发现上验证：约四分之三被重新评级且多为下调，约一半决定性证据在标记资源之外，实时探测证实99.4%决定性事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方云安全发现的严重性由规则静态指定，反映的是通用风险而非具体环境中的实际风险。评分标准本身规定环境上下文应纳入，但该上下文在生产中改变严重性的幅度、决定性证据所在位置、以及是否能经受实时环境检验，此前均未被测量。

Method: 提出情境化方法（contextualization）：基于预计算的跨信号资产图，使用深度研究智能体针对发现的资源状态、其图邻域以及其他产品的信号进行调查，返回带证据追踪的调整后严重性。在两家商业云安全平台的9967条厂商HIGH发现、8个真实生产环境上做生产实地研究，按三项标准评估：判决背后事实对实时环境的忠实度、决策对标记资源之外上下文的依赖程度、推理的规律性。

Result: 四分之三的发现被重新评级，且多为下调；同一规则常在同一环境内呈现相反方向。约一半决定性证据位于标记资源之外。只读的实时基础设施探测对99.4%的已判决发现确认了决定性事实。

Conclusion: 静态严重性无法反映环境特定的真实风险，情境化能显著改变评级并与实时环境保持高度一致，为安全团队基于环境证据进行修复优先级排序提供了可验证的依据。

Abstract: Finding severity is the main driver of how security teams prioritize remediation.
