For third-party cloud security findings, that severity is static: the rule that raised the finding assigns it before the rule meets any environment, so it reflects the risk of the condition in general rather than the risk the finding poses to the concrete environment where it lives. Scoring standards define where environment-specific context belongs. How far that context changes finding severities in production, where the deciding evidence lies, and whether it holds against the live environment have not been measured. We address this gap with contextualization, re-deriving each finding's severity from evidence in the environment where the finding lives. A deep research agent over a precomputed cross-signal asset graph investigates each finding against the resource's state, its graph neighborhood, and other products' signals, and returns an adjusted severity with an evidence trace. We evaluate it in a production field study of 9,967 vendor HIGH findings from two commercial cloud security platforms across eight real production environments, on three criteria: the faithfulness of the facts behind each verdict to the live environment, the dependence of each decision on context beyond the flagged resource, and the regularity of the reasoning. Three in four findings are re-graded, mostly downward, and the same rule often moves in opposite directions inside a single environment. About half of the decisive evidence lies beyond the flagged resource, and read-only probes of live infrastructure confirm the decisive fact for 99.4% of decided findings.

</details>


### [245] [Multi-Aspect Runtime Verification for Simulation-Based V&V of LLM-Enabled Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08928)
*Nikolaos Kekatos,Dimitrios Nikou,Anastasios Temperekidis,Alexios Lekidis,Nikolaos Kolokotronis,Panagiotis Katsaros,Stylianos Basagiannis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种多层面运行时验证框架，将自然语言政策条款分解为空间/时间/语义三元组，在统一事件流上分别监控并通过四值代数融合，实现组合级策略合规检查，攻击成功率降为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在国防参谋等决策支持场景中承担角色，其须遵守的义务已成文且具约束力，而模型作为采购组件无法通过再训练来控制。可施加工程控制的只有智能体与其操作对象之间的接口。这些义务兼具空间、时间和文本语义属性，且违规通常存在于多步交互的组合之中，因此逐事件的护栏会漏掉顺序型工具攻击链。

Method: 提出多层面运行时验证框架：将一个自然语言政策条款分解为同一规范事件流上的类型化空间/时间/语义三元组；每个层面用各自监控规约检查；通过带溯源的思维代数融合判定。空间层面在加权双类别位置图上解释（任务几何与信息发布拓扑视为同一对象）；过去时间层面运行于未修改的MonPoly引擎上。

Result: 在伤亡后送与争议补给两个任务域及一个民用域中，采用预防性阻断策略下的组合将攻击成功率降至零，未观察到误报，单事件成本为微秒级；而任一单层面或任一双层面组合都仍有相当比例的攻击得逞。闭环实验中，策略朴素规划器在多数无防护任务中抵达违规状态，而加防护后无一发生；五分之四的被拒回合仍能恢复至合规结果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明空间义务一般不为一阶时间规约所涵盖；组合式多层面运行时验证能有效实现顺序攻击链的防护，在多个任务域中达到零攻击成功率且无假阳性，并具备微秒级实时代价。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are entering decision-support roles in defence staff work, where the obligations they must respect are already written down and binding, and where retraining is not available as a control because models arrive as procured components. What can be placed under engineering control is the interface between the agent and the systems it acts on. Those obligations are at once spatial, temporal and text-semantic, and a violation typically lives in the composition of a multi-step interaction, which is why per-event guardrails miss sequential tool-attack chains. We present a multi-aspect runtime-verification framework that decomposes a natural-language policy clause into a typed spatial/temporal/semantic triple over one canonical event stream, checks each aspect with its own monitoring specification, and fuses the verdicts through a four-valued algebra that carries provenance. The spatial aspect is interpreted over a weighted two-sorted location graph in which mission geometry and information-release topology are one object; we show that these spatial obligations are not in general subsumed by a first-order temporal specification. The past-time aspect runs on the unmodified MonPoly engine, which agrees with our reference monitor at every time point. Across two mission domains, casualty evacuation and contested sustainment, and one civil domain, composition under the precautionary blocking policy drives attack success to zero with no observed false positives and microsecond-scale per-event cost, while every single aspect and every pair leaves a substantial share of attacks succeeding. In a closed-loop experiment a policy-naive planner reaches a violating state in most unshielded missions and in none when shielded, and four refused episodes in five still recover to a compliant outcome.

</details>


### [246] [ASPIRE: Agentic Safety & Prompt Injection Red-teaming Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08951)
*Pengfei He,Deep Mitra,Vishesh Sharma,Jiliang Tang,Vinay S Rao,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 ASPIRE，一个面向 LLM agent 的开放式行为级红队测试引擎，通过演化式安全行为图与探索/利用双专家机制，发现并泛化间接提示注入漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 会检索不可信内容并通过工具执行操作，带来间接提示注入风险，可能导致未授权操作或持久性状态改变。现有自动化红队方法多针对预设场景优化攻击载荷，无法覆盖 agent 行为空间中的潜在漏洞。

Method: ASPIRE 维护一个持续演化的 Agent 安全行为图，使用互补的探索专家与利用专家来发现、验证并泛化以后果为中心的测试用例；利用轨迹证据更新行为图，并对部分成功或失败的尝试进行诊断；同时通过跨运行记忆迁移有效的红队策略。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，ASPIRE 在后果类型、注入方法、环境和行为路径等维度上显著扩大了覆盖范围，同时保持了较高的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: ASPIRE 将红队测试从预设场景的载荷优化推进到开放式、行为级的漏洞发现，能够更全面地揭示 LLM agent 的间接提示注入安全隐患。

Abstract: LLM agents retrieve untrusted content and act through tools, creating indirect prompt-injection risks that can cause unauthorized actions or persistent state changes. Existing automated red-teaming largely optimizes payloads for pre-specified scenarios, leaving latent vulnerabilities across the agent's behavior space unexplored. We present ASPIRE, an Agentic Safety & Prompt Injection Red-teaming Engine for open-ended, behavior-level vulnerability discovery. ASPIRE maintains an evolving Agent Security Behavior Graph and uses complementary Explore and Exploit experts to discover, verify, and generalize consequence-centric tests. Trajectory evidence updates the graph and diagnoses partial or failed attempts, while cross-run memory transfers useful red-team strategies. Experiments on various benchmarks show that ASPIRE substantially expands coverage across consequences, injection methods, environments, and behavior paths while maintaining strong attack success.

</details>


### [247] [TwinGuard-Lite: A Rule-Based State-Admission Gateway for Generative Patient Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09012)
*Wenhui Chu,Sheikh Rabiul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TwinGuard-Lite是一种基于规则的网关，仅在更新通过两项完整性属性的可检验近似检查时，才允许其写入患者数字孪生的持久状态，实验显示融合GSC与CPN后达到99.1%精确率、90.5%召回率和94.6% F1值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来生成式患者数字孪生可能结合纵向病历与语言模型智能体并跨会话保存信息，但拟议更新的措辞无法揭示其来源是否可信、是否与患者记录冲突或是否属于他人，因此需要一种机制层面的防护手段。

Method: 提出TwinGuard-Lite规则式网关：用溯源检查与矛盾检查近似"接地状态一致性"（GSC），用基于可信传输元数据的命名空间检查近似"跨患者非干扰"（CPN）。在基于开放许可eICU数据库演示构建的半合成数据流上，用20个带种子的个体级划分评估三类攻击，每种子约29,131±2706条候选更新。

Result: 关键词过滤与异常检测分别检出16%和24%的攻击；仅溯源消融检出59.9%但漏掉全部跨患者攻击。GSC与CPN结合达到99.1%精确率、90.5%召回率、94.6% F1值和0.033%假阳性率。完整网关误纳的每次攻击都谎称可信来源；当检索通道如此声称时，92.9%的检索攻击被纳入。

Conclusion: 结果在所述信任假设下成立，且未执行任何语言模型、智能体或检索器；TwinGuard-Lite是机制层面的概念验证，用于推动经认证、绑定患者的摄取机制，而非临床安全保障。

Abstract: Future generative patient digital twins may combine longitudinal health records with language-model agents and keep information across sessions. The wording of a proposed update does not reveal whether it comes from an allowed source, conflicts with the patient's record, or belongs to someone else. We present TwinGuard-Lite, a rule-based gateway that admits an update to a twin's persistent state only if it passes checkable approximations of two integrity properties. Grounded state consistency (GSC) is approximated by a provenance check and a contradiction check; cross-patient noninterference (CPN) is approximated by a namespace check against trusted transport metadata. We evaluate three attack families over 20 seeded person-level splits of a semi-synthetic stream built from the openly licensed eICU database demo, with 29,131 $\pm$ 2706 candidate updates per seed. A keyword filter and an anomaly detector detect 16% and 24% of attacks, respectively; a provenance-only ablation detects 59.9% but misses every cross-patient attack. Combining GSC and CPN yields 99.1% precision, 90.5% recall, a 94.6% F1 score, and a 0.033% false-positive rate. Every attack the full gateway admits falsely claims a trusted source; when the retrieval channel does so, 92.9% of retrieval attacks are admitted. The results hold under stated trust assumptions, and no language model, agent, or retriever is executed: TwinGuard-Lite is a mechanism-level proof of concept that motivates authenticated, patient-bound ingestion, not a clinical safeguard.

</details>


### [248] [Visual Memory Attacks Can Persist Through The KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09027)
*David Dobre,Leo Schwinn,Gauthier Gidel,Spandana Gella,Perouz Taslakian,Pierre-André Noël*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究对抗性输入在被移出上下文后能否继续影响语言模型，提出持久视觉记忆注入（P-VMI）攻击，证明对抗影响可通过KV缓存长期存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型系统在包含不可信文本和图像的日益增长的上下文中自主运行。核心问题是：对抗性输入即使从上下文中被移除，是否仍能继续操控模型？这一安全隐患在现有研究中尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 研究基于视觉记忆注入（VMI）攻击设定，即上下文中保留的对抗图像植入隐藏后门（模型正常表现，直到特定触发词激活攻击者预设的响应）。首先用优化软提示展示持久性——即使从注意力中屏蔽提示仍有效；随后提出P-VMI，优化图像使其在被屏蔽出注意力后仍保持对抗行为；并进行缓存交换消融实验以定位持久影响的来源。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，P-VMI最强配置下目标成功率约达90%；在更严格的移除设定下（图像仅在第一轮暴露）仍保持有效。缓存交换消融实验将持久影响定位到KV缓存。攻击的影响持续时间远超优化时使用的对话长度，并能存活于保留同一模型生成摘要KV缓存的压缩过程中。

Conclusion: 对抗行为可以在没有持续访问其来源的情况下驻留于缓存状态中，表明KV缓存成为对抗影响长期存活的载体，对长上下文自主语言模型系统构成重要安全威胁。

Abstract: Modern language model systems operate autonomously over increasingly long contexts containing untrusted text and images. Can an adversarial input continue to steer a model even after that input is removed from its context? We show that attacks can be trained to persist through the key/value (KV) cache of subsequent tokens, allowing adversarial influence to outlive direct access to its source.We consider the Visual Memory Injection (VMI; Schlarmann and Hein, 2026) attack setting, in which an adversarial image that stays in the context plants a hidden backdoor: the model behaves normally until a chosen trigger elicits an attacker-chosen response. We first demonstrate persistence in this setting with optimized soft prompts, which remain effective after we mask the prompt from attention. We then introduce Persistent Visual Memory Injection (P-VMI), which optimizes images to preserve this adversarial behaviour after they are masked from attention. These attacks persist over conversations substantially longer than those used during optimization. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, P-VMI achieves up to approximately $90\%$ target success in its strongest configuration and remains effective under a stricter removal setting that exposes the image only on the first turn. A cache-swap ablation localizes the persistent influence to the KV cache. Finally, we show that these attacks can be trained to survive compaction that retains the KV cache of a summary generated by the same model, demonstrating that adversarial behaviour can persist in cached state without continued access to its source.

</details>


### [249] [Cost of Delay for Post-Quantum Migration: Putting Classical and Harvest-Now-Decrypt-Later Risk on One Ordered List](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09029)
*Animesh Shaw*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"延迟成本"（cost of delay，货币/年）的统一度量，将后量子迁移风险与经典风险放在同一资产上比较，用于资产迁移排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构在决定哪些资产优先迁移到后量子密码时，缺乏共同单位：后量子分数是无量纲且仅针对量子威胁，而经典风险是年化损失，二者难以直接比较。

Method: 将两类威胁表示为同一资产上的"延迟成本"（货币/年）。量子项由所需保密时长、可记录流量占比和数据周转率计算，表征推迟迁移所承诺的不可逆"先存后解"损失，并采用对公开专家锚点闭式拟合的量子到来定律；引入竞争风险因子耦合两项以避免重复计数。证明了该构造不是经典分数与量子分数的加权和，给出与资产价值无关的经典风险优势阈值，并证明在恒定损失率下其诱导的排序对任务排程是最优的。通过仿真、自适应求积和第二实现进行验证。

Result: 在四个公开系统上（参数按公开文档假设），量子项仅对一个系统在超出输入不确定性范围内重排资产顺序（信噪比1.36，对比0.14–0.46），对特定长寿命、低活动、可记录的资产产生决定性影响，其余情况仅改变量级。资产价值和数据周转率解释了量子项不确定性的73–91%，到来日期解释3–17%。

Conclusion: 案例研究仅作示例说明，未与真实结果进行验证。

Abstract: Organisations deciding which assets to migrate to post-quantum cryptography first, and how that work competes with a backlog of classical findings, lack a common unit: post-quantum scores are dimensionless and quantum-only, while classical risk is annualised loss. We express both threats as a cost of delay in currency per year on the same asset. The quantum term is the rate at which deferring migration commits irreversible harvest-now-decrypt-later loss, computed from required confidentiality duration, recordable traffic share and data turnover, using a quantum-arrival law fitted in closed form to published expert anchors. A competing-risks factor couples the two terms so the same loss is not counted twice. We prove that the construction is not a weighted sum of a classical and a quantum score, give a dominance threshold on the classical hazard that is independent of asset value, and show that the induced order is optimal for sequencing work under constant loss rates. We verify the implementation by simulation, adaptive quadrature and a second implementation. On four public systems with parameters assumed from public documentation, the quantum term reorders the asset list beyond input uncertainty for one system only (signal-to-noise 1.36 against 0.14-0.46), moves specific long-lived, quiet, recordable assets decisively, and otherwise changes magnitudes. Asset value and turnover explain 73-91% of the uncertainty in the quantum term; the arrival date explains 3-17%. The case studies are illustrative and are not validated against outcomes.

</details>


### [250] [SwarmReconGuard: Black-Box Detection of Distributed Collective Reconnaissance by Individually Benign-Looking Agent Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09138)
*Vahid Tavakkoli,Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文将自主智能体分散式侦察威胁形式化为分布式集体侦察（DCR），提出可复现的黑盒基准 SwarmReconGuard，评估11种行为、10–10,000个虚拟身份、440次运行、366万余请求，比较多类检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主与智能体化客户端可将侦察任务分散到大量身份上，使单次请求保持合法、低速率且看似良性，而整体却获取广泛系统知识；现有防御缺乏对该威胁的正式定义与可复现评测基准。

Method: 提出 Distributed Collective Reconnaissance（DCR）威胁模型；构建 Docker 隔离的黑盒基准 SwarmReconGuard，防御方仅观测服务边界遥测；在10–10,000个虚拟身份下评测11种良性与攻击行为，共440次测试、3,666,300次请求，并对比语义、高斯、条件、图、核、混合及 CUSUM 类检测器。

Result: 高斯似然比检测对已知攻击达100%检测率、0%观测误报，但对未见策略仅3%；CUSUM 整体检测率36.1%、误报1.25%；混合 CUSUM 在10,000身份下达85.7%检测率与0%观测误报。

Conclusion: 结果揭示严重的策略泛化差距，表明需要面向暴露感知与规模感知的防御方法；SwarmReconGuard 为 DCR 研究提供了可复现的评测基础。

Abstract: Autonomous and agentic clients can distribute reconnaissance across many identities so that each request remains valid, low-rate, and benign-looking while the population collectively acquires broad system knowledge. We formalize this threat as Distributed Collective Reconnaissance (DCR) and present SwarmReconGuard, a reproducible black-box benchmark in which the defender observes only service-boundary telemetry. The Docker-isolated study evaluates 11 benign and attack behaviors across 10-10,000 virtual identities, comprising 440 test runs and 3,666,300 requests, with complete telemetry integrity. We compare semantic, Gaussian, conditional, graph, kernel, hybrid, and CUSUM-based detectors. Gaussian likelihood-ratio detection achieves 100$\%$ detection with 0$\%$ observed false positives on known attacks but only 3$\%$ on unseen policies. CUSUM yields 36.1$\%$ overall detection at 1.25$\%$ false positives, while hybrid CUSUM reaches 85.7$\%$ detection with 0$\%$ observed false positives at 10,000 identities. Results expose a major policy-generalization gap and motivate exposure-aware, scale-aware defenses.

</details>


### [251] [Adversarial Images Hijack Web Agents from Visual Grounding to Browser Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09240)
*Wanjing Han,Levi Taiji Li,Mu Zhang,Yue Jiang,Guanhong Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出WebMirage框架，将视觉网页智能体的红队测试建模为端到端的"感知到执行"问题，通过局部视觉扰动使智能体选择攻击者控制的内容并执行相应浏览器动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对基于大视觉语言模型的网页智能体的视觉红队方法主要针对模型推理，未显式考虑结构化输入处理与动作后处理。因此模型层面的攻击成功并不能保证对浏览器执行的控制，也无法可靠刻画智能体的端到端鲁棒性。

Method: 将视觉网页智能体的红队测试形式化为端到端 grounding-to-execution 问题，提出WebMirage框架：构造局部视觉扰动，使智能体在不同页面渲染下选择攻击者控制的内容并执行对应浏览器动作。采用角色-槽位抽象（role-slot abstraction）与网页重组（webpage recomposition）刻画网页元素间的竞争关系，并用数据流分析使优化与动作后处理对齐。

Result: 在四种智能体配置、六种VLM骨干模型上评估，覆盖13个公开网站和沙盒基准的2,250个任务。WebMirage平均攻击成功率达91.9%，而最强基线为17.4%，并且对三种智能体级防御仍然有效。

Conclusion: 通过端到端建模，WebMirage显著优于现有基线并能抵抗智能体级防御，表明仅针对模型推理的红队方法不足以刻画网页智能体的真实鲁棒性，需从感知到执行整体考量。

Abstract: Modern web agents built on large vision-language models process webpages, select relevant UI elements, and translate model outputs into browser actions. Existing visual red-teaming approaches use adversarial visual content to manipulate this process. However, they primarily target model inference and do not explicitly account for structured input processing or action post-processing. Consequently, model-level success does not establish control over browser execution and cannot reliably characterize end-to-end agent robustness. To address this gap, we formulate red teaming for vision-grounded web agents as an end-to-end grounding-to-execution problem, and introduce WebMirage, a framework that crafts localized visual perturbations that cause agents to select attacker-controlled content and execute the corresponding browser action across varying webpage renderings. It uses a role-slot abstraction and webpage recomposition to capture competition among webpage elements, and dataflow analysis to align optimization with action post-processing. We evaluate WebMirage across four agent configurations and six VLM backbones on 2,250 tasks covering 13 public websites and a sandbox benchmark. WebMirage achieves an average attack success rate of 91.9%, compared with 17.4% for the strongest baseline, and remains effective against three agent-level defenses.

</details>


### [252] [Package Hallucination Attacks on Coding Agents through Prompt Injection in Rule Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09264)
*Yupu Wang,Zhengyuan Jiang,Reachal Wang,Neil Zhenqiang Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对编码智能体的"包幻觉攻击"：攻击者通过在社区共享的规则文件（如AGENTS.md、.cursorrules）中注入恶意提示，诱导编码智能体将合法依赖替换为攻击者控制的包；为此设计了PackHallu进化优化框架，实验证明其攻击成功率高且迁移性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体编码框架愈发依赖社区共享的规则文件来指导自动代码生成，但这一流程的安全风险尚未被充分研究。作者希望揭示该管道的漏洞，指出编码智能体易受攻击，呼吁加强自主编码系统的安全防护。

Method: 提出"包幻觉攻击"：向良性规则文件中注入恶意提示，诱导编码智能体用攻击者控制的包替换合法依赖。为获得有效的恶意提示，设计PackHallu进化优化框架，利用轨迹级反馈与LLM引导的变异，对注入提示进行迭代改写。

Result: 在多个基准、多个LLM和多种智能体框架上的评估表明，PackHallu实现了较高的攻击成功率，并在不同模型与智能体组合间表现出较强的迁移性。

Conclusion: 编码智能体确实易受包幻觉攻击的威胁，这凸显了在自主编码系统中建立更强安全保障机制的紧迫性。

Abstract: Modern agentic coding frameworks increasingly rely on community-shared rule files (e.g., AGENTS.md or .cursorrules) to guide autonomous code generation, yet the security risks of this pipeline remain underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce the package hallucination attack, where an attacker injects malicious prompts into benign rule files to induce coding agents to replace legitimate dependencies with attacker-controlled packages. To obtain effective malicious prompts injected into rule files, we propose PackHallu, an evolutionary optimization framework that iteratively rewrites these injected prompts using trajectory-level feedback and LLM-guided mutations. Evaluations across multiple benchmarks, LLMs, and agent frameworks show that PackHallu achieves high attack success rates and strong transferability across diverse models and agent combinations. Our findings demonstrate that coding agents are vulnerable to package hallucination attacks, highlighting the urgent need for stronger security safeguards in autonomous coding systems.

</details>


### [253] [Ask the Expert: LLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09337)
*Fernando Martinez,Abhishek Satyam,Tao Li,Junaid Farooq,Ying Wang,Juntao Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Ask the Expert 框架，将大语言模型（LLM）用于训练阶段的奖励塑形，以提升自主网络防御中策略强化学习的样本效率，部署阶段则完全去除 LLM 依赖，仅保留纯 RL 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主网络防御中的策略强化学习方法虽具潜力，但在延迟、部分可观测以及大动作空间的场景下样本效率低下。LLM 虽能对安全状态空间进行语义推理，但高延迟与信任假设使其难以直接在线部署。因此需要一种兼顾 LLM 语义推理优势与 RL 部署效率的方案。

Method: 提出 Ask the Expert 训练期指导框架：首先对困难的网络防御状态进行总结，然后通过受约束的动作接口间歇性地向 LLM 查询主机级防御建议，最后将这些建议转化为分层奖励塑形，用于 PPO 训练。LLM 在训练完成后被丢弃。

Result: 在 TTCP CAGE CC1 与 CC2 两个场景及两种攻击者类型下，该非对称设计相比 PPO 提升了样本效率，并优于所评估的基于势能的奖励塑形（PBRS）基线，同时保持最强的终端均值表现。

Conclusion: 通过将 LLM 仅用于训练期奖励塑形并在部署时去除，Ask the Expert 在提升样本效率与性能的同时避免了 LLM 在线部署的高延迟与信任问题，实现了纯 RL 策略部署。

Abstract: Policy-based reinforcement learning (RL) approaches have produced promising results for autonomous cyber defense; however, they are sample-inefficient in settings where defenders must respond under delayed, partial observations with actions from large action spaces. While large language models (LLMs) may reason semantically about security state space, high latency and trust assumptions prevent attractive in-line deployment models. We introduce Ask the Expert, a training-time guidance framework which first summarizes hard cyber-defense states, then intermittently queries an LLM for host-level defensive recommendations via a constrained action interface, and finally transforms those recommendations into tiered reward shaping for use with PPO. Because the LLM is discarded after training, deployment is a pure RL policy. Across TTCP CAGE CC1 and CC2 and both attacker types, this asymmetric design improves sample efficiency over PPO and outperforms the evaluated potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) baselines, while retaining the strongest terminal mean and requiring no LLM dependency at deployment time.

</details>


### [254] [Secure-CUA: Controlling Untrusted Influence in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09469)
*Sarthak Choudhary,Mihai Christodorescu,Ashish Hooda,Somesh Jha,Tongxin Li,Damien Octeau*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对计算机使用代理（CUA）在图形界面中执行任务时的安全隐患，提出安全需求形式化模型，并设计了Secure-CUA系统，通过“动作事务”机制在访问不可信内容前先固定其查询与允许用途，实现设计层面的安全性，同时保持较高的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUA通过图形界面跨应用执行点击、键入等操作，界面中可信控件与完成任务所需的不可信内容混在一起。控制不可信内容的攻击者可嵌入指令或误导性视觉线索，改变代理的预期动作或把命令重定向到错误的界面目标，因此需要形式化的安全需求来同时约束代理的决策与命令执行。

Method: 作者先形式化代理决策与GUI命令执行两方面的安全需求，证明在理想执行模型中逐步同时满足二者可保护整条执行轨迹。据此实现Secure-CUA：在访问不可信内容前先承诺一个显式的逐动作程序，即动作事务（action transaction），固定对不可信内容的查询及其响应的允许用途；系统对不可信区域进行遮蔽，用隔离的查询模型回答查询，并在遮蔽后的界面上定位预期交互目标。每一步重新生成事务以适配界面变化。

Result: 在WebArena的400个任务上，使用三个前沿模型、5个随机种子进行良性条件下的评估，共6,000条执行轨迹。Secure-CUA平均任务成功率为53.55%，Vanilla-CUA为55.12%，CaMeL-CUA为13.17%。

Conclusion: Secure-CUA在模型假设下具备设计层面的安全性，并通过每一步生成新的事务来适应界面变化，从而在保持较高任务实用性的同时提升安全性，成功率接近未加防护的Vanilla-CUA，明显优于CaMeL-CUA。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) perform tasks across applications (such as desktops, mobile apps, and web browsers) by observing graphical interfaces and issuing commands such as clicks and keystrokes. These interfaces combine trusted controls and content with untrusted content needed for legitimate tasks. An adversary controlling this untrusted content can embed instructions or misleading visual cues to change the agent's intended action or redirect its commands to the wrong interface target. We formalize security requirements for both the agent's decisions and their execution through GUI commands. In an ideal execution model, we show that enforcing both requirements at each step protects execution traces.
  We instantiate this model in Secure-CUA, our system for secure CUA execution. Its key idea is to commit to an explicit per-action program, called an $\textit{action transaction}$, before accessing untrusted content. Each transaction fixes its queries to untrusted content and the permitted uses of their responses. The system masks untrusted regions and evaluates each transaction to produce the next action, using an isolated query model to answer its queries. It then locates the intended interface target using the masked interface. Under the model's assumptions, Secure-CUA is secure by design, while generating a new transaction at each step helps maintain high task utility by adapting to changing interfaces.
  We evaluate Secure-CUA under benign conditions on 400 WebArena tasks using three frontier models across $5$ seeds, yielding $6,000$ execution traces. Secure-CUA achieves an average task success rate of $53.55\%$, compared with $55.12\%$ for Vanilla-CUA and $13.17\%$ for CaMeL-CUA.

</details>


### [255] [Constitution-Guided Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09552)
*Toluwani Aremu,Samuele Poppi,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Watermarking enables language model providers to identify text generated by their models. However, its desired properties can conflict (\ie~stronger watermark signals can degrade text quality), while designs that resist editing may also facilitate forgery. Providers address these trade-offs by choosing configurations that balance competing objectives or prioritize particular properties. Either approach imposes a shared operating point on requests with different requirements, potentially sacrificing quality where wording preservation matters or robustness where reliable attribution is essential. To allow flexible and adaptable designs, we introduce \emph{Constitution-Guided Watermarking}, a framework that selects request-appropriate trade-offs from provider requirements, listed as natural-language principles. \emph{Offline}, a pretrained reasoning agent examines constitutional rules alongside watermark implementations and iteratively refines rule-specific configurations using empirical feedback. \emph{At deployment}, a separate monitor identifies applicable rules and retrieves the corresponding policy, including watermarking exemptions, without modifying the serving model. Furthermore, our framework supports offline parallel optimization and refinement of rule-specific configurations based on evolving provider requirements without affecting deployment, and binds each deployed configuration to its evaluation evidence, making deployment decisions auditable. In a proof-of-concept evaluation using KGW and a five-rule constitution, our framework selects configurations responsive to provider priorities and improves post-paraphrase detection on robustness-prioritized requests by up to $14$ percentage points over fixed configurations, while matching or exceeding all baselines in aggregate quality and clean detection at a nominal $0.1\%$ false-positive rate.

</details>


### [256] [MARS: Malware Analysis with Rule-Based Scoring of LLM Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09553)
*Hyeongjun Choi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MARS框架对比"直接判定"与"主张(claim)评分"两种恶意软件分诊路径,发现仅需最终判定时直接分类更优,但保留的主张支持策略审查与修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型可通过直接给出判定结果或生成行为主张再经外部策略评分两种方式实现恶意软件分诊,但两种路径的优劣缺乏系统性对比。

Method: 提出MARS框架,在相同证据收集器与完全一致的静态证据包下,对6个语言模型同一证据分别进行直接分类与单遍主张评分;数据集为1,195个PE/ELF二进制(归为1,001个近重复簇),并以确定性规则作为基线;辅以子集研究(移除预定义指标字段)、家族识别探查,以及对已归档判定的精确复现与策略修订实验(含被撤回的两个模型输出)。

Result: 6个模型中直接分类全部更准确,在两条路径均有效的样本上优势为3.7–20.9个百分点,差异的95%簇自助置信区间均大于零;在12个平台×模型组合中有10个恶意告警召回更高。主张中介未带来跨模型性能波动的稳定降低。移除指标字段时,直接分类告警决策更一致,主张路径召回损失更大。家族识别中,主张准确率高于判定标签但低于证据文本。策略修订可精确复现已归档判定。

Conclusion: 在所评估的主张分类体系与加性策略下,若仅需最终判定,结果支持直接分类;但保留的主张可暴露判定计算输入,支持显式策略审查与在无需再次调用模型的情况下修订策略。

Abstract: Large language models can triage malware through direct verdicts or behavioral claims scored by an external policy. We present MARS, a malware triage framework, and compare direct classification with single-pass claim scoring using the same evidence collector and identical static evidence bundles for each model. The evaluation covers 1,195 PE and ELF binaries grouped into 1,001 near-duplicate clusters and six language models, with deterministic rules providing a baseline. Direct classification is more accurate for all six models. On samples with usable outputs from both paths, its accuracy advantage ranges from 3.7 to 20.9 percentage points, with all 95% cluster-bootstrap confidence intervals for the differences above zero. It also achieves higher malicious alert recall in ten of twelve platform and model combinations. Claim mediation provides no consistent reduction in performance variation across models. Separate subset studies find more consistent alert decisions for direct classification and a larger recall loss for the claim path when predefined indicator fields are removed. In a family identification probe, claims yield higher accuracy than verdict labels but lower accuracy than evidence text. Retained claims expose the inputs to verdict computation and permit policy revision without another model call. We reproduce archived verdicts exactly and apply a revised policy to the same records, including outputs from two additional models withdrawn by their provider. Under the evaluated claim taxonomy and additive policy, these results favor direct classification when only a verdict is required, while demonstrating that retained claims support explicit policy inspection and revision.

</details>


### [257] [Correct Answers, Unsupported Findings: Evidence Binding in Forensic Reconstruction of LLM Agent Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09581)
*Taehyeon Yun,Dongho Kim,Geonwoo Kim,Juyoung Seo,Minseok Hur,Moohong Min*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM智能体行为的取证重建，指出仅凭事实一致不足以评估重建质量，必须保留显式的"标识符—记录"绑定关系，才能区分真正有据可依的结论与"猜对"的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 取证重建不仅要恢复正确取值，还需确立哪条存档记录支撑该结论。工具日志、生成式解释与本地引用标识符各自捕捉证据的不同部分，但引用标识符本身并不构成来源，除非其与记录的绑定关系被保留下来。作者希望审计这一区分在评估中的重要性。

Method: 使用来自已保存的AgentDojo Banking执行记录的64个可机械校验案例。两个LLM阅读器在受控变化下重建来源关系，变化涉及可见证据与标识符—记录绑定。分别评估四项指标：完整记录一致性、有证据支撑的结论、有理由的弃权、无支撑的主张。另用一个确定性的同包比较器作对照。

Result: 在原始标识符且无绑定表的情况下，Sonnet恢复了全部字面来源位置，但在28个需要该缺失关系的案例中有26个做出了无支撑的引用—来源断言，其中22个仍与完整参考匹配。加入显式绑定后，两个阅读器的有据重建均得到改善；仅重命名标识符则没有一致性的补救效果。确定性同包比较器在整个过程中或正确解决该有界任务，或选择弃权。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅靠事实一致不足以评估智能体日志的取证重建；应保留显式的记录绑定，以区分有支撑的发现与正确的猜测。

Abstract: Forensic reconstruction of LLM-agent actions requires not only recovering the correct value, but establishing which preserved record supports that finding. Tool logs, generated explanations, and local citation identifiers capture different parts of this evidence, yet a citation identifier does not establish a source unless its binding to a record is preserved. We audit this distinction using 64 mechanically checkable cases from saved AgentDojo Banking executions. Two LLM readers reconstruct source relationships under controlled variations in visible evidence and identifier-to-record bindings. We separately evaluate complete-record agreement, evidence-grounded findings, justified abstention, and unsupported assertions. With original identifiers and no binding table, Sonnet recovered every literal source location but made unsupported citation-source assertions in 26 of 28 cases requiring the missing relation; 22 nevertheless matched the complete reference. Adding explicit bindings improved grounded reconstruction for both readers, whereas identifier renaming alone provided no consistent remedy. A deterministic same-packet comparator correctly resolved the bounded task or abstained throughout. These results show that factual agreement alone is insufficient for evaluating forensic reconstruction of agent logs and motivate preserving explicit record bindings to distinguish supported findings from correct guesses.

</details>


### [258] [Automotive Hardware Attacks: An Architect's Guide to TARA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09697)
*Jakub Breier,Xiaolu Hou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出 ISO/SAE 21434 的 TARA 对硬件攻击处理不一致，并提出硬件感知的 TARA 扩展以覆盖侧信道、故障注入及调试/测试接口滥用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准中的三种攻击可行性评估方法对需物理访问的攻击路径评分不一致：两种会构造性地给出极低评分，第三种则依攻击者知识与画像可变，导致实现级硬件攻击被系统性低估。

Method: 提出硬件感知 TARA 扩展，包含：覆盖车辆生命周期的物理访问攻击者模型；攻击潜力因子上的路径支配关系；三级硬件相关性门限 H0–H2 及明确决策规则（抵抗性可论证/须记录/须测试证明）；以及在证据具备前不给予实现特定抵抗性加分的评级规则。该门限与网络安全保障等级及定向攻击可行性相关联。

Result: 在参考网关架构上应用该方法，并在十项已公开的汽车部件攻击上检验其结构性输入：十项中有七项涉及调试、编程、启动、诊断或更新机制，仅四项涉及密码计算攻击。

Conclusion: 硬件攻击路径应被一致且显式地纳入 TARA；通过物理访问攻击者模型、路径支配关系、H0–H2 门限与证据驱动的评级规则，可避免对硬件抵抗性的无据假设。

Abstract: Threat analysis and risk assessment (TARA) according to ISO/SAE 21434 treats implementation-level hardware attacks inconsistently. We show that two of the three attack-feasibility approaches exemplified in the standard rate every attack path that requires physical access as very low by construction, independent of the implementation, while the third can assign the same path any of the four ratings, depending on the assumed state of attacker knowledge and on the attacker profile. This paper presents a hardware-aware extension of the TARA that covers side-channel analysis (SCA), fault-injection attacks (FIA), and abuse of debug and test interfaces. It adds an attacker model for physical access over the vehicle lifecycle; a path-dominance relation on the attack-potential factors that identifies when a simpler path makes a sophisticated one irrelevant; a three-level hardware relevance gate (H0 to H2) with an explicit decision rule that fixes whether resistance of a hardware step may be argued, must be documented, or must be demonstrated by test; and a rating rule under which a hardware step receives no credit for implementation-specific resistance until the required evidence exists. The gate is positioned relative to cybersecurity assurance levels and targeted attack feasibility. The method is applied to a reference gateway architecture, and its structural inputs are examined on ten published attacks on automotive components. In seven of the ten studies, the demonstrated attack included a debug, programming, boot, diagnostic, or update mechanism, whereas only four studies included an attack on a cryptographic computation.

</details>


### [259] [Understanding and Mitigating Token-Pruning-Induced Vulnerabilities in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09703)
*Shuailong Wang,Xinyu Lyu,Shengming Yuan,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen,Lianli Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估Token-Pruning对视听语言模型安全性的影响，发现多数剪枝策略随剪枝率上升显著损害安全，而Query-based Compression极端剪枝反而提升安全。作者揭示“剪枝诱导恶意放大”机制，并提出即插即用的安全感知剪枝（SAP），在保持效率与效用的同时将ASR最多降低62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Token-Pruning虽能加速视觉语言模型，但其安全影响尚未被充分探索。作者希望回答不同剪枝策略如何重塑模型安全行为，以及能否在不牺牲加速的前提下增强安全性。

Method: 作者识别出“Pruning-Induced Malicious Amplification”机制：背景token被移除后，注意力坍缩到少数保留的恶意前景锚点，放大其有害语义。为此提出推理时即插即用的Safety-Aware Pruning（SAP），包含三步：(1)识别恶意锚点，(2)恢复被剪枝的良性token，(3)将恶意锚点上的过度注意力重新分配给良性token。

Result: 在三个安全基准和四个效用基准上的大量实验表明，SAP缓解了剪枝引发的脆弱性，将ASR最多降低62%，且不损害效率或效用。

Conclusion: 该工作首次揭示Token-Pruning的安全隐患及其内在机制，并提出SAP作为有效且高效的解决方案，实现安全性与加速的兼顾。

Abstract: Token-Pruning accelerates Vision-Language Models by removing redundant visual tokens, yet its safety implications remain underexplored. In this work, we present the first comprehensive safety evaluation of Token-Pruning mechanisms and find that: most pruning strategies significantly degrade safety as pruning ratios increase, whereas Query-based Compression shows the opposite, with extreme pruning (up to 99.8%), unexpectedly improves model safety. This sharp contrast prompts a key question: How do different Token-Pruning strategies reshape model safety behavior, and is it possible to enhance safety without sacrificing acceleration? To answer this, we identify an unrecognized mechanism, termed Pruning-Induced Malicious Amplification, where removal of background tokens triggers a side effect: forcing the model's attention to collapse onto a few retained malicious anchors within the foreground, inadvertently amplifying their toxic semantics under jailbreak. To address that, we propose an inference-time and plug-and-play Safety-Aware Pruning (SAP) mechanism that counteracts such dominance via three steps: (1) identifying malicious anchors, (2) restoring pruned benign tokens, and (3) reallocating excessive attention from malicious anchors to benign tokens. Extensive experiments across three safety and four utility benchmarks demonstrate that SAP mitigates pruning-induced vulnerabilities, i.e., reducing ASR by up to 62%, without compromising efficiency or utility.

</details>


### [260] [Black-Box Adversarial Patch Attacks on VLAs via Ancestor VLM Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09708)
*Xiaoyi Pang,Haoyue Feng,Quanxin Shou,Yikun Miao,Zhengyang Yan,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出仅利用VLA的祖先VLM来生成对抗补丁的攻击方法，证明VLA会继承基础模型的对抗脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA越来越多用于安全攸关的物理环境，但其对抗鲁棒性研究不足。现有攻击多假设白盒访问或依赖替代VLA，现实中难以成立。作者的关键洞察是：多数VLA由公开预训练VLM适配而来，继承了视觉感知与指令条件接地两项能力，因此可仅凭祖先VLM发起攻击。

Method: 提出三种对抗补丁攻击：1）视觉破坏攻击，通过相对项与绝对项破坏投影后的视觉token；2）指令接地语义证据抑制攻击，移除指令接地概念所需的视觉证据；3）联合攻击，用两阶段课程统一上述两个目标。补丁在祖先VLM上优化后迁移至VLA。

Result: 在多个VLA家族上，仿真与静态真实图像实验表明，在祖先VLM上优化的补丁会使VLA任务成功率大幅下降，说明VLA连同基础能力一起继承了对抗脆弱性。

Conclusion: 该继承效应并非均匀：在需要精确指令接地定位的任务上最强，而在适配过程重写了共享视觉-语义表征、或动作头能迭代平滑扰动的策略上几乎消失。本文刻画了脆弱性继承的边界条件并分析其成立或失效的原因，推进了VLA相关系统的安全理解。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical physical environments, yet their adversarial robustness remains poorly understood. Existing attacks typically assume white-box access or rely on surrogate VLAs, which rarely holds in real-world deployments. Our key insight is that most VLAs are adapted from a publicly released pretrained vision-language model (VLM), inheriting two capabilities essential for action generation: visual perception and instruction-conditioned grounding. Therefore, this paper explores a previously unaddressed question: can an adversary attack deployed VLAs using only their ancestor VLMs? To this end, we propose three adversarial patch attacks that disrupt the inherited capabilities: a vision disruption attack that corrupts the projected visual tokens through relative and absolute terms, an instruction-grounded semantic evidence suppression attack that removes the visual evidence required for instruction-grounded concepts, and a joint attack that unifies both objectives under a two-phase curriculum. Experiments across different VLA families on both simulation and static real-world images show that patches optimized on the ancestor VLM cause substantial degradations in VLA task success rates, demonstrating that VLAs inherit adversarial vulnerabilities alongside their foundational capabilities. This effect is not uniform: it is strongest on tasks that require precise instruction-grounded localization, and nearly vanishes on policies whose adaptation rewrites the shared visual-semantic representation or whose action head iteratively smooths perturbations away. By characterizing the boundary conditions of vulnerability inheritance and providing analysis of why the inheritance effect holds or fails, we advance the understanding of safety for VLA-involved systems.

</details>


### [261] [Trust a Few: The Weakest Assumptions a Protocol Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09730)
*Bhumika Mittal,Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文回答了Rowe、Guttman和Liskov提出的开放问题：协议达到某个安全目标所需的最弱信任假设是什么。作者将"阻止所有最小攻击的最小假设集"刻画为最弱信任，证明该答案在引入"或"型假设后具有唯一性，并用Galois连接解释其结构，最后通过迭代循环调用验证器计算该答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协议验证器在给定信任假设下检查协议是否满足安全目标（如密钥不泄露、值新鲜、信道真实），但不会指出目标需要哪些假设。Rowe、Guttman和Liskov因此提出：协议在什么最弱假设下才能达成目标，并将该问题留作开放。本文旨在为密钥、值和信道相关的假设回答这一问题。

Method: 作者将违反目标的执行称为攻击，将其排除所需的假设称为停止集。核心结论是：协议达成目标所需的最少信任恰好是"停止所有最小攻击的所有最小方式"的集合。通过允许"或"型假设使答案唯一，并利用假设与目标之间的Galois连接解释其结构。计算方法是一个循环：询问验证器某个候选假设是否充分，记录每次报告的攻击对应的停止假设，再重新计算候选集。同时证明"至多k个假设的信任是否充分"这一判定问题是NP难的。

Result: 作者用自研的有界分析器和CPSA运行该循环，在十个协议及其变体的18个目标上找到了最弱信任，且每次结果与用同一验证器枚举所有信任假设的评估一致。结果包括：在作者对Kerberos PKINIT所采用修复的模型中，存在一个模型声明需要但客户端对服务器的认证并不需要的假设；以及在有界模型下Needham-Schroeder达成目标所需的信道假设。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了协议达成安全目标所需最弱信任假设的完整刻画与可计算算法，揭示了信任假设与目标之间的Galois结构，并证明了相关判定问题的NP难性，同时通过实验验证了方法的正确性和实用性。

Abstract: Protocol verifiers check whether a protocol meets a security goal under stated trust assumptions, such as that a key is never leaked, a value is fresh, or a channel is authentic. They do not say which of those assumptions the goal needs. Rowe, Guttman and Liskov asked for the weakest assumptions under which a protocol achieves a goal and left the question open. We answer it for assumptions about keys, values and channels. Call a run that violates the goal an attack, and the assumptions that would rule it out its stopping set. The least a protocol must trust to meet a goal is exactly the set of minimal ways to stop all of its minimal attacks. Several such sets may exist. The answer becomes unique once either/or assumptions are allowed, and it is a single set exactly when every minimal attack is stopped by a single assumption. A Galois connection between assumptions and goals explains why: a goal may end in "or", but its hypothesis may not. The same structure gives the trust needed by a conjunction of goals and an exact condition under which composed protocols need no extra trust. To compute the answer, a loop asks a verifier whether a candidate suffices, records what stops each attack it reports, and recomputes the candidates. Deciding whether some trust of at most k assumptions suffices is NP-hard. With a bounded analyser of our own and with CPSA, the loop finds the weakest trust for 18 goals over ten protocols and their variants, and on each it agrees with evaluating every trust using the same verifier. The answers include an assumption that our model of the adopted fix of Kerberos PKINIT states but the client's authentication of the server does not need, and channel assumptions under which Needham-Schroeder meets its goal in a bounded model.

</details>


### [262] [Faster PMNS Multi-precision Multiplications Using Truncated Montgomery Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09767)
*Laurent-Stéphane Didier,Alexy Dutois-Ruiz,Jean-Marc Robert*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文改进多项式模数系统(PMNS)的软件实现：提出更优的PMNS教科书式实现，并借鉴截断Montgomery约减提出新的内部约减方法，实现更小元素尺寸与最高15%的模乘加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PMNS可用多项式表示大特征域/环元素并在密码计算中加速，近期工作通过改进参数生成和使用多精度多项式系数获得加速。但现有Toeplitz实现存在内存开销较大、参数n选择受限等问题，需要更优的实现方案进一步降低复杂度并提升速度。

Method: 1) 提出PMNS教科书式软件实现，利用可自由选择更小n参数的优势降低内存开销，同时最小化乘法复杂度；2) 对PMNS的内部约减(internal reduction)应用类似Didier等人提出的截断Montgomery约减技术，面向AVX512指令集扩展的软件实现，模数规模至8192位。

Result: 教科书式实现相比Meloni等人的Toeplitz最新方案，在4096位模数下元素尺寸减小11%，速度提升20%；新的截断Montgomery式内部约减在AVX512下、模数至8192位时，模乘计算相比传统方法加速最高达15%。

Conclusion: 通过优化参数选择和小n的教科书式实现，以及引入截断Montgomery式内部约减，PMNS在元素尺寸与模乘速度上均获得显著改进，为高效密码计算提供了更佳方案。

Abstract: The Polynomial Modular Number Systems (PMNS) aim to represent elements of fields or rings of large characteristics using polynomials satisfying bounds on some parameters (degree, absolute values of the coefficients). Those PMNS, while some conditions are fulfilled, using convenient parameters and implementation features, allow some speed-ups in cryptographic computations. Recent works (Meloni \emph{et al.} \cite{MeloniPV25}) propose better speed-ups by improving the parameter generation of the system, and by using multi-precision polynomial coefficients, i.e. coefficients stored using several machine words. In this work, we first present PMNS schoolbook software implementation better in some context than the Toeplitz state-of-the-art counterpart of \cite{MeloniPV25}, taking advantage of smaller memory cost by being free to choose a smaller $n$ parameter, minimizing at the same time the complexity of the multiplication. This implementation for 4096 bit modulo size shows element size smaller by 11\% and 20 \% speed-up. We thus present a new improvement on the PMNS, applying to the \textsl{internal reduction} an approach similar to the truncated Montgomery reduction technique presented by Didier \emph{et al.} in \cite{DidierEGR24}. In the context of software implementations using \texttt{AVX512} instruction set extension, and modulo size up to 8192 bits, this new approach allows speed-ups up to 15\% in modular multiplication computation, in comparison with conventional approaches.

</details>


### [263] [Formal Runtime Verification for Tool-Using LLM Agents: An Offline Same-Benchmark Study on AgentDojo and STAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09793)
*Nikolaos Kekatos,Stylianos Basagiannis,Marinelio Chintri,Alexios Lekidis,Tom Nianios,Ioannis Seitoglou,Anastasios Temperekidis,Panagiotis Katsaros*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文用度量一阶时序逻辑（MFOTL）与 MonPoly 监控器离线重放 AgentDojo、STAC、R-Judge 的轨迹，检验声明式安全策略对工具型 LLM 代理护栏的适用性。结论：形式化时序监控只有在轨迹提供可信历史时才真正增值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具型 LLM 代理的护栏多为应用特定的规则，导致多步、依赖数据的安全策略难以被明确表述、审计和复用。作者希望评估一种声明式的替代方案——度量一阶时序逻辑（MFOTL）——是否能统一表达这类策略。

Method: 离线重放 AgentDojo、STAC 与 R-Judge 已有的记录轨迹，通过未经修改的 MonPoly 监控器执行五条通用义务，全程不实际运行代理。同时检验带溯源（provenance）的策略，并测试溯源上下文自身被攻击时的规避现象及其修复方法。

Result: 五条通用义务能标记出 STAC 攻击链的 71.8% 和 AgentDojo 成功攻击的 70.1%，但也会在 29.3% 的良性运行上误报。这种不精确性源自语料库本身而非逻辑：语料极少记录审批、从不记录时间戳，使依赖历史的义务退化为检测风险动作类型。在轨迹携带关系上下文的场景中，溯源感知策略区分度更好；但该上下文本身可被攻击，一条植入的记录即可在原本会标记的 94-99% 运行上击败朴素溯源检查。将溯源绑定到产生它的查询可无损地消除该规避。

Conclusion: 形式化时序监控恰好在轨迹暴露可信历史时才有价值。作者据此量化了现有基准与该目标的差距，并提出一个十二字段的、可直接用于执行的轨迹 schema。

Abstract: Guardrails for tool-using LLM agents are usually application-specific rules, which makes multi-step, data-dependent safety policies hard to specify, audit and reuse. As a declarative alternative, we evaluate metric first-order temporal logic (MFOTL), replaying the recorded trajectories that AgentDojo, STAC and R-Judge already ship through the unmodified MonPoly monitor, offline and without running an agent. On these corpora, five generic obligations flag 71.8% of STAC attack chains and 70.1% of successful AgentDojo attacks, but also fire on 29.3% of benign runs. This imprecision stems from the corpora rather than the logic: they rarely record approvals and never record timestamps, so history-dependent obligations reduce to detecting risky action types. Where the trace does carry relational context, provenance-aware policies discriminate better; that context, however, is itself attackable, and one planted line defeats a naive provenance check on 94-99% of the runs it would otherwise flag. Binding provenance to the lookup that produced it closes this evasion at no cost in detection or benign firing. Taken together, these results show that formal temporal monitoring adds value exactly when the trace exposes trustworthy history. We therefore quantify how far current benchmarks are from that point and propose a twelve-field enforcement-ready trace schema.

</details>


### [264] [Hierarchical Security Monitoring for Edge-IoT: A Formal Methods Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09817)
*Nikolaos Kekatos,Marinelio Chintri,Panagiotis Katsaros,Alexios Lekidis,Tom Nianios,Ioannis Seitoglou,Stylianos Basagiannis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于形式化运行时验证的轻量级分层安全监控框架，在边缘设备运行TeSSLa流规格、在网关运行MonPoly一阶监控，以极低的通信与计算成本检测协调式多设备攻击，将非对称成本转向防御方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘IoT部署中的网络弹性本质是经济问题：检测需在受攻击时维持关键进程运行，但防御方资源（计算、带宽、运维注意力）有限。集中式云监控跨设备检测能力强但带宽成本过高；纯边缘本地监控廉价却对协调式多设备攻击视而不见，非对称平衡有利于攻击者。因此需要占据实际中间地带、成本可量化的方案。

Method: 构建于形式化运行时验证方法之上的轻量级分层监控框架：每台边缘设备运行轻量级TeSSLa流规格（尺寸、载荷有效性、速率、时间戳漂移谓词），每个聚合窗口输出四值裁决，单事件成本亚微秒级；网关运行参数化一阶MonPoly监控器处理各设备裁决流，单裁决成本微秒级。边到网关上行每节点每聚合窗口约传输一个布尔量，比原始数据包流小几个数量级。每条告警携带包含设备、监控层、触发谓词的见证集，形成可审计记录。

Result: 在跨正常与攻击节点的容器主机测试床上评估，覆盖四类攻击（缓冲区溢出、时间欺骗、拒绝服务、混合高级持续性威胁模式）。同时描述了边缘层与网关层规格，以及在增加监控级别时的成本与覆盖权衡。

Conclusion: 该分层框架在可量化成本下占据云监控与纯边缘监控之间的实际中间地带，网关层能检测单节点监控器无法发现的协调式攻击模式，将非对称成本平衡转向防御方，并提供可审计的告警见证记录。

Abstract: Cyber resiliency in edge-IoT deployments is fundamentally an economic problem: detection must keep critical processes operating under attack, but defender resources (compute, bandwidth, operator attention) are bounded. Centralised cloud monitoring offers expressive cross-device detection at prohibitive bandwidth cost; purely edge-local monitoring is cheap but blind to coordinated multi-device attacks where the asymmetric balance favours the attacker. We propose a lightweight hierarchical security-monitoring framework, built on formal runtime-verification methods, that occupies the practical middle ground at quantified cost. Each edge device runs a lightweight TeSSLa stream specification (size, payload validity, rate, and timestamp-drift predicates) that emits a four-valued verdict per aggregation window at sub-microsecond per-event cost; the gateway runs a parametric first-order MonPoly monitor over the per-device verdict streams at microsecond-scale per-verdict cost. The edge-to-gateway uplink carries roughly one Boolean per aggregation window per node, orders of magnitude smaller than the raw packet stream. The gateway tier detects coordinated attack patterns that no single-node monitor can see, shifting the asymmetric cost balance toward the defender. Every alert carries a witness set naming the device, the monitor tier, and the predicate that fired, providing an auditable record of the decision. We evaluate the framework on a container-host testbed spanning nominal and attacker nodes across four attack classes (buffer overflow, time spoofing, denial-of-service, and mixed advanced-persistent-threat patterns), and describe the edge- and gateway-tier specifications together with the cost-versus-coverage trade-off as monitor levels are added.

</details>


### [265] [Hybrid Hierarchical Runtime Verification for Edge-IoT Security: Combining MonPoly and RTLola](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09825)
*Nikolaos Kekatos,Marinelio Chintri,Panagiotis Katsaros,Alexios Lekidis,Tom Nianios,Ioannis Seitoglou,Anastasios Temperekidis,Stylianos Basagiannis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出三层层次化运行时验证框架，解决边缘IoT集群安全监控中跨设备协同攻击、带宽开销与单引擎难以检测静默攻击三大结构性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘IoT集群安全监控面临三类结构性挑战：(i) 单节点监控成本低但无法发现跨设备协同攻击；(ii) 云端监控可见全集群但带宽代价高；(iii) 即便在云端，基于单一RV引擎的监控也易被攻击者欺骗——攻击者攻陷设备后发起一次恶意请求随即静默，事件触发式监控在事件停止后无从评估。

Method: 提出三层层次化运行时验证（RV）框架：边缘层实时分类事件；网关层聚合各设备短时行为窗口；云端层运行两个互补RV引擎。MonPoly负责合并告警流上的一阶时序关联，涵盖真正跨设备量化的协同溢出及单设备多向量APT、升级、持久化攻击模式；RTLola负责时间触发的静默节点属性，可检测事件触发引擎在有界延迟内无法发现的静默攻击。

Result: 在包含15个actor的Docker测试台上评估，覆盖8种攻击画像及一个静默绕过场景。在受控带标签测试台中，框架报告的每起可归因设备的事件均指向攻击者标记的设备，RTLola层能捕获事件触发层遗漏的静默绕过尝试。边缘与网关的逐事件监控保持在微秒级，云端端到端告警到事件延迟较低。

Conclusion: 三层RV框架同时应对跨设备可见性、带宽成本与静默攻击检测三大挑战，实验表明其在攻击归因准确性、静默绕过检测能力及低延迟性能上均表现良好。

Abstract: Security monitoring of edge-IoT fleets faces three structural challenges. (i) A per-node monitor is cheap but cannot see attacks that coordinate across devices. (ii) A cloud monitor sees the full fleet but pays for that view in bandwidth. (iii) Even at the cloud, a monitor built on a single RV engine can be fooled by an attacker who compromises a device, raises one malicious request, and then goes silent: once the events stop, an event-triggered monitor has nothing left to evaluate. We propose a three-layer hierarchical runtime-verification framework that addresses all three. The edge layer classifies events as they happen, the gateway layer aggregates short windows of per-device behaviour, and the cloud layer runs two complementary RV engines. MonPoly handles first-order temporal correlation over the merged alert stream: coordinated overflow (which genuinely quantifies across devices) plus per-device multi-vector APT, escalation, and persistent-campaign patterns. RTLola handles a time-triggered silent-node property that an event-triggered engine cannot detect within a bounded delay under fleet silence. We evaluate the framework on a 15-actor Docker testbed covering eight attack profiles plus a silent-bypass scenario. In the controlled labelled testbed, every device-attributable incident the framework raises names an attacker-labelled device, and the RTLola tier catches silent-bypass attempts the event-triggered tier misses. Per-event monitoring stays in the microsecond range at the edge and gateway, with low end-to-end alert-to-incident latency at the cloud.

</details>


### [266] [CYBERFORT: A Compliance-Chain Platform Operationalising the Cyber Resilience Act for SMEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09918)
*Nikolaos Kekatos,Georgios Koutidis,Mihaela Curcă,Angelos Fountoulakis,Marios Ioannou,Michael Ioannou,Elias Iosif,Paul Lacatus,Alexios Lekidis,Avgi Michael,Tom Nianios*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍 CYBERFORT，一个开源、以欧盟《网络弹性法案》(CRA) 为核心的合规平台，面向缺乏专门 GRC 能力的中小企业，将 CRA 义务转化为可追溯的“合规链”，并已在 43 家机构试用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRA 将产品网络安全变为对欧盟市场数字元素产品制造商、进口商、分销商和集成商的整个生命周期合规义务，而多数中小企业缺乏专门的治理、风险与合规(GRC)能力，难以承担这一负担。因此需要一个 CRA 优先、可操作且低成本的合规支持平台。

Method: 提出开源平台 CYBERFORT，通过引导式范围自评、与 CRA 附件一及漏洞处理义务绑定的题库，以及合规检查引擎，将每个答案关联到控制措施、政策与机器验证证据；仅在 CRA 义务重合处复用 ISO/IEC 27001、NIS2 和 GDPR 控制，并构建“合规链”将产品风险、控制、政策、CRA 义务、证据、技术文档和欧盟符合性声明串联起来。

Result: 平台已部署于 https://access.cyber-fort.eu/，首批服务 43 家机构；论文报告了合规链背后的工程实现，以及针对一个 SIEM/XDR 产品的端到端案例研究测量结果，该案例含 AI 驱动的补救措施并覆盖 CRA 义务各章节；受控工作量研究仍在进行中。

Conclusion: CYBERFORT 可帮助中小企业以可追溯、可核查的方式识别并弥补合规缺口，从而在上市前完成 CRA 合规准备，为 CRA 落地提供实用工具与初步实证支持。

Abstract: The EU Cyber Resilience Act (CRA) turns product cybersecurity into a lifecycle compliance obligation for manufacturers, importers, distributors, and integrators of products with digital elements on the EU market, a load that falls largely on small and medium-sized enterprises (SMEs) that rarely have dedicated governance, risk, and compliance (GRC) capacity. We present CYBERFORT, an open-source CRA-first compliance platform developed under the EU Digital Europe Programme and one of twelve projects in the EU CRA cluster. CYBERFORT operationalises the CRA through a guided scope self-assessment, a question bank tied to Annex I and the vulnerability-handling obligations, and a compliance-checking engine that links every answer to controls, policies, and machine-attested evidence, reusing ISO/IEC 27001, NIS2, and GDPR controls only where they coincide with CRA obligations. Its central contribution is the compliance chain, a traceable structure linking each product risk through its controls and policies to the CRA obligations it satisfies, and onward through evidence to the technical-documentation file and EU declaration of conformity, so that every operational gap is traceable and can be closed before market placement. Deployed at https://access.cyber-fort.eu/ for a first cohort of 43 organisations, the platform is presented with the engineering behind the chain, measured results from a completed end-to-end case study on a SIEM/XDR product with AI-driven remediation spanning the CRA obligation chapters, and the controlled effort study that remains in progress.

</details>


### [267] [Defensive Sufficiency in a Stackelberg Model of AI Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09892)
*Subhabrata Majumdar,Rajlakshmi Chavan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究AI系统防御中"发现失败→有效修复"的反馈闭环何时能提供充分保护、以及投资该过程是否经济划算，通过概率分析给出保护收敛条件与时间界，并用Stackelberg博弈刻画防御者的最优投资配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 来自自动化测试、人类红队和事件响应的反馈可以强化AI系统防御，但这一反馈过程并非总能提供足够保护，也可能在经济上不值得投资。论文旨在明确反馈式防御在什么条件下充分有效，以及防御者应如何在主动发现与被动修复之间分配资源。

Method: 首先进行理论分析：证明当攻击面为有限输入集合、每个未解决攻击都有持续被发现的机会、修复有效且后续更新保留先前保护时，防御成功的概率为1；推导完成时间界，并扩展到攻击面增长、修复跨相关攻击泛化及多种发现机制的场景。随后构建防御者主导的Stackelberg博弈，防御者投资主动发现与被动修复，并预判攻击者的搜索努力选择，求解威慑攻击的最小成本配置与均衡区域。

Result: 理论结果区分了对每个固定攻击的"最终保护"与单一时刻的"完全保护"，并给出完成时间界。博弈分析刻画了威慑攻击的最小成本分配，以及防御者不投资、只投资一种能力或同时投资两种能力的均衡区域。数值实验展示了这些均衡区域，并表明更快的修复可以缩短被攻陷持续时间，但不降低被攻陷概率。

Conclusion: 反馈驱动的修复过程在持续发现、有效修复和保护可继承的条件下能够实现对固定攻击的最终保护，但其充分性与经济性取决于攻击面是否增长及攻击者努力。防御者应依据均衡区域决定投资主动发现、被动修复或两者兼顾，并注意加快修复只能缩短暴露时长而非消除被攻陷风险。

Abstract: Feedback from automated testing, human red teaming, and incident response can strengthen an AI system's defenses when discovered failures lead to effective repairs. We study when this feedback process provides sufficient protection and when investing in it is economically worthwhile. We begin by showing that an attack surface composed of finite number of inputs is defended with probability 1 if every unresolved attack has a persistent chance of discovery, repairs are effective, and subsequent updates preserve earlier protection. We derive completion-time bounds and extend the analysis to growing attack surfaces, repairs that generalize across related attacks, and multiple discovery mechanisms. These results distinguish eventual protection against each fixed attack from complete protection at a single time. We then formulate a defender-led Stackelberg game in which the defender invests in proactive discovery and reactive repair, anticipating the attacker's choice of search effort. We characterize the least-cost allocation that deters attack and the equilibrium regimes in which the defender funds neither capability, one capability, or both. Numerical experiments illustrate these regimes and show how faster repair can reduce compromise duration without reducing compromise probability.unified theory of performance limits in generative language models.

</details>


### [268] [On the Reliability of LLM-Based Vulnerability Patching Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10150)
*Dang K Le,Wenxuan Shi,Xinyu Xing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文指出当前LLM自动漏洞修补基准测试可能严重扭曲性能评估，从智能体、框架、数据集三个维度识别隐患，并构建含112个历史漏洞的新基准，结合PoC、回归与开发者测试进行受控实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞修补基准的评估设计可能导致报告性能失真：提示方式、工具可用性、详细指令可能在不提升补丁质量的情况下抬高成功率；权限错误、基础设施缺陷和超时处理会静默地抑制或夸大性能；而缺陷报告与单一PoC测试无法判断补丁是否解决根因或符合开发者意图。因此需要更严谨、可靠的评估方法。

Method: 从84个开源C/C++、Go和Rust项目中整理112个历史漏洞，每个漏洞配备PoC测试、回归测试以及额外的开发者测试（用于评估是否符合原开发者设计原则）。从智能体层面、框架层面、数据集层面三个维度进行受控实验与案例研究。

Result: LLM在理想条件下可获得较高的PoC通过率，但基准执行方式的选择会实质性改变测得的成功率；更重要的是，开发者测试通过率仍然较低，且新模型仅带来边际提升，说明模型更多是在抑制症状而非持续产出上游质量级别的修复。

Conclusion: 基准分数对评估设计高度敏感，作者提供实践指南以推动更严谨、可靠且可复现的LLM漏洞修补评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for automated vulnerability patching, but current benchmarks can substantially distort reported performance. Drawing on extensive experience developing, running, and stress-testing such frameworks, we identify under-examined pitfalls across three dimensions: (1) agent-level factors, where prompting, tool availability, and detailed instructions can raise success rates without improving developer-aligned patch quality; (2) framework-level factors, where permission errors, infrastructure bugs, and timeout handling can silently suppress or inflate performance; and (3) dataset-level factors, where bug reports and single proof-of-concept (PoC) tests fail to capture whether patches address root causes or follow developer intent. We curate 112 historical bugs from 84 open-source C/C++, Go, and Rust projects, each with PoC tests, regression tests, and additional developer tests that assess alignment with the original developers' design principles. Through controlled experiments and case studies, we show that LLMs can achieve high PoC passing rates under ideal conditions, yet benchmark execution choices can materially change measured success. More importantly, developer-test passing rates remain low and improve only marginally with newer models, suggesting that models increasingly suppress symptoms without consistently producing upstream-quality fixes. These results show that benchmark scores are highly sensitive to evaluation design, and we provide practical guidelines for more rigorous, reliable, and reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [269] [Constrained-Action AI Remediation for SIEM/XDR via a NeMo-Guardrails Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09906)
*Georgios Koutidis,Nikolaos Kekatos,Tom Nianios,Alexios Lekidis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受限动作架构，通过SIEM/XDR控制平面配合NeMo-Guardrails代理，将SOC中LLM的输出限制在封闭意图词汇表内，防止对抗性告警变成远程代码路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IT与OT的SOC共同面临告警洪水与分析师不足的问题，而将LLM作为自主推理引擎来执行封IP、杀进程、隔离文件等命令，会引入新风险：单条对抗性告警可能通过LLM推理变成远程代码路径，促使SOC执行错误动作。

Method: 提出两层协调的受限动作架构：(1) SIEM/XDR控制平面，把修复动作锚定在关联的主机事件上，并将LLM输出限制在封闭意图词汇表，其模板化命令由轻量端点代理执行，并由参数校验器兜底；(2) NeMo-Guardrails代理，用输入和输出护栏策略包裹SOC分析师LLM，并针对发布的SOC专用对抗语料库进行开箱评估。

Result: 现成代理将注入召回率从25.0%提升到94.5%，误报率为0.1%；实弹红队演练确认封闭意图词汇表和参数校验器能在命令跨越信任边界前遏制观察到的LLM失效模式。该架构作为适配性（而非已测量的OT实际部署）适用于关键基础设施场景。护栏延迟使实时内联控制不在范围内。

Conclusion: 受限动作架构能有效缓解LLM驱动的SOC自主修复风险，适合在错误修复会产生物理后果的关键基础设施中使用，但最佳运行方式是人机协同或延迟执行，因为当前护栏延迟不适合内联实时控制。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) for information technology and operational technology share one incident-response problem: a flood of correlated alerts and too few analysts. Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed as reasoning engines that triage alerts and, in autonomous deployments, issue commands that block IPs, kill processes, or quarantine files on production hosts. This coupling introduces a new risk: a single adversarial alert can become a remote code path through the LLM's reasoning, leading it to recommend an action the SOC then executes. We present a constrained-action architecture with two coordinated layers: (i) a SIEM/XDR control plane that grounds remediation in correlated host events and confines the LLM's output to a closed intent vocabulary whose templated commands are executed by thin endpoint agents, backstopped by an argument validator; and (ii) a NeMo-Guardrails proxy that wraps the SOC-analyst LLM with input- and output-rail policies, evaluated out-of-the-box against a SOC-specific adversarial corpus we release. The stock proxy lifts injection recall from 25.0% to 94.5% at a 0.1% false-positive rate, and a live red-team exercise confirms that the closed intent vocabulary and argument validator contain the observed LLM failure modes before any command crosses the trust boundary. As an architectural fit (not yet a measured operational-technology deployment), the constrained-action property suits critical-infrastructure settings where a wrong remediation has physical, not merely operational, consequences. The loop is best run human-in-the-loop or delayed: the measured rail latency keeps inline control out of scope.

</details>


### [270] [Sensitive-Topic Leakage Through LLM Routing Metadata: Measurement and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09981)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究揭示LLM路由器的模型选择日志(即使关闭内容日志)构成隐私侧信道，可用于推断请求的敏感类别和用户身份，且现有防御多失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM路由器按请求内容在廉价/昂贵模型间选择，许多网关和云平台会记录该选择但关闭内容日志，作者想测量这一超出token计数的隐私旁路，并考虑噪声标签和重复提示。

Method: 在170万真实请求(WildChat-1M、LMSYS-Chat-1M)上预注册研究，使用两个成本/质量路由器和一域路由器；调查11个系统的日志记录方式；测试后处理防御。

Result: 长度匹配下偏移方向取决于类别与路由器：RouteLLM 50%工作点中骚扰/自残请求较少到强模型(19点)、医疗请求少31点、性相关多10点；20次决策以AUC 0.71区分频繁医疗询问者(未达0.75主要终点)；域路由器0.92为上限估计；长度匹配+准确标签的均等化可在真实流量消除差距，RouterBench代价≤0.2准确点；会话粘性、用户预算带与池化均等化均失败；精确每用户速率仅隐藏偶数前缀强计数，保留奇数位置决策，日志攻击20次后AUC达0.73。

Conclusion: 路由选择日志构成可测量的隐私信道，暴露敏感类别与用户特征，简单防御难以兼顾效用与隐私，需更系统的路由日志保护设计。

Abstract: LLM routers pick a cheap or expensive model per request by its content, and many gateways and some cloud platforms can log that choice with content logging off. We measure this privacy channel beyond token counts, accounting for noisy labels and repeated prompts. We run pre-registered studies on 1.7 million real requests (WildChat-1M, LMSYS-Chat-1M) with two cost/quality routers and a domain router, survey eleven systems' logging, and test post-processing defenses. At matched length, the shift's direction depends on category and router. For RouteLLM at the 50% operating point, harassment and self-harm requests reach the strong model 19 points less often than comparable ones on prompts unseen in exploration, medical requests (exploratory: LLM labels failed their gate) 31 points less often on distinct prompts (both post hoc), and sexual requests 10 points more often (secondary); the other router's four are negative. Twenty RouteLLM decisions separate frequent medical askers with AUC 0.71, exploratory and below the pre-registered primary endpoint's 0.75 (domain router: 0.92, an upper estimate). Per-category length-matched parity with accurate labels removes the gap on real traffic, costing at most 0.2 accuracy points on RouterBench (post hoc), where routers' gaps on sensitive subjects (13-42 points, pre-registered) exceed those of an oracle routing by realized accuracy gain (1-11, post hoc). Per-conversation stickiness, per-user budget bands, and pooled parity fail, the last as categories' shifts differ in size or sign. A post hoc exact per-user rate hides only even-prefix strong counts and forfeits most self-assessed routing value; it preserves odd-position decisions, from which a post hoc log attack reaches AUC 0.73 after 20 RouteLLM requests (exploratory).

</details>


### [271] [Defining Purpose-Limited Secrets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10010)
*Bhumika Mittal,Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，把"用途"（purpose）变成密钥/令牌本身的属性，用可容许操作集 A(P) 代替声明的意图 I，并为同一 I 定义三种约束制度：限制（confinement）、中介（mediation）和问责（accountability），并以支付令牌为例展示三者可作用于同一秘密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学秘密通常为某一用途签发，却赋予更大的能力（如为聚合计算的解密密钥可读取每条记录，支付单张发票的令牌可掏空账户）。现有系统只在策略中声明用途，却只对能力做强制，导致用途与能力之间存在缺口。作者希望让用途成为秘密本身的属性，从而弥补这一缺口。

Method: 将声明的预期用途 I 映射为可容许操作集 A(P)，机制只看到操作而非理由。围绕同一 I 定义三种制度：限制（超出用途的操作不可行）、中介（可信组件拒绝）、问责（超出声明界限的使用可行但归因于持有者）。用博弈刻画限制制度下的不可预测性与不可区分性；并将支付令牌设计为同时使用三种制度：在单一商户支付一笔有上限的费用、只能收窄不能放宽、由商户检查、重复使用时可识别取款账户。安全性归约到不可伪造签名与抗碰撞哈希（无随机预言机），可追溯性与不可陷害性在离散对数与不可伪造签名下（随机预言机模型中）成立。

Result: 限制博弈（不可预测性与不可区分性）分别与功能签名不可伪造性和受限 PRF 伪随机性的密钥查询形式一致，并在可行性边界内对应模拟安全的功能加密；相对于 I 表述时，它们也记录了一个允许过多的谓词。支付令牌满足衰减不可伪造性（归约到不可伪造签名与抗碰撞哈希，无需随机预言机），可追溯性与不可陷害性在离散对数和不可伪造签名假设下（随机预言机模型）成立。

Conclusion: 本文不主张新的原语、困难假设或一般组合定理；该框架是一个通用规范，用以表述和比较此类原语，并将用途建模义务划定在策略接管之处。

Abstract: A cryptographic secret is issued for a purpose but grants a capability, and the capability is usually larger: a decryption key meant for computing aggregates can read every record, and a token meant to pay one invoice can drain the account. Deployed systems state the purpose in policy and enforce only the capability. We make the purpose a property of the secret. A mechanism sees operations, not reasons, so a scheme enforces an admissible set $A(P)$ of operations that stands in for a declared intended use $I$; whether $I$ captures the human purpose is a modeling obligation that marks where policy takes over. Against the same $I$ we define three regimes: under confinement an operation outside the intended use is infeasible, under mediation a trusted component refuses it, and under accountability use beyond a stated bound is possible but attributed to the holder. The confinement games, for unpredictability and indistinguishability, coincide with the key-query forms of functional-signature unforgeability and constrained-PRF pseudorandomness and correspond to simulation-secure functional encryption within its feasibility boundary; stated against $I$, they also register a predicate that permits too much. A payment token uses all three regimes on one secret: it pays one capped charge at one merchant, can be narrowed but not widened, is checked by the merchant, and identifies the withdrawing account if spent twice. Attenuation unforgeability reduces to unforgeable signatures and a collision-resistant hash without random oracles; traceability and non-frameability hold under discrete log and unforgeable signatures in the random-oracle model. We claim no new primitive, hardness assumption, or general composition theorem; the framework is a common specification against which such primitives are stated and compared.

</details>


### [272] [BetweenCut: Private Heavy-Node Classification with Doubly Logarithmic Error in Tree Height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10075)
*Ergute Bao,Graham Cormode,Xiaokui Xiao,Ting Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了BetweenCut算法，在树结构数据中寻找重节点时实现了记录级差分隐私，其加性误差为O(log log h)，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化数据上寻找计数超过阈值的重节点是分析的基础，但实现记录级差分隐私且不牺牲精度具有挑战性，因为每条记录会沿着整条根到叶路径贡献计数，隐私开销随层级累积。

Method: 提出BetweenCut算法，一种(ε,δ)-差分隐私算法，通过更精细的机制处理每条记录的多重阈值比较问题，从而降低累积误差。

Result: BetweenCut实现了O_{ε,δ}(log log h)的加性误差，显著优于现有方法（误差为Ω(log h)或Ω(√log h)），且该误差对所有节点同时成立，与输入数据库大小无关。

Conclusion: 对于深层树，BetweenCut改进了现有的误差界限，兼顾了差分隐私与精度，且误差不依赖数据库规模。

Abstract: Finding heavy nodes in a tree---those whose counts exceed a given threshold---is a building block for analysis and learning over structured data. Achieving record-level differential privacy (DP) without sacrificing accuracy is challenging because each record contributes to counts along an entire root-to-leaf path, allowing privacy costs to accumulate across levels. Existing methods account for the multiple threshold comparisons for each record incur additive error margins of $Ω_{\varepsilon,δ}(\log h)$ or $Ω_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for tree height $h$. We introduce \textsc{BetweenCut}, an $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP algorithm with an additive error margin of $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\log\log h)$, improving the existing bounds for deep trees. This error holds simultaneously for all nodes and is independent of the input database size.

</details>


### [273] [Pump-and-Dump meets Honeypot Tokens: Detection and Analysis of Telegram Bait-and-Trap Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10149)
*Federico Cernera,Massimo La Morgia,Alessandro Mei,Francesco Sassi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究去中心化交易所(DEX)中的拉高出货骗局，发现其机制实为"诱饵陷阱"(Bait-and-Trap)：组织者利用蜜罐代币让Telegram订阅者能买不能卖，99.3%情况下获利，总额超700万美元，并提出交易模拟工具进行防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉高出货(Pump-and-Dump)在中心化交易所已被广泛研究，但其在去中心化交易所(DEX)中的运作方式基本未被探索。作者希望填补这一空白，揭示DEX环境下此类骗局的真实机制。

Method: 监控83个用于协调拉高出货活动的Telegram频道，在BNB Smart Chain和Ethereum上收集3,677个事件进行分析。此外开发了一个交易模拟工具，通过测试用户能否在代币的实时合约状态下成功卖出来检测蜜罐代币。

Result: DEX中的骗局表面上类似CEX的拉高出货，但底层机制完全不同：组织者并非操纵价格，而是围绕蜜罐代币策划欺骗性活动，这些代币的智能合约允许Telegram订阅者买入却阻止其卖出。不知情的受害者买入后无法取回资金。该机制比传统拉高出货更可靠地获利：组织者在99.3%的情况下获利，提取利润超700万美元。

Conclusion: 作者将这一新型欺诈机制命名为Bait-and-Trap(诱饵陷阱)，并提出的交易模拟工具可在购买前通过测试实时合约状态下的卖出可行性来检测蜜罐代币，为防御Bait-and-Trap操作提供了实用手段。

Abstract: While Pump-and-Dump schemes have been extensively studied on centralized exchanges (CEXs), how they operate in decentralized exchanges (DEXs) remains largely unexplored. We monitor 83 Telegram channels used to coordinate Pump-and-Dump campaigns and collect 3,677 events across the BNB Smart Chain and Ethereum. Our analysis reveals that, although these operations superficially resemble CEX-based Pump-and-Dump schemes, their underlying mechanism is fundamentally different. Rather than manipulating prices, organizers orchestrate deceptive Pump-and-Dump campaigns around honeypot tokens whose smart contracts allow Telegram subscribers to buy but prevent them from selling. Unaware of this restriction, victims purchase these tokens and are unable to recover their funds. We refer to this new fraud mechanism as Bait-and-Trap. We show that Bait-and-Trap provides a substantially more reliable profit strategy than Pump-and-Dump schemes: organizers gain in 99.3% of cases, extracting over $7 million in profit. To counter this threat, we develop a transaction-simulation tool that detects honeypot tokens before purchase by testing whether a user can successfully sell against the token's live contract state, providing a practical defense against Bait-and-Trap operations.

</details>


### [274] [Collusion-Secure Semi-Quantum Secret Sharing Scheme using a Quantum Third Party](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10176)
*Santanu Majhi,Nirupam Basak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型半量子秘密共享（SQSS）协议，消除合谋攻击漏洞，实现信息论安全，并优化量子比特效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SQSS协议中参与者量子能力有限，可能存在合谋攻击漏洞：协助的量子第三方可与不诚实的经典参与者联手窃取秘密而不被检测。

Method: 提出一种新的SQSS协议，采用基础Bell态、经典dealer，并针对合谋攻击进行安全设计，随后证明其抵御多种内外部攻击的安全性。

Result: 协议实现对合谋攻击的信息论安全，能抵抗广泛的内外部攻击；对比分析显示其在量子比特效率、经典dealer支持和抵御DCNA攻击方面均优于现有协议。

Conclusion: 该构造同时优化了量子比特效率、引入经典dealer并增强抗DCNA攻击能力，是对现有SQSS协议的重要改进。

Abstract: The security of a secret sharing protocol is compromised if one or more dishonest participants can reconstruct the secret by deviating from the prescribed protocol, particularly when such malicious behavior remains undetected. Semi-Quantum Secret Sharing (SQSS) is a variant of secret sharing in which the participants possess only classical capabilities, such as preparing and measuring qubits in the computational ($Z$) basis, while a quantum-capable third party assists the dealer in generating and distributing quantum shares of a classical secret. The limited quantum capabilities of the participants in SQSS protocols may introduce vulnerabilities to collusion attacks, enabling an assisting quantum third party, in collaboration with a dishonest classical participant, to jointly recover the secret even without detection. In this work, we propose a novel SQSS protocol that eliminates this vulnerability and achieves information-theoretic security against collusion attacks. We further prove that the proposed protocol is secure against a broad class of external and internal attacks. Finally, a comparative analysis demonstrates that our construction advances existing SQSS protocols by simultaneously optimizing qubit efficiency, involves a classical dealer, and resilience against DCNA attacks using basic Bell states.

</details>


### [275] [SLDR: Defending Against Malicious Fine-tuning via Selective Layers Recovery and Dynamic Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10345)
*Hui Zhang,Yachao Yuan,Jiayun Wang,Yuanzhuo Li,Hongtao Wang,Yali Yuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SLDR方法，通过选择性地恢复关键层并动态路由，在微调后大幅降低有害输出同时保留下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调即服务允许用户适配对齐的LLM，但恶意微调会削弱模型的拒绝行为，同时仍能在合法输入上保持任务性能，这构成安全隐患。作者重新审视分层安全诊断，发现安全敏感性具有符号性（signed）：不同层的缩放可能增强、削弱或几乎不影响拒绝行为，这一观察启发了针对性防御。

Method: SLDR是一种微调后的防御方法，基于选择性层恢复与动态路由。它仅在有符号敏感性谱中敏感性得分最高和最低的层上训练LoRA恢复适配器，并采用基于表征的动态路由推理，仅对恶意查询激活该适配器。

Result: 在四种模型架构、五个下游任务和四个有害基准上，SLDR大幅减少有害输出并保持下游效用。在Llama3.1/SST2上，平均有害得分从11.54降至0.08，同时保持下游准确率，且在污染比例高达0.9时有害得分仍接近零。

Conclusion: SLDR通过针对敏感性极值层的选择性恢复与动态路由，有效缓解恶意微调带来的安全退化，兼顾安全性与任务效用，具有良好的泛化性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning-as-a-service enables users to adapt aligned large language models (LLMs) to specialized tasks, but malicious fine-tuning can erode refusal behavior while preserving task performance on legitimate inputs. We revisit recent layer-wise safety diagnostics and find that safety sensitivity is signed: scaling different layers can strengthen refusal, weaken it, or have little effect. Motivated by this observation, we propose SLDR, a post-fine-tuning defense based on Selective Layers Recovery and Dynamic Routing. SLDR trains a LoRA recovery adapter only on the layers with the maximum and minimum sensitivity scores in the signed spectrum, and uses representation-based dynamic routing inference to activate the adapter only for malicious queries. Across four model architectures, five downstream tasks, and four harmful benchmarks, SLDR substantially reduces harmful outputs while preserving downstream utility. On Llama3.1/SST2, SLDR reduces the average harmful score from 11.54 to 0.08 while maintaining downstream accuracy, and the harmful score remains near zero under poisoning ratios up to 0.9. The code is available at https://github.com/Stardust457/SLDR.

</details>


### [276] [Receiver-Domain Behavioral Probing for Backdoor-Resilient Federated GPS Spoofing Detection in UAV Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10360)
*Will Jedrzejczak,Cole Walther,Dilpreet Gill,Moh Khalid Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示联邦学习无人机GPS欺骗检测中"客户端自报验证精度"这一权重机制可被恶意客户端利用放大后门攻击，并提出"接收机域行为探测"方法，用反事实欺骗样本评估模型行为来分配权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV联邦学习设计常以客户端自报的验证精度作为加权依据，作者发现该自报值是可被利用的攻击杠杆，而Byzantine鲁棒聚合方法依赖精确的恶意客户端数量假设，因此需要一种不依赖自报、也不需要已知攻击者数量的防御方案。

Method: 提出接收机域行为探测：协调方将每个提交模型在反事实欺骗样本（把各判别性GPS特征驱动到良性值）上评估，依据模型实际行为而非自报精度来对客户端加权。

Result: 在IID客户端下，攻击引起的后门提升从+0.3036降至-0.0265，与诚实舰队在统计上不可区分，并能标记出被攻陷的飞行器；当真实攻击者数量超过配置值时，Multi-Krum从+0.0061恶化到+0.2837，而本方法仍接近基线。评估基于一个公开单接收机数据集划分为模拟客户端，并报告了在强客户端异质性下的失效情形。

Conclusion: 客户端自报验证精度是联邦学习中的可攻击点，基于接收机域行为探测的权重分配能在无需精确攻击者数量、仅需诚实多数的前提下有效抑制后门并识别恶意客户端，但在强客户端异质性下存在局限。

Abstract: Federated learning lets a UAV fleet train a shared GPS spoofing detector without raw receiver data leaving any aircraft, and several recent UAV-FL designs weight each client by the validation accuracy it reports about itself. We show this self-report is an exploitable attack lever: two compromised clients of ten that poison part of their data, scale their updates, and inflate their reported accuracy raise backdoor lift to +0.3036, higher than the same attack achieves without lying. We propose receiver-domain behavioral probing, in which the coordinator evaluates every submitted model on counterfactual spoofed samples built by driving each discriminative GPS feature to a benign value, weighting clients by what their models do rather than what they claim. Under independent and identically distributed clients this reduces attacker-induced lift to -0.0265, statistically indistinguishable from an honest fleet, while flagging the compromised aircraft. Unlike Byzantine-robust aggregation it needs no exact attacker count, only an honest majority: when the true count exceeds the configured value, Multi-Krum degrades from +0.0061 to +0.2837 while ours stays near baseline. Evaluation uses one public single-receiver dataset partitioned into simulated clients; we also report where the mechanism fails, under strong client heterogeneity.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [Adaptive Workflow Intelligence: A Cognitive Architecture for Context-Driven Enterprise Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08793)
*Sreedevi Pandiyath Viswambaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自适应工作流智能（AWI）认知架构，基于感知-认知-行动-反思（PCAR）四层循环，用于应对非平稳环境下的企业智能体决策，并通过漂移与延迟压力测试验证其在合规前提下的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业系统日益依赖自动化工作流，但许多AI方案在非平稳条件、政策演变和延迟反馈下表现脆弱；强化学习与大语言模型智能体虽具部分适应性，却缺乏持续反思机制，也难以与受政策约束的企业运营直接集成。

Method: 提出AWI认知架构，围绕感知-认知-行动-反思（PCAR）四层循环组织，将反思作为持续策略精炼机制，结合混合推理、反思性记忆与反馈驱动适应，在环境漂移与运营约束下支持决策；在具有延迟结果和受控机制转变的模拟企业决策工作流中评估。

Result: 在漂移-延迟压力测试中，受护栏约束的自适应方法相比静态自动化恢复更快，同时保持政策合规；AWI的反思组件在此设定下小幅降低了行为振荡与反馈方差。

Conclusion: AWI展示了反思式策略适应所带来的稳定性-敏捷性权衡，说明反思机制有助于在合规约束下提升企业智能体的适应稳定性，但收益幅度较为有限。

Abstract: Enterprise systems increasingly rely on automated workflows, yet many AI-driven solutions remain brittle under non-stationary conditions, evolving policies, and delayed operational feedback. While reinforcement learning and large language model (LLM) agents offer partial adaptability, they do not by themselves provide persistent reflection mechanisms or straightforward integration with policy-constrained enterprise operations. This paper introduces Adaptive Workflow Intelligence (AWI), a cognitive architecture for context-driven enterprise agents organized around a four-layer Perception-Cognition-Action-Reflection (PCAR) loop. AWI treats reflection as a mechanism for continuous policy refinement and combines hybrid reasoning with reflective memory and feedback-driven adaptation to support decision making under environmental drift and operational constraints. We evaluate AWI in a simulated enterprise decision workflow characterized by delayed outcomes and a controlled regime shift. In a drift-and-delay stress test, guardrail-constrained adaptive approaches recover more rapidly than static automation while maintaining policy compliance. Within this setting, AWI's reflective components modestly reduce behavioral oscillation and feedback variance, illustrating the stability-agility trade-off introduced by reflective policy adaptation.

</details>


### [278] [Accelerating Floating-Point Satisfiability Solving via Gradient Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08808)
*Yuanzhuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GradSAT 将 SMT 子句求解建模为多任务学习任务，用 GradNorm 动态平衡梯度，配合 GPU 加速连续松弛与位精确局部搜索两阶段流程，缓解梯度主导问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优化的 SMT 求解器（尤其针对 QF_FP 理论）虽可对逻辑公式做连续松弛并用梯度下降求解，但受困于"梯度主导"：少数难子句劫持优化轨迹，使求解器无法满足整体公式并陷入局部极小。作者希望解决这一脆弱性问题。

Method: GradSAT 将每条 SMT 子句视为独立的 MTL 任务，运行时应用动态梯度归一化（GradNorm），抑制主导梯度、加速滞后子句以实现均衡收敛。实现为两阶段混合流水线：第一阶段用 GPU 加速的 PyTorch 后端（符号编译与算子融合）在连续松弛上导航到高质量盆地；第二阶段将候选赋值交给位精确局部搜索引擎，快速求得严格精确解。

Result: 报告称 GradSAT 稳定了连续搜索动力学，缓解了以往基于梯度的求解器的脆弱性，并提供了鲁棒、高度可并行的复杂约束求解架构（摘要未给出量化基准数据）。

Conclusion: 通过将 SMT 求解与多任务学习结合，GradSAT 为 QF_FP 等理论的约束求解提供了一种稳健、可并行的新范式，兼顾连续优化效率与位精确求解的正确性。
