This feedback provides an actor-alignment signal by measuring how replacing the retrieved skill with a proposed skill changes the current actor's action log-likelihood gap between previously collected successful and failed trajectories from the same task, thereby avoiding new rollouts for each proposal. Since proposal-level feedback may suppress an otherwise appropriate edit operation when the proposed skill content scores poorly, we further apply skill-edit support regularization to preserve exploration. Empirically, UniSkill achieves strong performance, reaching 98.4% success on ALFWorld and 84.7% on WebShop while maintaining stable joint training. Further ALFWorld experiments show that UniSkill remains effective when the shared policy uses a smaller backbone. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/LimOkii/UniSKill.

</details>


### [378] [Stale, Misattributed, or Late: Where Personal Memory Fails Before Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10265)
*Haonan Deng,Park Sinchaisri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张在生成之前评估语言智能体的个人记忆，将存储状态有效性、身份消解、弃答与服务延迟分开衡量，并发现时间有效性主要取决于记忆构建阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人记忆评估只看最终答案是否正确，这种分数掩盖了生成前就已出现的错误：记忆块可能包含过时值、属于错误人物的信息，或在服务截止时间前没有可用事实。作者希望直接测量这些失效模式。

Method: 以个人事实记忆（PFM）作为参照层，在受控修订基准上仅服务每个正确键控槽的当前值；比较参与者感知 BM25 与参考排序器；用四种 LLM 键分配器与规则抽取器对比；在 LongMemEval 上测开放域合并召回，在 LoCoMo 上做同名说话人区分；并用两个冻结语言模型复现提示错误。

Result: 仅服务活跃值可消除观察到的过时暴露，而无更新消解时 70.3% 的提示会暴露被取代值；参与者感知 BM25 在预设 0.02 边际内等价于参考排序器；LLM 键分配器键召回更高但干净检索率更低，LongMemEval 开放域合并召回不超过 0.062；实体后验可降低受控数据上的同名暴露却无法区分 LoCoMo 中同名说话人；两个冻结模型可复现提示错误；提示预填充在本地硬件上主导轮级延迟。

Conclusion: 评估智能体记忆应在生成之前进行，并分离存储状态有效性、身份消解、弃答与服务延迟四个维度。

Abstract: Personal memory for language agents is usually judged by whether the final an- swer is correct. That score hides errors that arise before generation: the memory block may contain an obsolete value, a fact about the wrong person, or no use- ful fact before the serving deadline. We measure these failures directly. Using Personal Fact Memory (PFM) as a reference layer, we find that temporal validity is primarily a property of memory construction in our setting. On a controlled revision benchmark, serving only the active value of each correctly keyed slot eliminates observed stale exposure; without update resolution, 70.3% of prompts expose a superseded value. Once retrievers share the same active store and par- ticipant information, participant-aware BM25 is equivalent to the reference ranker within a prespecified 0.02 margin. The harder problem is assigning revisions to the right slot. Missed merges leave stale values active, whereas false merges silently remove current values; four LLM key assigners achieve higher key re- call than a rule extractor yet produce lower clean-retrieval rates, and open-domain merge recall on LongMemEval never exceeds 0.062. Misattribution survives va- lidity filtering: an entity posterior reduces same-name exposure on controlled data but cannot distinguish identically named speakers in LoCoMo. Two frozen lan- guage models reproduce prompt errors in generated text. Retrieval latency varies across rankers, but prompt prefill dominates turn-level latency on our hardware. These results argue for evaluating agent memory before generation, separating stored-state validity, identity resolution, abstention, and serving latency.

</details>


### [379] [AI Safety Considerations for Agents With Limited Time to Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10285)
*Leo Zeitler,Jack Richings,Victoria Nockles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面证明：在部分可观测且需限时行动的环境中，即便存在完美智能体，也无法保证安全行为，因此AI安全或对齐的证明必须同时考虑环境与相应的安全动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关于AI对齐的公开讨论增多，已有研究尝试提出行为安全的AI架构，但这些"已证明对齐"的论证大多忽略了智能体实际所处的环境，存在论证不完整的问题。

Method: 讨论与智能体无关（agent-agnostic）的安全保证在部分可观测、限时决策环境下的理论边界，并构造两个贴近现实的场景：一个具有无限状态空间，另一个存在信号混合。

Result: 在上述两个场景中，作者证明即使是一个"完美"的智能体也无法保证安全行为。

Conclusion: 任何关于AI安全或对齐的证明，都必须将环境及其对应的安全动作与智能体一并纳入考虑，而不能仅针对智能体本身。

Abstract: In the wake of the increasingly public discussion about AI alignment, recent work has tried to propose specific AI architectures that behave safely. However, the proposed arguments that seemingly demonstrate proved alignment mostly neglect the environment the agent needs to act in. We discuss theoretical bounds for agent-agnostic safety guarantees in environments that can only be partially observed and within which an action is required within limited time. We introduce two realistic scenarios, one with an infinite state space and one with signal mixture. In these scenarios, we prove that even a perfect agent cannot guarantee safe behaviour. It will be argued that for any proof of AI safety or alignment, the environment and associated safe actions need to be specifically considered together with the agent.

</details>


### [380] [The Handover Problem: Governing Autonomy Transitions in Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10352)
*Vicente Pelechano,Antoni Mestre,Manoli Albert,Miriam Gil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"交接问题"（Handover Problem），即如何多周期决定AI自主权升级、维持或回退；构建可审计的交接就绪评分（HRS），融合操作员就绪、人机信任、学习稳定性与运行绩效四维信号，并配合滞回切换策略与硬安全护栏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机系统很少在固定AI自主水平下运行，随协作演进控制权需动态转移。现有自适应自动化、监督控制、自动化信任与技能退化研究只能解释部分问题，缺乏可审计、多信号的标准来判断自主权何时应变更。

Method: 形式化"交接问题"：每个运行周期决定升级、维持或回退自主权，并保证过程可逆、可恢复、可审计。提出透明的复合指标HRS，整合操作员就绪度、人机信任、学习稳定性、运行绩效四个信号维度；结合基于滞回的转移策略，要求持续正向证据才提升自主权，条件恶化时迅速回退；并配合硬性安全护栏。

Result: 在软件工程与制造领域，HRS与硬安全护栏覆盖互补的失效情形：护栏在单一指标突破临界阈值时强制即时纠正；HRS则能捕捉单一护栏无法观察到的、操作员就绪度的缓慢多信号侵蚀。

Conclusion: 该框架将自主权交接确立为需要显式、复合且可审计标准的治理问题，为自适应自动化研究提供了此前缺失的概念与形式化基础。

Abstract: Human-machine systems rarely operate at a fixed level of AI autonomy. As operators and AI systems collaborate over time, control must shift: the AI can take on more responsibility when collaboration is stable, maintain its current role when evidence is ambiguous, or return control to the human when conditions deteriorate. Existing work on adaptive automation, supervisory control, trust in automation, and deskilling explains parts of this problem, but provides no auditable, multi-signal criterion for governing when autonomy should change across multi-cycle workflows.
  We formalise this challenge as the Handover Problem: deciding, at each operational cycle, whether to escalate, maintain, or revert AI autonomy while keeping the process reversible, recoverable, and auditable. We introduce the Handover Readiness Score (HRS), a transparent composite measure that integrates four signal dimensions: operator readiness, human-AI trust, learning stability, and operational performance. It is combined with a hysteresis-based transition policy that requires sustained positive evidence before increasing autonomy but reverts promptly when conditions worsen.
  Across software engineering and manufacturing domains, the HRS and hard safety guards address complementary failure regimes: guards enforce immediate corrective action when a single indicator breaches a critical threshold, while the HRS detects the slow, multi-signal erosion of operator readiness that no individual guard can observe. The framework establishes autonomy handover as a governance problem requiring explicit, composite, and auditable criteria. This provides a conceptual and formal foundation that adaptive automation research has not previously provided.

</details>


### [381] [Open-MMUnlearning: Unifying Methods and Evaluation for MLLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10358)
*Junkai Chen,Yuhao He,Qianshan Wei,Junxiang You,Jingwen Shao,Junkai Lin,Zhongkai Yue,Xiaotian Ye,Zhengbo Jiao,Jiali Cheng,Zhijie Deng,Kening Zheng,Ruiqi Liu,Hadi Amiri,Yi Yu,Zhenan Sun,Qi Li,Ka-Ho Chow,Sijia Liu,Liang Wang,Jiaqi Li,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出开源可扩展框架 Open-MMUnlearning，统一多模态大模型遗忘（unlearning）的实现与评测协议，并系统比较了10种遗忘方法及13种评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态大模型能力增强并广泛部署，隐私与安全问题日益突出。机器遗忘可从已训练模型中移除指定信息并保留无关能力，但现有实现与评测碎片化、鲁棒性测试不完整、对指标可靠性理解有限，导致该领域进展难以系统评估。

Method: 构建 Open-MMUnlearning 框架，通过共享接口与结构化配置整合目标模型准备、多模态数据处理、遗忘与评估。支持5个覆盖隐私、安全与版权的基准、4个模型家族的8个MLLM、12种遗忘方法；评估套件联合考察遗忘有效性、保留效用以及对模型干预、对抗输入和成员推理攻击的鲁棒性。另引入指标元评估协议，用受控知识暴露模型检验忠实性，并在量化与再学习下检验鲁棒性。

Result: 在统一协议下比较10种代表性遗忘方法：GD 与 MIP-Editor 总分并列最高，GD 的遗忘质量最高，MIP-Editor 保留更多模型效用。在13个评估指标中，BLEU 综合可靠性得分最高；KS-Test 忠实性AUC最高，但鲁棒性表现较差。

Conclusion: 该框架与研究发现共同支持 MLLM 遗忘方法的可复现比较以及对评测可靠性的系统评估。

Abstract: As multimodal large language models (MLLMs) become more capable and widely deployed, concerns about privacy and safety have become increasingly pressing. Machine unlearning offers one approach to addressing these concerns by removing designated information from trained models while preserving unrelated capabilities. However, fragmented implementations and evaluation protocols, incomplete robustness testing, and limited understanding of metric reliability make progress in MLLM unlearning difficult to assess systematically. We introduce Open-MMUnlearning, an open-source, extensible framework that integrates target-model preparation, multimodal data processing, unlearning, and evaluation through shared interfaces and structured configurations. The framework supports five benchmarks spanning privacy, safety, and copyright, eight MLLMs from four model families, and twelve unlearning methods. Its evaluation suite jointly assesses forgetting effectiveness, retained utility, and robustness to model interventions, adversarial inputs, and membership inference attacks. Using a common evaluation protocol, we compare ten representative unlearning methods. In this comparison, GD and MIP-Editor tie for the highest overall score: GD achieves the highest Forget Quality, while MIP-Editor preserves more Model Utility. We further introduce a metric meta-evaluation protocol that tests faithfulness using models with controlled exposure to target knowledge and robustness under quantization and relearning. Among the thirteen evaluated metrics, BLEU achieves the highest aggregate reliability score. KS-Test attains the highest faithfulness AUC but performs less well on robustness. Together, the framework and these findings support reproducible comparison of MLLM unlearning methods and systematic assessment of evaluation reliability.

</details>


### [382] [SOTA: Stock Options Trading Agents Guided by Option-Implied Return Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10407)
*Yizhen Xie,Mengyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SOTA 期权交易智能体框架，将庞大期权宇宙抽象为策略级决策，由确定性解析器实现组合，基于 Qwen3 微调+强化学习，样本外 6 个月取得 18.3% 收益、Sharpe 1.60、最大回撤 8.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期权市场规模扩大且 AI 发展，语言模型智能体可推理新闻等情境信息，但期权交易决策极难：单只股票可有数千合约，需同时决定交易哪些合约及如何组合。现有方法多将策略限制为固定结构（如跨式），无法随市场变化切换策略。

Method: 提出 SOTA（Stock Options Trading Agents）框架，将庞大期权集合抽象为策略层决策，组合实现交由确定性解析器处理。训练上对 Qwen3.8-27B 先做监督微调，再做强化学习。评估在九只美股大盘股及 SPY 期权上进行，与规则型和机器学习型策略选择器在同一交易环境中对比。

Result: 六个月样本外期间，SOTA 总收益 18.3%，Sharpe 比率 1.60，最大回撤 8.96%，优于基线选择器。此外发现新闻的非对称作用：新闻可改进前沿教师轨迹，但在强化学习阶段保留新闻会使样本外收益从 18.3% 降至 -2.7%。

Conclusion: 策略级抽象加确定性执行是应对期权组合复杂性的有效路径，微调加强化学习可提升策略选择能力；同时提醒在强化学习阶段引入新闻信息可能损害泛化表现，需要谨慎设计信息使用方式。

Abstract: As option markets grow and AI advances, agentic systems for option trading are gaining increasing attention. Language-model-based agents can reason over contextual information such as news, but option trading presents a particularly challenging decision problem: a single stock can have thousands of contracts, and the agent must decide both which contracts to trade and how to combine them. Existing approaches often sidestep this complexity by restricting the policy to a fixed strategy structure, such as a straddle, limiting their ability to switch strategies as market conditions change. We present SOTA (Stock Options Trading Agents), an agentic trading framework for structured option-strategy selection. SOTA abstracts the large option universe into strategy-level decisions while deterministic resolvers handle portfolio implementation. We develop SOTA by post-training Qwen3.8-27B with supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning. SOTA is evaluated on options on nine large-cap U.S. equities and SPY against rule-based and machine-learning strategy selectors in the same trading environment. Over a six-month out-of-sample period, SOTA earns an 18.3% total return with a Sharpe ratio of 1.60 and a maximum drawdown of 8.96%. We also document an asymmetric role of news: news improves frontier-teacher trajectories, but retaining news during reinforcement learning reduces out-of-sample return from 18.3% to -2.7%.

</details>


### [383] [EmbodiedRSI: Active Continual Robot Learning Through Hypothesis-Guided Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10498)
*Python Song,Zhixuan Liang,Kelsey Fu,Mengdi Wang,Junfeng Yang,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EmbodiedRSI，一种自演化智能体框架，通过快慢双系统架构与假设图驱动的实验选择，将物理交互转化为代码与技能的协同进化，显著提升机器人基础模型在分布变化下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型具备较强视觉运动控制能力，但当物体位置或任务指令变化时性能会下降。改进通常依赖大量机器人数据后训练，成本高昂；而现有自演化智能体框架在决定探索哪些代码与技能改动时，对机器人试验的利用效率不高。

Method: 采用快慢双系统架构：竞争性的代码与技能假设保存在假设图中；通过信息价值实验选择挑选能区分假设的物理实验；实验结果驱动代码-技能协同进化；慢系统构建分层记忆，并用奖励接地记忆学习按价值筛选有效记忆，用于后续快系统改进。

Result: 在 RoboCasa365 上达到 77.0% 总体成功率和 71.3% 的 Composite-Unseen 成功率，最佳基线为 40.1%；在 LIBERO-Pro 上达到 86.8% 总体成功率；在真实机器人上零样本迁移，多个挑战任务总体成功率达 71.3%。

Conclusion: EmbodiedRSI 通过自主决定探索方向并高效利用物理交互，实现代码与技能的协同进化与记忆学习，在仿真基准和真实机器人上均大幅超越基线，展示了自演化智能体框架提升机器人基础模型适应性的潜力。

Abstract: Robot foundation models provide strong visuomotor control, yet their performance can degrade when object positions or task instructions change. Further improvements often require post-training on substantial robot data, which can be costly to collect through methods such as teleoperation. Agentic harnesses can adapt around the model, but current self-evolving harnesses use robot trials inefficiently when deciding which code and skill changes to pursue. We introduce EmbodiedRSI, a self-evolving agentic harness that autonomously decides where to explore next and turns the resulting physical interaction into improved code and skills. EmbodiedRSI realizes this through a Fast-Slow Dual-System Architecture, in which competing code and skill hypotheses are maintained in a Hypothesis Graph. Value-of-Information Experiment Selection chooses physical experiments that can distinguish these hypotheses. Their outcomes guide Code-Skill Co-Evolution. The Slow System builds Hierarchical Memory, and Reward-Grounded Memory Learning selects effective memory according to their value for later Fast-System improvement. On RoboCasa365, EmbodiedRSI reaches 77.0% overall success and 71.3% on Composite-Unseen, compared with 40.1% for the best baseline. EmbodiedRSI also reaches 86.8% overall success on LIBERO-Pro. Beyond benchmark performance, EmbodiedRSI transfers zero-shot to real-world robot, achieving 71.3% overall success across multiple challenging tasks.

</details>


### [384] [RECAST: Learning to Compute the Right Context through Adaptive Evidence Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10507)
*Yilun Hao,Krishna Sayana,Isabella Ye,James S Ren,Sukhdeep Sodhi,Craig Boutilier,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECAST 将"证据构建"建模为在异构检索与计算操作上的序列决策过程，用轻量 RouterLM 主动推导证据，再由冻结的 CompilerLM 与 AnswerLM 完成求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多任务的证据并不显式存在于任何单一来源中，而需跨多个来源项进行过滤、聚合或计算才能得到；传统 RAG 依赖固定相似度检索，agentic 变体虽能改写查询与使用工具，但仍以检索为中心，无法主动"构造"证据。

Method: 提出 RECAST 框架：将证据构建视为对异构检索与计算操作的序列决策。轻量 RouterLM 迭代地选择并构造原始操作，或指定定制操作交给冻结的 CompilerLM 翻译为可执行代码；当判定证据充分时，RouterLM 将接受的证据传给冻结的 AnswerLM 生成最终答案。训练采用监督微调（SFT）加组相对策略优化（GRPO）。

Result: 在六个异构基准家族上平均成功率达 75.6%，超过最强的大模型基线 15.9%；训练使 Qwen3.5-9B RouterLM 比免训练的 Gemini 3.5 Flash RouterLM 高 5.0%。在三个留出基准上平均超出最强基线 15.0%，展现出跨任务与异构来源表示的强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以"主动推导证据"取代"被动检索证据"，并通过小模型路由 + 冻结大模型编译/回答的分工，RECAST 在显著降低训练成本的同时取得更强的性能与泛化，为长而异构信息源上的证据构建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly applied to tasks grounded in long, heterogeneous information sources. Conventional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) relies on fixed similarity-based retrieval, while agentic variants adapt queries and tool use but remain largely retrieval-centric. However, in many tasks, the evidence required for a solution is not explicitly present in any single source item. Instead, it must be derived through filtering, aggregation, or computation across multiple source items. In this work, we introduce RECAST (Routing Evidence through Computation, Access, and Synthesized Tools), a learned framework that formulates evidence construction as a sequential decision process over heterogeneous retrieval and computation operations, allowing evidence to be actively derived rather than merely retrieved. A lightweight RouterLM iteratively selects and formulates primitive operations or specifies customized operations for a frozen CompilerLM to translate into executable code. Once it judges the evidence sufficient, RouterLM passes the accepted evidence to a frozen AnswerLM to produce the final solution. We train RouterLM with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by group relative policy optimization (GRPO). Across six heterogeneous benchmark families, RECAST achieves a mean success rate of 75.6%, outperforming the strongest large-model baseline by 15.9%. Moreover, training enables the Qwen3.5-9B RouterLM to outperform a training-free Gemini 3.5 Flash RouterLM by 5.0%. On three held-out benchmarks, RECAST improves over the strongest baseline by 15.0% on average, demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization across tasks and heterogeneous source representations.

</details>


### [385] [SciExam for ENSO: Can AI Agents Build Climate Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10513)
*Yinling Zhang,Langchen Liu,Dongbin Xiu,Xueyan Zou,Xu Kuang,Mengdi Wang,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SciExam for ENSO基准：让语言模型智能体在6小时内基于真实观测构建ENSO低阶随机模型，并由隐藏评分器以统计再现、变量恢复与预测检验；12个系统中6个超越已发表模型，且强模型结构触及未点明的ENSO不对称性争论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体科研评测多依赖已知答案、评分量表或语言模型评审，无法判断新科学模型是否有效，尤其缺乏"无答案"情形下的评测方式。

Method: 构建SciExam for ENSO基准：智能体在六小时预算内处理真实观测、自行编写诊断代码并冻结，仅凭这些诊断反馈开发ENSO低阶随机模型；隐藏评分器从ENSO统计特征复现、未观测变量恢复、留出年份预测三方面评分，并以同一标准评估一个已发表模型；同时测试12个智能体系统，并开展信息变体的受控实验。

Result: 12个智能体系统中6个模型得分高于已发表模型，主要优势在重建与预测；强模型的简化形式分别与ENSO冷热不对称性的两种竞争解释之一相容；受控实验表明高分并非来自记忆陈旧观测记录，且所获信息会影响建模方式。

Conclusion: SciExam for ENSO可用于在无已知答案情形下评测智能体科研能力；结果表明智能体已能构建有竞争力的模型，其结构还与科学家仍存的争论相关。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly asked to carry out open-ended scientific research, yet their results are usually graded against a known answer, a rubric, or a language-model reviewer, none of which can tell whether a new scientific model is valid. The AI Science Exam for El Nino-Southern Oscillation (SciExam for ENSO) is a benchmark in which agents build low-order stochastic models of ENSO, the dominant mode of interannual climate variability, from real observations. Within a six-hour budget, agents process the observations, write their own diagnostics, which are then frozen, and develop a model using only these diagnostics as feedback. Hidden graders then test whether the model reproduces ENSO's statistics, recovers unobserved variables, and forecasts held-out years, and score a published model in the same way. Across twelve agent systems, six produce models that score higher than the published model, mainly through better reconstruction and forecasting. The simplified forms of the stronger models are each compatible with one of the two competing explanations of ENSO's warm-cold asymmetry, an open debate that the task never mentions. Controlled runs of the top system under varied information suggest that its scores do not come from recalling the dated observational record and that the information it receives shapes how it builds its model. SciExam for ENSO can thus evaluate agent research where no answer is known, and the results suggest that agents can already build competitive models whose structures bear on questions that scientists still debate.

</details>


### [386] [RoboJEPA: Scaling Robotic Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10515)
*Artem Zholus,Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Jianhao Yuan,Sarath Chandar,Tushar Nagarajan,Daniel Severo,Koustuv Sinha,Michal Drozdzal,Adriana Romero Soriano,Jeannette Bohg,Nicolas Ballas,Mahmoud Assran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于JEPA的8B参数世界模型RoboJEPA，训练于12种机器人本体的数据集，首次建立了多本体机器人世界模型的扩展规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型在预测未来状态和规划方面能力突出，但缺乏一种基于原则的方法来估计其能力如何随模型规模、数据和计算量扩展，这一开放问题阻碍了领域进展。

Method: 基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）构建世界模型RoboJEPA，在涵盖12种机器人本体的大规模数据集上训练；测量其"想象误差"（潜在rollout的误差）随计算量的变化规律，并将想象误差与下游规划性能进行关联分析。

Result: 想象误差随计算量呈二阶幂律下降，可外推预测超出拟合范围的模型质量；下游机器人规划性能随计算量可预测地提升，且与想象误差强相关；潜在世界模型可零样本部署为机器人智能体，仅凭单张目标图像在真实硬件上完成需要长时程规划的任务。

Conclusion: 首次为基于真实机器人数据训练的多本体机器人世界模型建立扩展规律；RoboJEPA达到8B参数，是迄今训练的最大JEPA预测器模型；全部模型检查点、训练与部署代码均已开源。

Abstract: Latent world models have shown a remarkable ability to predict future states and to plan in the real world. In practice, however, we lack a principled way to estimate how their capabilities scale with model size, data, and compute, an open problem that slows progress in the field. In this work we present RoboJEPA, a world model based on the Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and trained on a large-scale dataset spanning 12 robotic embodiments. We show that RoboJEPA's imagination error, the error of its latent rollouts, follows a second-order power law in compute, allowing us to predict model quality well beyond the scale at which the law is fit. We further show that downstream robotic planning performance improves predictably with compute, and that imagination error is strongly correlated with it, making it a reliable proxy for real-robot evaluation. Finally, we demonstrate that latent world models can be deployed zero-shot as robotic agents, planning toward a single goal image to solve tasks requiring long-horizon planning on real hardware. We release all model checkpoints together with our training and robot deployment code. To our knowledge, this is the first work to establish scaling laws for multi-embodiment robotic world models trained on real robot data, and RoboJEPA, at 8B parameters, is the largest JEPA predictor model trained to date.

</details>


### [387] [Efficient Dense Crowd Trajectory Prediction Via Dynamic Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18166)
*Antonius Bima Murti Wijaya,Paul Henderson,Marwa Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对密集人群场景下人群轨迹预测的计算成本高、跟踪输出噪声大等问题，提出一种基于聚类的即插即用方法，将个体按相似属性随时间分组，用组质心替代行人输入，从而提升速度、降低内存并保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人群轨迹预测对公共安全和管理（如预防踩踏等灾难）至关重要。现有方法多基于人工标注数据预测个体轨迹并考虑周围对象，但往往忽视密集人群场景。在密集场景中，跟踪输出具有海量性、噪声性和不准确性，导致自动化和计算成本面临更大挑战。

Method: 提出一种新颖的基于聚类的方法，随时间将具有相似属性的个体分组，通过准确的群体摘要实现更快执行。该方法是即插即用的，可与现有轨迹预测器结合：用聚类输出的质心替代原预测器的行人输入。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的密集人群场景上进行评估，结果表明：与最先进方法相比，该方法处理速度更快、内存占用更低，同时保持了预测精度。

Conclusion: 基于聚类的群体摘要方法能在密集人群轨迹预测中有效降低计算与内存开销，并可作为即插即用模块与现有预测器兼容，兼顾效率与精度。

Abstract: Crowd trajectory prediction plays a crucial role in public safety and management, where it can help prevent disasters such as stampedes. Recent works address the problem by predicting individual trajectories and considering surrounding objects based on manually annotated data. However, these approaches tend to overlook dense crowd scenarios, where the challenges of automation become more pronounced due to the massiveness, noisiness, and inaccuracy of the tracking outputs, resulting in high computational costs. To address these challenges, we propose and extensively evaluate a novel cluster-based approach that groups individuals based on similar attributes over time, enabling faster execution through accurate group summarisation. Our plug-and-play method can be combined with existing trajectory predictors by using our output centroid in place of their pedestrian input. We evaluate our proposed method on several challenging dense crowd scenes. We demonstrated that our approach leads to faster processing and lower memory usage when compared with state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining the accuracy

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [388] [CANDO: Cooperative Agentic Network for Layout Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10044)
*Athanasios Masouris,Zheng Jing,Benjamin Sam Chandler,Hadi Jamali-Rad*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出ALPS-Bench基准与CANDO免训练多智能体框架，用于真实设施布局生成，并在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实设施布局生成需处理不规则场地边界、异构朝向、可达性布置与运动规划可行性等复杂问题，而现有生成式AI布局基准多针对矩形域上的简单布置，且依赖FID、IoU等分布性指标，只奖励对数据先验的拟合，忽视设计创新。

Method: 提出ALPS-Bench：包含1000个专业标注的真实设施布局，并配备基于结构化设计手册的实例级评分协议；同时提出CANDO，一个免训练的多智能体框架，专用智能体通过验证驱动的循环迭代优化布局，将推理集中于策略性空间决策。

Result: CANDO在广泛采用的PubLayNet、RICO和PKU-PosterLayout基准上超越了当前最优的已训练模型和基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 协作式智能体设计是约束感知布局合成的广泛有效方案，ALPS-Bench为真实设施布局提供了更贴近实际需求的评估基准。

Abstract: Layout generation for real-world facilities is a challenging problem, requiring reasoning over irregular site boundaries, heterogeneous orientations, access-aware placements, and motion-planning feasibility. Yet, most existing layout benchmarks in the generative AI space target simpler placements over rectangular domains and rely on distributional metrics such as FID and IoU that reward conformity to dataset priors, thus discounting design innovation. Motivated by these gaps, we introduce ALPS-Bench, a benchmark of $1,000$ professionally annotated real-world facility layouts paired with an instance-specific scoring protocol grounded in a structured design manual. As a strong baseline for ALPS-Bench, we propose CANDO, a training-free multi-agent framework in which specialized agents iteratively refine layouts through a verification-grounded loop, concentrating reasoning on strategic spatial decisions. We demonstrate that CANDO surpasses state-of-the-art trained and LLM-based baselines on the widely adopted PubLayNet, RICO, and PKU-PosterLayout benchmarks, establishing cooperative agentic design as a broadly effective recipe for constraint-aware layout synthesis.

</details>


### [389] [The Cost of Classical Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10100)
*Alvin Combrink,Sabino Francesco Roselli,Martin Fabian*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文对比经典离散时间MAPF与连续时间MAPF$_R$，量化经典形式在解质量上的损失及其来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MAPF长期占主导，其离散时间与图冲突假设限制了可适用的真实环境和智能体，并对解质量设下了任何算法都无法突破的上限。作者希望弄清经典形式究竟在多大程度上、在什么情境下牺牲了解质量。

Method: 以连续时间MAPF（MAPF$_R$）为对照形式，放松离散时间与图冲突假设，并在不同智能体数量与尺寸、图的连通性、拓扑结构及分辨率下进行系统比较，以分离各因素的贡献。

Result: 连续时间与智能体形状本身价值有限；真正价值在于可扩展的移动动作集合，在狭窄受限地图上平均提升至少5%，在开阔空间地图上平均提升17%，部分情况超过20%。将经典MAPF地图分辨率翻倍仅挽回不到3%的差距。

Conclusion: 经典MAPF在何时是合理简化、何时MAPF$_R$能带来显著更优解，取决于移动动作是否受限；通过增加算力难以弥补经典形式所放弃的解质量。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) is the problem of planning conflict-free paths for multiple agents in a shared space, each from its start to its goal. Classical MAPF has been the dominant formulation for many years, with its assumptions of discrete time and graph-based conflicts presumably easing the search for solutions. These assumptions limit the physical environments and agents for which a solution is truly collision-free, and also place an upper bound on solution quality that no algorithmic improvements can lift. This work investigates how much solution quality, and in what contexts, the classical MAPF formulation forfeits. Continuous-time MAPF (MAPF$_R$) relaxes these assumptions, making it a natural counter-formulation to compare against across various agent counts and sizes, and graph connectedness, topologies, and resolutions. We find that continuous time and agent shape consideration are worth relatively little on their own; their value comes from enabling an expanded range of move actions, on average improving solution quality by at least $5\%$ on narrow and constrained maps and $17\%$ on maps with open spaces. In some cases, the improvements exceed $20\%$. Doubling the map resolution with classical MAPF recovers less than $3\%$, meaning that little of what is forfeited can be bought back through more compute. This work therefore provides insight on when classical MAPF is a reasonable simplification, and when MAPF$_R$ unlocks significantly higher-quality solutions.

</details>


### [390] [Know the Shape, Find the Fault: Topology-Conditioned Diagnosis of Multi-Agent LLM Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10126)
*Xinwen Liu,Zhuocheng Pan,Isabella Zhu,Jawei Zhang,Xudong Liu,Tianyu Wo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文分析通信拓扑与多智能体LLM系统故障模式的关系，并提出两阶段框架MAScope，通过恢复通信拓扑实现拓扑条件化的故障诊断，显著提升诊断性能并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过信息交换协调任务，当协调失败时，执行轨迹中的相似症状可能反映信息传递、使用或验证中的不同问题。通信拓扑捕获智能体间的信息交换方式，为区分协调故障模式提供结构线索，但需要建立拓扑与故障模式的关系，并从缺乏显式拓扑标签的执行轨迹中恢复相关结构。

Method: MAScope包含两个阶段：轨迹结构提取器TSE通过将交互图锚定在消息证据上，从异构执行轨迹中恢复通信拓扑；拓扑条件化评判器TC-Judge利用轨迹、预测拓扑、从单独标注轨迹估计的经验故障先验，以及拓扑特定故障模式的简短描述来分类故障。在固定编排结构下，恢复的拓扑可跨执行复用。

Result: 通信拓扑与故障类型之间存在统计显著关联（χ²=409.9，p=1.2×10⁻⁷⁰）。在851条MAST-clean轨迹上，真实拓扑上下文将gpt-mini的Macro-F1从0.173提升至0.350；使用预测拓扑时达到0.346，接近仅用轨迹的gpt-5.4基线0.372。在固定编排结构下的1000条轨迹中，包含一次拓扑提取的预计流水线成本约为重复gpt-5.4诊断成本的6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，拓扑条件化上下文能改善故障诊断，并支持更低成本的部署。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems coordinate task execution through exchanges of information among agents. When coordination breaks down, similar symptoms in execution traces can reflect different problems in how information is passed, used, or verified. Communication topology captures how agents exchange information and provides structural cues for distinguishing coordination failure modes. Using these cues for diagnosis requires establishing how topology relates to failure patterns and recovering the relevant structure from execution traces that lack explicit topology labels. We analyze the relationship between communication topology and failure patterns and introduce MAScope, a two-stage framework for topology-conditioned diagnosis. Its Trace Structural Extractor TSE recovers communication topology from heterogeneous execution traces by grounding an interaction graph in message evidence. The Topology-Conditioned Judge TC-Judge then classifies failures using the trace, predicted topology, an empirical failure prior estimated from separate labeled traces, and a short description of topology-specific failure patterns. Under a fixed orchestration structure, the recovered topology can be reused across executions. Experimental results show a statistically significant association between communication topology and failure type, with $χ^2 = 409.9$ and $p = 1.2 \times 10^{-70}$. On the \num{851} MAST-clean traces, ground-truth topology context raises gpt-mini's Macro-F1 from $0.173$ to $0.350$. With predicted topology, the pipeline achieves $0.346$, approaching the trace-only gpt-5.4 baseline of $0.372$. For \num{1000} traces under a fixed orchestration structure, the projected pipeline cost, including one topology extraction, is approximately $6\%$ of repeated gpt-5.4 diagnosis cost. These results show that topology-conditioned context improves failure diagnosis and supports lower-cost deployment.

</details>


### [391] [A Society of Researchers: Designing Institutions for Populations of Autonomous Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10468)
*Ali Asaria,Deep Gandhi,Tony Salomone*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文主张应显式地为大规模研究型智能体群体设计"社会"组织结构，并提出由六项原则构建的"研究者社会"，包含竞争性经费申请、独立评审与人类市长资源分配机制；在万名研究者规模的运行实验中，某实验室报告可节省约30%算力达到同等质量，但结果尚未获得重复验证的共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究型智能体的部署规模正迈向数千乃至上万、共享同一算力池的阶段，而现有系统多以单项目为单位组织，或对整体群体缺乏组织设计。作者认为，如此规模的群体无论设计者是否提供，都会自发形成某种组织形态，因此不如显式地加以设计；同时多智能体系统社区已具备实现这一目标的理论与工具基础。

Method: 提出"智能体社会"框架：由持久存在的智能体群体在显式制度下运行，并将其发展为一支"研究者社会"，建立在六项原则之上。核心机制包括：首席研究员通过提案征集、独立评审与资助来竞争算力；由人类"市长"作为治理者负责资源分配，但不向智能体指派任务。据此运行了一个包含一万名研究者的社会，其唯一任务目标是改进语言模型的预训练。

Result: 在该万人规模的研究者社会中，有一个实验室报告了一种方法，能够在达到相同质量的前提下减少约30%的算力消耗；不过，负责测试该结果的其他实验室目前尚未就此达成一致意见。

Conclusion: 大规模研究智能体群体需要显式的组织与制度设计，多智能体社区具备构建此类社会的能力。文中最后提出面向智能体社区的六个开放问题，以推动该方向的进一步研究。

Abstract: Deployments of research agents are moving to populations of thousands that share one pool of compute, while most current systems organize one project at a time or leave the population unorganized. We argue that such a population will acquire an organization whether or not its designers provide one, so designers should provide it explicitly, and that the multi-agent systems community holds the tools to do so. We propose a society of agents, a population of persistent agents under explicit institutions, and develop it for science as a society of researchers built on six principles. Principal investigators compete for compute through requests for proposals, independent review, and grants; a human governor, the mayor, allocates resources and assigns no tasks. In a running society of ten thousand researchers, asked only to improve the pretraining of language models, one lab reported a way to reach the same quality with about 30% less compute, a result the labs that tested it do not yet agree on. We close with six open problems for the agents community.

</details>
